  <script type="module">
    import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-app.js";
    import { getAuth, signInAnonymously, signInWithCustomToken, onAuthStateChanged } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-auth.js";
    import { getFirestore, doc, getDoc, setDoc, onSnapshot } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.6.1/firebase-firestore.js";

    window.__cloudSaveBridge = {
      ready: false,
      userId: null,
      cachedSave: null,
      saveCareerCloud: async () => false,
      loadCareerCloud: async () => null
    };

    (async function initCloudSave() {
      const idBar = document.getElementById('cloud-player-id-bar');
      try {
        if (typeof __firebase_config === 'undefined') {
          if (idBar) idBar.textContent = 'PLAYER SAVE ID: OFFLINE SESSION';
          return;
        }
        const appId = typeof __app_id !== 'undefined' ? __app_id : 'default-app-id';
        const firebaseConfig = JSON.parse(__firebase_config);
        const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
        const auth = getAuth(app);
        const db = getFirestore(app);

        if (typeof __initial_auth_token !== 'undefined' && __initial_auth_token) {
          await signInWithCustomToken(auth, __initial_auth_token);
        } else {
          await signInAnonymously(auth);
        }

        const user = auth.currentUser;
        if (!user) return;

        const userId = user.uid;
        window.__cloudSaveBridge.userId = userId;
        window.__cloudSaveBridge.ready = true;
        if (idBar) idBar.textContent = `PLAYER SAVE ID: ${userId}`;

        // Strict private user path: /artifacts/{appId}/users/{userId}/dynastySaves/activeCareer
        const saveDocRef = doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', userId, 'dynastySaves', 'activeCareer');

        onSnapshot(saveDocRef, (snap) => {
          if (snap.exists()) {
            const data = snap.data();
            if (data && data.payload) {
              try {
                window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave = JSON.parse(data.payload);
              } catch (err) {
                console.warn('Parse snapshot failed:', err);
              }
            }
          }
        }, (err) => {
          console.warn('Cloud save listener error:', err);
        });

        window.__cloudSaveBridge.saveCareerCloud = async (careerObj) => {
          if (!auth.currentUser || !careerObj) return false;
          try {
            const ref = doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', auth.currentUser.uid, 'dynastySaves', 'activeCareer');
            const serialized = JSON.stringify(careerObj);
            window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave = JSON.parse(serialized);
            await setDoc(ref, {
              ownerUid: auth.currentUser.uid,
              teamId: careerObj.teamId || 'BUF',
              season: careerObj.season || 1,
              week: careerObj.week || 1,
              updatedAt: Date.now(),
              payload: serialized
            });
            return true;
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn('Cloud save write failed:', err);
            return false;
          }
        };

        window.__cloudSaveBridge.loadCareerCloud = async () => {
          if (!auth.currentUser) return null;
          try {
            const ref = doc(db, 'artifacts', appId, 'users', auth.currentUser.uid, 'dynastySaves', 'activeCareer');
            const snap = await getDoc(ref);
            if (!snap.exists()) return null;
            const data = snap.data();
            if (!data || data.ownerUid !== auth.currentUser.uid || !data.payload) return null;
            const parsed = JSON.parse(data.payload);
            window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave = parsed;
            return parsed;
          } catch (err) {
            console.warn('Cloud save read failed:', err);
            return null;
          }
        };

        // Pre-fetch user's cloud save immediately on load
        await window.__cloudSaveBridge.loadCareerCloud();
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Cloud save initialization skipped:', e);
        if (idBar) idBar.textContent = 'PLAYER SAVE ID: SESSION ONLY';
      }
    })();
  </script>

  <script>
    /* =========================================================
       1. RETRO 8-BIT / 16-BIT WEB AUDIO SYNTHESIZER
       ========================================================= */
    class RetroAudio {
      constructor() {
        this.enabled = true;
        this.ctx = null;
      }
      init() {
        if (!this.ctx) {
          const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
          if (AudioCtx) this.ctx = new AudioCtx();
        }
        if (this.ctx && this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
          this.ctx.resume();
        }
      }
      tone(freq, type = 'square', duration = 0.12, vol = 0.08, delay = 0) {
        if (!this.enabled) return;
        this.init();
        if (!this.ctx) return;
        const now = this.ctx.currentTime + delay;
        const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
        osc.type = type;
        osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now);
        gain.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, now);
        gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + duration);
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
        osc.start(now);
        osc.stop(now + duration);
      }
      playClick() { this.tone(520, 'square', 0.06, 0.06); }
      playWhistle() {
        this.tone(2650, 'sine', 0.14, 0.06, 0);
        this.tone(2850, 'sine', 0.18, 0.06, 0.08);
      }
      playThrow() {
        this.tone(320, 'triangle', 0.08, 0.08, 0);
        this.tone(540, 'triangle', 0.1, 0.07, 0.05);
      }
      playCatch() {
        this.tone(440, 'square', 0.07, 0.08, 0);
        this.tone(660, 'square', 0.11, 0.08, 0.06);
      }
      playTackle() {
        this.tone(130, 'sawtooth', 0.14, 0.12, 0);
        this.tone(75, 'square', 0.18, 0.12, 0.04);
      }
      playTouchdown() {
        const notes = [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5, 783.99, 1046.5];
        notes.forEach((n, i) => this.tone(n, 'square', 0.14, 0.09, i * 0.11));
      }
      playTurnover() {
        const notes = [330, 293.66, 261.63, 196];
        notes.forEach((n, i) => this.tone(n, 'sawtooth', 0.16, 0.08, i * 0.12));
      }
    }
    const sfx = new RetroAudio();

       4. GLOBAL APPLICATION & FRANCHISE STATE
       ========================================================= */
    const SAVE_KEY = 'gridiron_dynasty_92_save_v3';
    let career = null;
    let pendingMode = 'CAREER';
    let quickPlayStep = 1;
    let quickUserTeamId = 'BUF';
    let selectedTeamId = 'BUF';
    let selectedConfFilter = 'ALL';
    let selectedDifficulty = 'PRO';
    let userUniformChoice = null; // Must be explicitly chosen ('HOME' or 'AWAY') before starting a game
    let currentHubTab = 'office';
    let rosterUnitFilter = 'ALL';
    let faPosFilter = 'ALL';
    let offPlayStep = 'FORMATION';
    let selectedOffFormationId = 'SINGLEBACK';
    let offCarouselIdx = 0;
    let defPlayStep = 'FORMATION';
    let selectedDefFormationId = '4-3';
    let defCarouselIdx = 0;

    const keys = {};
    let match = null;
    let lastFrameTime = 0;
    let hoverFieldPos = null;

    function showToast(msg, duration = 2600) {
      const box = document.getElementById('toast-box');
      const txt = document.getElementById('toast-msg');
      if (!box || !txt) return;
      txt.textContent = msg;
      box.classList.remove('hidden');
      clearTimeout(box._toastTimer);
      box._toastTimer = setTimeout(() => box.classList.add('hidden'), duration);
    }

    function switchScreen(screenId) {
      ['screen-title', 'screen-team-select', 'screen-hub', 'screen-game'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.classList.toggle('hidden', id !== screenId);
      });
      if (screenId === 'screen-game') resizeCanvas();
    }

    function setUserUniformChoice(choice) {
      userUniformChoice = choice;
      const desc = document.getElementById('uniform-select-desc');
      if (desc) {
        if (choice === 'HOME') {
          desc.textContent = 'HOME UNIFORM (COLORED JERSEY) SELECTED';
          desc.className = 'text-xs sm:text-sm lg:text-base text-emerald-400';
        } else if (choice === 'AWAY') {
          desc.textContent = 'AWAY UNIFORM (WHITE JERSEY) SELECTED';
          desc.className = 'text-xs sm:text-sm lg:text-base text-emerald-400';
        } else {
          desc.textContent = 'REQUIRED — SELECT HOME OR AWAY UNIFORM';
          desc.className = 'text-xs sm:text-sm lg:text-base text-rose-400';
        }
      }
      document.querySelectorAll('.uni-choice-btn').forEach(btn => {
        const active = btn.getAttribute('data-uni-choice') === choice;
        btn.classList.toggle('btn-gold', active);
        btn.classList.toggle('btn-slate', !active);
      });
      if (career && !document.getElementById('screen-hub').classList.contains('hidden')) {
        openHub(currentHubTab);
      }
    }

    function buildTeamUniform(team, mode) {
      if (mode === 'AWAY') {
        return {
          jersey: '#f8fafc',
          trim: team.primary || '#1e293b',
          helmet: team.primary || '#1e293b'
        };
      }
      return {
        jersey: team.primary || '#1d4ed8',
        trim: team.secondary || '#facc15',
        helmet: team.secondary || '#ffffff'
      };
    }

    function openTeamSelector(mode = 'CAREER') {
      pendingMode = mode;
      quickPlayStep = 1;
      setUserUniformChoice(null);
      const titleEl = document.getElementById('team-select-title');
      const subEl = document.getElementById('team-select-subtitle');
      const uniBar = document.getElementById('uniform-select-bar');
      if (uniBar) {
        uniBar.classList.toggle('hidden', mode === 'CAREER');
      }
      if (titleEl) {
        titleEl.textContent = mode === 'CAREER'
          ? 'SELECT YOUR DYNASTY FRANCHISE'
          : 'QUICK PLAY: STEP 1 — SELECT YOUR TEAM';
      }
      if (subEl) {
        subEl.textContent = mode === 'CAREER'
          ? 'CLICK ANY FRANCHISE TO BEGIN YOUR DYNASTY'
          : 'CHOOSE UNIFORM & YOUR TEAM FIRST, THEN PICK YOUR OPPONENT';
      }
      renderTeamGrid();
      switchScreen('screen-team-select');
    }

    function renderTeamGrid() {
      const grid = document.getElementById('team-grid');
      if (!grid) return;
      const list = TEAMS.filter(t => selectedConfFilter === 'ALL' || t.conf === selectedConfFilter);
      grid.innerHTML = list.map(t => `
        <button data-pick-team="${t.id}" class="pixel-box p-5 sm:p-6 bg-slate-900 hover:border-yellow-400 text-left flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 h-auto min-h-[210px] w-full overflow-visible">
          <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 w-full">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-4 min-w-0">
              <div class="w-14 h-14 sm:w-16 sm:h-16 border-2 border-white flex items-center justify-center text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-white shrink-0" style="background:${t.primary}">${t.id}</div>
              <div class="min-w-0">
                <div class="text-lg sm:text-xl text-yellow-400 leading-snug">${t.city} ${t.name}</div>
                <div class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-300 mt-1">${t.conf} ${t.div} &bull; ${t.tier}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t-2 border-slate-800 pt-3 w-full">
            <span class="text-emerald-400 text-sm sm:text-base flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>OFF:</span>
              <span class="text-lg sm:text-xl tracking-wider leading-none">${'★'.repeat(t.off)}</span>
            </span>
            <span class="text-sky-400 text-sm sm:text-base flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span>DEF:</span>
              <span class="text-lg sm:text-xl tracking-wider leading-none">${'★'.repeat(t.def)}</span>
            </span>
          </div>
          <div class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-slate-200 leading-normal break-words whitespace-normal pt-1 pb-2 w-full">
            <span class="text-amber-300">STARS:</span> ${t.stars.map(s => s.name).join(', ')}
          </div>
        </button>
      `).join('');

      grid.querySelectorAll('[data-pick-team]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const tid = btn.getAttribute('data-pick-team');
          sfx.playClick();
          if (pendingMode === 'CAREER') {
            createNewCareer(tid);
            openHub('office');
          } else if (quickPlayStep === 1) {
            if (!userUniformChoice) {
              showToast('SELECT HOME OR AWAY UNIFORM FIRST!');
              return;
            }
            quickUserTeamId = tid;
            quickPlayStep = 2;
            const userT = getUserTeam(tid);
            document.getElementById('team-select-title').textContent = `QUICK PLAY: STEP 2 — SELECT OPPONENT VS ${userT.id}`;
            document.getElementById('team-select-subtitle').textContent = 'CLICK ANY OPPONENT TO KICK OFF!';
            renderTeamGrid();
          } else {
            if (!userUniformChoice) {
              showToast('SELECT HOME OR AWAY UNIFORM FIRST!');
              return;
            }
            startMatch(quickUserTeamId, tid, true);
          }
        });
      });
    }

    function getPayroll() {
      if (!career || !career.roster) return 0;
      return career.roster.reduce((sum, p) => sum + (p.salary || 3), 0);
    }

    // Scaled from 53-man NFL roster minus 19 offensive/defensive linemen = 34 active players
    const BASE_ROSTER_LIMIT = 34;
    const BASE_SALARY_CAP = 175;
    const ANNUAL_CAP_GROWTH_RATE = 0.08; // Proportional 8% annual cap & contract ceiling growth
    const STARTER_SLOTS_BY_POS = { QB: 1, RB: 1, WR: 3, TE: 1, LB: 4, CB: 2, S: 2 };

    const FACILITY_POOL = [
      {
        key: 'stadium',
        name: 'STADIUM & REVENUE',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 14,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${lvl - 1} CC / WIN • +$${(lvl - 1) * 4}M CAP BOOST`
      },
      {
        key: 'training',
        name: 'WEIGHT ROOM & LAB',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 16,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${(lvl - 1) * 6}% TRAINING ODDS • -${(lvl - 1) * 8}% VET DECLINE`
      },
      {
        key: 'scouting',
        name: 'DRAFT SCOUTING HQ',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 15,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${(lvl - 1) * 2}% SUPERSTAR DRAFT ODDS (${5 + (lvl - 1) * 2}%)`
      },
      {
        key: 'medical',
        name: 'SPORTS MED & RECOVERY',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 14,

      },
      {
        key: 'filmRoom',
        name: 'FILM & ANALYTICS ROOM',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 16,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${(lvl - 1) * 2}% PLAY EXECUTION & ROUTE/COVERAGE IQ`
      },
      {
        key: 'luxurySuites',
        name: 'LUXURY SUITES & VIP CLUB',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 15,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${lvl - 1} CC / WIN • +$${(lvl - 1) * 3}M CAP SPACE`
      },
      {
        key: 'qbAcademy',
        name: 'QUARTERBACK LAB & SIM',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 16,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${(lvl - 1) * 5}% PASSING DRILL ODDS • +${lvl - 1} QB PEAK CAP`
      },
      {
        key: 'speedTrack',
        name: 'INDOOR TURF & SPEED TRACK',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 15,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${(lvl - 1) * 4}% IN-GAME TURBO STAMINA & SPEED DRILLS`
      },
      {
        key: 'collegeCombine',
        name: 'PRO DAY & COMBINE HUB',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 16,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${(lvl - 1) * 2}% ROOKIE BREAKOUT ODDS (${5 + (lvl - 1) * 2}%)`
      },
      {
        key: 'cryoChamber',
        name: 'CRYO & NUTRITION CENTER',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 14,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `-${(lvl - 1) * 7}% VETERAN DECLINE • +${lvl - 1} LONGEVITY YRS`
      },
      {
        key: 'fanEngagement',
        name: 'MERCH & BROADCAST STUDIO',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 14,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${lvl - 1} CC / WIN • +$${(lvl - 1) * 5}M CAP BOOST`
      },
      {
        key: 'defensiveLab',
        name: 'DEFENSIVE FILM & VR LAB',
        maxLvl: 5,
        baseCost: 15,
        bonusDesc: (lvl) => `+${(lvl - 1) * 5}% LB/DB DRILL ODDS • +${lvl - 1} DEF PEAK CAP`
      }
    ];

    function rotateSeasonFacilities(seasonNum = 1, prevKeys = []) {
      const candidates = FACILITY_POOL.filter(f => !prevKeys.includes(f.key));
      const pool = candidates.length >= 5 ? candidates : [...FACILITY_POOL];
      const shuffled = [...pool].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
      return shuffled.slice(0, 5).map(f => f.key);
    }

    function getActiveSeasonFacilities() {
      if (!career) return FACILITY_POOL.slice(0, 5);
      if (!Array.isArray(career.activeFacilityKeys) || career.activeFacilityKeys.length !== 5) {
        career.activeFacilityKeys = rotateSeasonFacilities(career.season || 1, []);
      }
      return career.activeFacilityKeys
        .map(k => FACILITY_POOL.find(f => f.key === k))
        .filter(Boolean);
    }

    function getFacilityLevel(key) {
      if (!career || !career.facilities) return 1;
      return Math.max(1, Math.min(5, career.facilities[key] || 1));
    }

    function getCombinedFacilityBonuses() {
      const s = getFacilityLevel('stadium') - 1;
      const tr = getFacilityLevel('training') - 1;
      const sc = getFacilityLevel('scouting') - 1;
      const md = getFacilityLevel('medical') - 1;
      const lx = getFacilityLevel('luxurySuites') - 1;
      const qb = getFacilityLevel('qbAcademy') - 1;
      const sp = getFacilityLevel('speedTrack') - 1;
      const cc = getFacilityLevel('collegeCombine') - 1;
      const cr = getFacilityLevel('cryoChamber') - 1;
      const fn = getFacilityLevel('fanEngagement') - 1;
      const df = getFacilityLevel('defensiveLab') - 1;

      return {
        winCcBonus: s + lx + fn,
        capBoostM: s * 4 + lx * 3 + fn * 5,
        trainOddsBonus: tr * 0.04 + qb * 0.025 + sp * 0.02 + df * 0.025,
        declineMitigation: tr * 0.06 + cr * 0.05,
        superstarOddsBonus: sc * 0.02 + cc * 0.02,
        staminaReduction: md * 0.05 + sp * 0.04,
        extraCareerYears: md + cr,
        qbPeakBonus: qb,
        defPeakBonus: df
      };
    }

    function getPlayerSoftMaxOvr(player) {
      const age = player.age || 25;
      const superBonus = player.isSuperstar ? 4 : 0;
      const b = getCombinedFacilityBonuses();
      const posBonus = player.pos === 'QB' ? b.qbPeakBonus : (['LB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(player.pos) ? b.defPeakBonus : 0);
      if (age <= 22) return Math.min(97, 84 + superBonus + posBonus);
      if (age <= 24) return Math.min(97, 87 + superBonus + posBonus);
      if (age <= 27) return Math.min(97, 90 + superBonus + posBonus);
      return Math.min(97, 88 + superBonus + posBonus);
    }

    function getSeasonalDrillLimit() {
      // Weight Room & Lab unlocks 1 extra weekly team drill per 2 facility levels (max 6 per season)
      return 4 + Math.floor((getFacilityLevel('training') - 1) / 2);
    }

    function runBalancedSquadTraining(focusPlayerIdx = null) {
      if (!career || !Array.isArray(career.roster) || !career.roster.length) return [];
      const bonuses = getCombinedFacilityBonuses();
      const maxDrillsPerSeason = getSeasonalDrillLimit();
      const drillsUsed = career.seasonDrillsUsed || 0;
      if (drillsUsed >= maxDrillsPerSeason) {
        return 'SEASON_CAP_REACHED';
      }

      // Escalating CC cost per drill within the same season: 10, 14, 18, 22...
      const drillCost = 10 + drillsUsed * 4;
      if (career.cc < drillCost) return null;

      // Choose 2 to 3 players across the roster (prioritizing focus player if clicked, plus random teammates)
      const eligible = career.roster.filter(p => (p.ovr || 70) < getPlayerSoftMaxOvr(p));
      if (!eligible.length) {
        return [];
      }

      career.cc -= drillCost;
      career.seasonDrillsUsed = drillsUsed + 1;
      const summary = [];

      const selected = [];
      if (focusPlayerIdx !== null && career.roster[focusPlayerIdx]) {
        const fp = career.roster[focusPlayerIdx];
        if ((fp.ovr || 70) < getPlayerSoftMaxOvr(fp)) selected.push(fp);
      }

      const pool = eligible.filter(p => !selected.includes(p)).sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
      const targetGroupSize = Math.min(eligible.length, 2 + (Math.random() < 0.35 ? 1 : 0));
      while (selected.length < targetGroupSize && pool.length > 0) {
        selected.push(pool.shift());
      }

      selected.forEach(pl => {
        const softCap = getPlayerSoftMaxOvr(pl);
        const curOvr = pl.ovr || 72;
        const gap = Math.max(0, softCap - curOvr);
        if (gap <= 0) return;

        // Steep diminishing returns as OVR climbs: 86+ rarely gains +1; 70s gain +1 (and +2 only rarely)
        const facilityBonus = bonuses.trainOddsBonus;
        const baseChance = curOvr >= 88 ? 0.16 : curOvr >= 84 ? 0.28 : curOvr >= 79 ? 0.44 : 0.62;
        const successChance = Math.min(0.82, baseChance + facilityBonus);

        let ovrGain = 0;
        if (Math.random() < successChance) {
          ovrGain = (curOvr < 76 && gap >= 4 && Math.random() < (0.14 + facilityBonus * 0.35)) ? 2 : 1;
          ovrGain = Math.min(gap, ovrGain);
        }

        // Distribute 1 random sub-attribute bump occasionally (strictly capped so attributes take multiple seasons)
        let attrNote = '';
        const attrChance = 0.14 + facilityBonus * 0.45;
        if (Math.random() < attrChance) {
          const attrPool = pl.pos === 'QB' ? ['arm', 'acc', 'spd'] : ['spd', 'acc'];
          const chosenAttr = attrPool[Math.floor(Math.random() * attrPool.length)];
          const attrCap = pl.isSuperstar ? 10 : 9;
          if ((pl[chosenAttr] || 7) < attrCap) {
            pl[chosenAttr] = (pl[chosenAttr] || 7) + 1;
            attrNote = ` (+1 ${chosenAttr.toUpperCase()})`;
          }
        }

        if (ovrGain > 0 || attrNote) {
          pl.ovr = Math.min(softCap, curOvr + ovrGain);
          summary.push(`${pl.name}: ${ovrGain > 0 ? `+${ovrGain} OVR` : 'DRILL REP'}${attrNote}`);
        } else {
          summary.push(`${pl.name}: CONDITIONING (+0 OVR)`);
        }
      });

      return summary;
    }

    function getCapScaleMultiplier() {
      const capLimit = (career && career.capLimit) ? career.capLimit : BASE_SALARY_CAP;
      return capLimit / BASE_SALARY_CAP;
    }

    function calculateProportionalSalary(pos, ovr, isSuperstar = false) {
      const normPos = normalizePlayerPosition(pos);
      // Base Season 1 ($175M cap) positional salary tiers
      let baseSal = 2;
      if (normPos === 'QB') {
        baseSal = ovr >= 93 ? 16 : ovr >= 88 ? 13 : ovr >= 83 ? 9 : ovr >= 78 ? 6 : 3;
      } else if (normPos === 'WR' || normPos === 'CB' || normPos === 'LB') {
        baseSal = ovr >= 92 ? 12 : ovr >= 86 ? 9 : ovr >= 80 ? 6 : ovr >= 75 ? 3 : 2;
      } else if (normPos === 'RB' || normPos === 'TE' || normPos === 'S') {
        baseSal = ovr >= 91 ? 10 : ovr >= 85 ? 7 : ovr >= 79 ? 5 : ovr >= 74 ? 3 : 2;
      }
      if (isSuperstar && baseSal < 12) baseSal += 2;

      // Scale strictly proportionally to current cap vs $175M base cap (never exceeding the 8%/yr cap growth rate)
      const capScale = getCapScaleMultiplier();
      const maxPosCapShare = normPos === 'QB' ? 0.10 : 0.08;
      const currentCap = (career && career.capLimit) ? career.capLimit : BASE_SALARY_CAP;
      const scaled = Math.round(baseSal * capScale);
      const proportionalCeiling = Math.max(2, Math.floor(currentCap * maxPosCapShare));
      return Math.max(2, Math.min(scaled, proportionalCeiling));
    }

    function getSoftCapStatus() {
      const capBonus = getCombinedFacilityBonuses().capBoostM;
      const capLimit = ((career && career.capLimit) ? career.capLimit : BASE_SALARY_CAP) + capBonus;
      const payroll = getPayroll();
      const overBy = Math.max(0, payroll - capLimit);
      let penaltySpots = 0;
      let tierLabel = 'UNDER CAP (NO PENALTY)';
      if (overBy > 100) {
        penaltySpots = 2;
        tierLabel = `+$${overBy}M OVER CAP (-2 ROSTER SPOTS)`;
      } else if (overBy > 0) {
        penaltySpots = 1;
        tierLabel = `+$${overBy}M OVER CAP (-1 ROSTER SPOT)`;
      }
      const maxRoster = BASE_ROSTER_LIMIT - penaltySpots;
      return {
        capLimit,
        payroll,
        capSpace: capLimit - payroll,
        overBy,
        penaltySpots,
        maxRoster,
        tierLabel
      };
    }

    function normalizePlayerPosition(pos) {
      if (pos === 'DB') return 'CB';
      if (pos === 'DL') return 'LB';
      return DRAFT_POSITIONS.includes(pos) ? pos : 'WR';
    }

    function autoAssignStarters(rosterList) {
      if (!Array.isArray(rosterList)) return;
      Object.keys(STARTER_SLOTS_BY_POS).forEach(pos => {
        const limit = STARTER_SLOTS_BY_POS[pos];
        const candidates = rosterList
          .filter(p => normalizePlayerPosition(p.pos) === pos)
          .sort((a, b) => (b.isStarter ? 1 : 0) - (a.isStarter ? 1 : 0) || (b.ovr || 7) - (a.ovr || 7));
        candidates.forEach((p, idx) => {
          p.pos = pos;
          p.isStarter = idx < limit;
        });
      });
    }

    function generateFullNoLinemanRoster(team) {
      const firstNames = [
        'MARCUS', 'JAYLEN', 'DEVON', 'CALEB', 'TREVOR', 'MALIK', 'BRYCE', 'ZAY', 'NICO', 'BROCK',
        'JAXON', 'TYRONE', 'KOBE', 'DARIUS', 'MICAH', 'XAVIER', 'QUENTIN', 'NATHAN', 'ISAIAH', 'JALEN',
        'TREVON', 'DARNELL', 'CAMERON', 'TERRENCE', 'CHRIS', 'KYLE', 'BRANDON', 'JORDAN', 'DEVONTE', 'KHALIL',
        'ANDRE', 'DESMOND', 'KEVIN', 'ELIJAH', 'NOAH', 'MASON', 'LOGAN', 'HUNTER', 'COLE', 'GRANT'
      ];
      const lastNames = [
        'VANCE', 'STEELE', 'MERCER', 'KNOX', 'GRAVES', 'CARTER', 'CROSS', 'REED', 'BROOKS', 'HUNTER',
        'COLE', 'BANKS', 'HAYES', 'FORD', 'WALKER', 'MITCHELL', 'BENNETT', 'SIMMONS', 'HARPER', 'DOUGLAS',
        'WASHINGTON', 'SCOTT', 'CLARK', 'DAVIS', 'ROBINSON', 'KING', 'WILLIAMS', 'WHITE', 'WRIGHT', 'YOUNG',
        'HARRIS', 'JACKSON', 'THOMAS', 'TAYLOR', 'MOORE', 'MARTIN', 'LEE', 'PEREZ', 'THOMPSON', 'ALLEN'
      ];

      const used = new Set();
      const roster = [];

      // Import team's signature real-world stars first (mapping DL->LB, DB->CB so zero linemen exist)
      (team.stars || []).forEach((s, idx) => {
        const mappedPos = normalizePlayerPosition(s.pos);
        const ovr80 = s.ovr <= 12 ? Math.min(96, 68 + s.ovr * 2.7) : s.ovr;
        used.add(s.name);
        roster.push({
          id: `${team.id}_STAR_${idx}_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 999)}`,
          name: s.name,
          pos: mappedPos,
          ovr: Math.round(ovr80),
          spd: s.spd || 8,
          arm: mappedPos === 'QB' ? (s.arm || 9) : 4,
          acc: s.acc || 8,
          age: 25 + (idx % 3),
          salary: calculateProportionalSalary(mappedPos, Math.round(ovr80), s.ovr >= 9),
          contract: 3,
          isSuperstar: s.ovr >= 9,
          isStarter: true
        });
      });

      // Scaled 34-man target breakdown across offense skill + defense LB & Secondary (no linemen)
      const targetCounts = { QB: 3, RB: 4, WR: 7, TE: 3, LB: 7, CB: 6, S: 4 };
      const tierBoost = team.tier === 'DYNASTY' ? 3 : team.tier === 'CONTENDER' ? 2 : team.tier === 'PLAYOFF' ? 1 : 0;

      Object.entries(targetCounts).forEach(([pos, count]) => {
        while (roster.filter(p => p.pos === pos).length < count) {
          const curAtPos = roster.filter(p => p.pos === pos).length;
          let fullName = '';
          let tries = 0;
          do {
            const fn = firstNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * firstNames.length)];
            const ln = lastNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * lastNames.length)];
            fullName = `${fn} ${ln}`;
            tries++;
          } while (used.has(fullName) && tries < 30);
          used.add(fullName);

          const isStarterTier = curAtPos < (STARTER_SLOTS_BY_POS[pos] || 1);
          const ovr = isStarterTier
            ? Math.min(89, 76 + tierBoost + Math.floor(Math.random() * 8))
            : Math.min(80, 68 + tierBoost + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7));
          const attr = Math.max(6, Math.min(9, Math.round((ovr - 45) / 4.5)));
          const salary = calculateProportionalSalary(pos, ovr, false);

          roster.push({
            id: `${team.id}_${pos}_${curAtPos}_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 9999)}`,
            name: fullName,
            pos,
            ovr,
            spd: Math.min(10, attr + (['WR', 'RB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(pos) ? 1 : 0)),
            arm: pos === 'QB' ? Math.min(10, attr + 1) : 4,
            acc: attr,
            age: 22 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7),
            salary,
            contract: 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3),
            isSuperstar: false,
            isStarter: isStarterTier
          });
        }
      });

      autoAssignStarters(roster);
      return roster;
    }

    function generateInitialFreeAgents(count = 28) {
      const firstNames = ['DEVON', 'TARIK', 'MALCOLM', 'JOSHUA', 'BRIAN', 'KENNY', 'CURTIS', 'MARLON', 'TRE', 'DEANDRE', 'JALEN', 'RASHEED', 'COREY', 'TERRELL'];
      const lastNames = ['BOYD', 'FLOWERS', 'JENKINS', 'MURRAY', 'FULLER', 'JONES', 'HARMON', 'GRIFFIN', 'BELL', 'HOWARD', 'PETERSON', 'GILMORE', 'CLOWNEY', 'MATHIEU'];
      const pool = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const pos = DRAFT_POSITIONS[i % DRAFT_POSITIONS.length];
        const fn = firstNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * firstNames.length)];
        const ln = lastNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * lastNames.length)];
        const ovr = 70 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 13); // 70 - 82 OVR
        const attr = Math.max(6, Math.min(9, Math.round((ovr - 45) / 4.5)));
        pool.push({
          id: `FA_${i}_${Date.now()}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 9999)}`,
          name: `${fn} ${ln}`,
          pos,
          ovr,
          spd: Math.min(10, attr + (['WR', 'RB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(pos) ? 1 : 0)),
          arm: pos === 'QB' ? Math.min(10, attr + 1) : 4,
          acc: attr,
          age: 24 + (i % 6),
          salary: calculateProportionalSalary(pos, ovr, false),
          contract: 2,
          isSuperstar: false,
          isStarter: false
        });
      }
      return pool.sort((a, b) => b.ovr - a.ovr);
    }

    function computeRosterUnitStars(rosterList, fallbackOff = 4, fallbackDef = 4) {
      if (!Array.isArray(rosterList) || !rosterList.length) {
        return { off: fallbackOff, def: fallbackDef };
      }
      const offStarters = rosterList.filter(p => p.isStarter && ['QB', 'RB', 'WR', 'TE'].includes(p.pos));
      const defStarters = rosterList.filter(p => p.isStarter && ['LB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(p.pos));
      const offPlayers = offStarters.length ? offStarters : rosterList.filter(p => ['QB', 'RB', 'WR', 'TE'].includes(p.pos));
      const defPlayers = defStarters.length ? defStarters : rosterList.filter(p => ['LB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(p.pos));
      const toStar = (ovrVal) => (ovrVal > 12 ? (ovrVal - 64) / 5.6 : ovrVal / 1.9);
      const avgOff = offPlayers.length ? offPlayers.reduce((s, p) => s + toStar(p.ovr), 0) / offPlayers.length : fallbackOff;
      const avgDef = defPlayers.length ? defPlayers.reduce((s, p) => s + toStar(p.ovr), 0) / defPlayers.length : fallbackDef;
      return {
        off: Math.max(2, Math.min(5, Math.round(avgOff))),
        def: Math.max(2, Math.min(5, Math.round(avgDef)))
      };
    }

    function getTeamRatings(teamId) {
      const base = getUserTeam(teamId);
      if (career && career.teamId === teamId && career.roster && career.roster.length) {
        return computeRosterUnitStars(career.roster, base.off, base.def);
      }
      if (career && career.leagueRosters && Array.isArray(career.leagueRosters[teamId]) && career.leagueRosters[teamId].length) {
        return computeRosterUnitStars(career.leagueRosters[teamId], base.off, base.def);
      }
      return { off: base.off, def: base.def };
    }

    const DRAFT_POSITIONS = ['QB', 'RB', 'WR', 'TE', 'LB', 'CB', 'S'];
    const DRAFT_ROUNDS = 4; // 4 rounds (128 picks) tailored to no-lineman rosters (QB, RB, WR, TE, LB, CB, S)

    function computeDraftPickOrder() {
      const champId = career?.playoffs?.championId || null;
      const runnerUpId = (career?.playoffs?.superBowl?.played)
        ? (career.playoffs.superBowl.afcId === champId ? career.playoffs.superBowl.nfcId : career.playoffs.superBowl.afcId)
        : null;

      const sorted = [...TEAMS].sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.id === champId) return 1;
        if (b.id === champId) return -1;
        if (a.id === runnerUpId) return 1;
        if (b.id === runnerUpId) return -1;
        // Worst record picks first (reverse of NFL standings sort)
        return compareTeamsNFL(b, a, false);
      });

      return sorted.map(t => t.id);
    }

    function generateDraftClass(seasonNum = 1) {
      const firstNames = [
        'MARCUS', 'JAYLEN', 'DEVON', 'CALEB', 'TREVOR', 'MALIK', 'BRYCE', 'ZAY', 'NICO', 'BROCK',
        'JAXON', 'TYRONE', 'KOBE', 'DARIUS', 'MICAH', 'XAVIER', 'QUENTIN', 'NATHAN', 'ISAIAH', 'JALEN',
        'TREVON', 'DARNELL', 'CAMERON', 'TERRENCE', 'CHRIS', 'KYLE', 'BRANDON', 'JORDAN', 'DEVONTE', 'KHALIL'
      ];
      const lastNames = [
        'VANCE', 'STEELE', 'MERCER', 'KNOX', 'GRAVES', 'CARTER', 'CROSS', 'REED', 'BROOKS', 'HUNTER',
        'COLE', 'BANKS', 'HAYES', 'FORD', 'WALKER', 'MITCHELL', 'BENNETT', 'SIMMONS', 'HARPER', 'DOUGLAS',
        'WASHINGTON', 'SCOTT', 'CLARK', 'DAVIS', 'ROBINSON', 'KING', 'WILLIAMS', 'WHITE', 'WRIGHT', 'YOUNG'
      ];

      const usedNames = new Set();
      const list = [];
      const totalProspects = 144; // Enough prospects for 4 rounds x 32 teams (128 picks) + UDFA pool

      for (let i = 0; i < totalProspects; i++) {
        let fullName = '';
        let tries = 0;
        do {
          const fn = firstNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * firstNames.length)];
          const ln = lastNames[Math.floor(Math.random() * lastNames.length)];
          fullName = `${fn} ${ln}`;
          tries++;
        } while (usedNames.has(fullName) && tries < 25);
        usedNames.add(fullName);

        const pos = DRAFT_POSITIONS[(i + Math.floor(Math.random() * DRAFT_POSITIONS.length)) % DRAFT_POSITIONS.length];

        // Realistic draft prospect OVR distribution (66 - 86 OVR)
        let ovr;
        if (i < 12) {
          // Top Round 1 prospects: Mid-80s (82-86)
          ovr = 82 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
        } else if (i < 36) {
          // Late 1st / Early 2nd: High 70s to low 80s (78-82)
          ovr = 78 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
        } else if (i < 84) {
          // Middle-round prospects (Rounds 2-3): Mid-to-high 70s (73-78)
          ovr = 73 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 6);
        } else {
          // Late-round prospects (Round 4): High 60s to low 70s (66-72)
          ovr = 66 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 7);
        }

        // Convert OVR to 1-10 on-field gameplay ratings (spd, arm, acc)
        const baseAttr = Math.max(5, Math.min(9, Math.round((ovr - 45) / 4.5)));
        const spd = Math.min(10, Math.max(5, baseAttr + (['WR', 'RB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(pos) ? 1 : 0)));
        const arm = pos === 'QB' ? Math.min(10, Math.max(6, baseAttr + 1)) : 4;
        const acc = Math.min(10, Math.max(5, baseAttr));
        const rookieSalary = Math.min(
          calculateProportionalSalary(pos, ovr, false),
          Math.max(2, Math.round((ovr >= 83 ? 6 : ovr >= 79 ? 4 : ovr >= 74 ? 3 : 2) * getCapScaleMultiplier()))
        );

        list.push({
          id: `ROOKIE_S${seasonNum}_${i}_${Math.floor(Math.random() * 9999)}`,
          name: fullName,
          pos,
          ovr,
          spd,
          arm,
          acc,
          age: 21 + (i % 2),
          salary: rookieSalary,
          contract: 4,
          isSuperstar: false,
          draftRank: i + 1
        });
      }

      // Sort board from highest OVR prospect to sleeper prospects
      list.sort((a, b) => b.ovr - a.ovr || b.spd - a.spd);
      list.forEach((p, idx) => { p.draftRank = idx + 1; });
      return list;
    }

    function applySuperstarRollOnDraft(prospect) {
      // Base 5% (1-in-20) chance + Scouting HQ & Combine Hub bonuses
      const superstarOdds = 0.05 + getCombinedFacilityBonuses().superstarOddsBonus;
      const isBreakout = Math.random() < superstarOdds;
      if (isBreakout) {
        const boost = 7 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 4); // +7 to +10 OVR jump
        prospect.ovr = Math.min(97, prospect.ovr + boost);
        prospect.spd = Math.min(10, prospect.spd + 1);
        prospect.acc = Math.min(10, prospect.acc + 1);
        if (prospect.pos === 'QB') prospect.arm = Math.min(10, prospect.arm + 1);
        prospect.isSuperstar = true;
      }
      return isBreakout;
    }

    function initializeOffseasonDraft() {
      if (!career) return;
      // Ensure every team (both user and CPU) clears enough roster spots for the incoming 4-round draft
      // by auto-cutting their lowest-overall players into Free Agency if needed
      clearRosterSpaceForDraft(DRAFT_ROUNDS);
      const order = computeDraftPickOrder();
      const prospects = generateDraftClass((career.season || 1) + 1);
      career.draftClass = prospects;
      career.draftState = {
        active: true,
        complete: false,
        rounds: DRAFT_ROUNDS,
        order,
        pickIndex: 0, // 0 .. (32 * DRAFT_ROUNDS - 1)
        history: []
      };
      advanceCpuDraftPicks();
    }

    function autoTrimRosterToTarget(rosterList, targetMaxCount) {
      const cutPlayers = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(rosterList)) return cutPlayers;
      while (rosterList.length > targetMaxCount && rosterList.length > 14) {
        // Sort lowest OVR first (preferring non-superstars and non-starters when tied)
        let lowestIdx = 0;
        for (let i = 1; i < rosterList.length; i++) {
          const cur = rosterList[i];
          const best = rosterList[lowestIdx];
          if ((cur.ovr || 0) < (best.ovr || 0) ||
             ((cur.ovr || 0) === (best.ovr || 0) && !cur.isStarter && best.isStarter)) {
            lowestIdx = i;
          }
        }
        const cutPlayer = rosterList.splice(lowestIdx, 1)[0];
        if (cutPlayer) {
          cutPlayer.isStarter = false;
          cutPlayers.push(cutPlayer);
        }
      }
      autoAssignStarters(rosterList);
      return cutPlayers;
    }

    function clearRosterSpaceForDraft(incomingDraftSlots = DRAFT_ROUNDS) {
      if (!career) return;
      if (!Array.isArray(career.freeAgents)) career.freeAgents = generateInitialFreeAgents(28);
      if (!career.leagueRosters) career.leagueRosters = {};

      // 1. Auto-cut lowest OVR players on the user's team if active roster + incoming picks exceeds soft-cap roster limit
      const userCapStatus = getSoftCapStatus();
      const userTargetMax = Math.max(14, userCapStatus.maxRoster - incomingDraftSlots);
      const userCuts = autoTrimRosterToTarget(career.roster, userTargetMax);
      if (userCuts.length > 0) {
        userCuts.forEach(p => career.freeAgents.unshift(p));
        const names = userCuts.map(p => `${p.name} (${p.ovr})`).join(', ');
        showToast(`DRAFT ROSTER CLEAR: AUTO-CUT ${userCuts.length} LOWEST OVR TO FA (${names})`, 4200);
      }

      // 2. Auto-cut lowest OVR players on all 31 CPU franchises so they also have room for incoming rookies
      const cpuTargetMax = Math.max(14, BASE_ROSTER_LIMIT - incomingDraftSlots);
      TEAMS.forEach(t => {
        if (t.id === career.teamId) return;
        if (!Array.isArray(career.leagueRosters[t.id]) || !career.leagueRosters[t.id].length) {
          career.leagueRosters[t.id] = generateFullNoLinemanRoster(t);
        }
        const cpuCuts = autoTrimRosterToTarget(career.leagueRosters[t.id], cpuTargetMax);
        cpuCuts.forEach(p => career.freeAgents.push(p));
      });

      career.freeAgents.sort((a, b) => (b.ovr || 0) - (a.ovr || 0));
    }

    function advanceCpuDraftPicks() {
      if (!career || !career.draftState || career.draftState.complete) return;
      const ds = career.draftState;
      const totalPicks = ds.order.length * ds.rounds;

      while (ds.pickIndex < totalPicks) {
        const teamId = ds.order[ds.pickIndex % ds.order.length];
        if (teamId === career.teamId) {
          // Pause on user's pick!
          break;
        }
        const round = Math.floor(ds.pickIndex / ds.order.length) + 1;
        const pickOverall = ds.pickIndex + 1;

        if (career.draftClass && career.draftClass.length > 0) {
          // CPU selects among top 3 available prospects with slight positional variance
          const poolSize = Math.min(3, career.draftClass.length);
          const pickIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * poolSize);
          const chosen = career.draftClass.splice(pickIdx, 1)[0];
          const breakout = applySuperstarRollOnDraft(chosen);
          chosen.isStarter = false;
          if (career.leagueRosters && Array.isArray(career.leagueRosters[teamId])) {
            career.leagueRosters[teamId].push(chosen);
            // Trim lowest-rated non-starter if CPU exceeds 34-man roster limit
            if (career.leagueRosters[teamId].length > BASE_ROSTER_LIMIT) {
              career.leagueRosters[teamId].sort((a, b) => (b.ovr || 0) - (a.ovr || 0));
              const cutPlayer = career.leagueRosters[teamId].pop();
              if (cutPlayer) {
                cutPlayer.isStarter = false;
                career.freeAgents.push(cutPlayer);
              }
            }
            autoAssignStarters(career.leagueRosters[teamId]);
          }
          ds.history.unshift({
            round,
            pickOverall,
            teamId,
            playerName: chosen.name,
            pos: chosen.pos,
            ovr: chosen.ovr,
            isSuperstar: breakout
          });
        }
        ds.pickIndex += 1;
      }

      if (ds.pickIndex >= totalPicks) {
        ds.complete = true;
        ds.active = false;
      }
    }

    function simSingleMatchupScore(homeId, awayId) {
      const hRat = getTeamRatings(homeId);
      const aRat = getTeamRatings(awayId);
      const hPower = hRat.off * 3.2 + hRat.def * 2.4 + 2.5 + Math.random() * 14;
      const aPower = aRat.off * 3.2 + aRat.def * 2.4 + Math.random() * 14;
      let homeScore = Math.max(7, Math.round(hPower));
      let awayScore = Math.max(3, Math.round(aPower));
      if (homeScore === awayScore) homeScore += 3;
      return { homeScore, awayScore };
    }

    function applyGameResultToStandings(homeId, awayId, homeScore, awayScore) {
      if (!career || !career.standings) return;
      if (!career.standings[homeId]) career.standings[homeId] = createEmptyStandingRecord();
      if (!career.standings[awayId]) career.standings[awayId] = createEmptyStandingRecord();

      const hRec = career.standings[homeId];
      const aRec = career.standings[awayId];
      const hTeam = getUserTeam(homeId);
      const aTeam = getUserTeam(awayId);
      const sameConf = hTeam.conf === aTeam.conf;
      const sameDiv = sameConf && hTeam.div === aTeam.div;

      hRec.pf += homeScore;
      hRec.pa += awayScore;
      aRec.pf += awayScore;
      aRec.pa += homeScore;

      if (homeScore >= awayScore) {
        hRec.w += 1;
        aRec.l += 1;
        if (sameConf) { hRec.confW += 1; aRec.confL += 1; }
        if (sameDiv)  { hRec.divW += 1;  aRec.divL += 1; }
      } else {
        aRec.w += 1;
        hRec.l += 1;
        if (sameConf) { aRec.confW += 1; hRec.confL += 1; }
        if (sameDiv)  { aRec.divW += 1;  hRec.divL += 1; }
      }
    }

    function simulateLeagueWeek(weekNum, skipUserTeam = true) {
      if (!career || !career.weekGames || !career.weekGames[weekNum - 1]) return;
      career.weekGames[weekNum - 1].forEach(g => {
        if (g.played) return;
        const hId = g.homeId || g.home;
        const aId = g.awayId || g.away;
        if (skipUserTeam && (hId === career.teamId || aId === career.teamId)) return;
        const res = simSingleMatchupScore(hId, aId);
        g.homeId = hId;
        g.awayId = aId;
        g.played = true;
        g.homeScore = res.homeScore;
        g.awayScore = res.awayScore;
        applyGameResultToStandings(hId, aId, res.homeScore, res.awayScore);
      });
    }

    function checkRegularSeasonComplete() {
      if (!career || career.phase !== 'REGULAR') return;
      const allDone = career.schedule.every(g => g.played);
      if (allDone) {
        for (let w = 1; w <= 18; w++) simulateLeagueWeek(w, false);
        career.phase = 'PLAYOFFS';
        career.playoffs = buildPlayoffBracket();
      }
    }

    function getUserActivePlayoffMatchup() {
      if (!career || career.phase !== 'PLAYOFFS' || !career.playoffs) return null;
      const po = career.playoffs;
      const userTeam = getUserTeam(career.teamId);
      const confBracket = po[userTeam.conf];

      if (po.round === 'WILDCARD' && confBracket) {
        const m = confBracket.wildCard.find(x => !x.played && (x.highId === career.teamId || x.lowId === career.teamId));
        if (m) return { round: 'WILDCARD', roundLabel: 'WILD CARD ROUND', oppId: m.highId === career.teamId ? m.lowId : m.highId, matchObj: m };
      } else if (po.round === 'DIVISIONAL' && confBracket) {
        const m = confBracket.divisional.find(x => !x.played && (x.highId === career.teamId || x.lowId === career.teamId));
        if (m) return { round: 'DIVISIONAL', roundLabel: 'DIVISIONAL ROUND', oppId: m.highId === career.teamId ? m.lowId : m.highId, matchObj: m };
      } else if (po.round === 'CHAMPIONSHIP' && confBracket && confBracket.championship && !confBracket.championship.played) {
        const m = confBracket.championship;
        if (m.highId === career.teamId || m.lowId === career.teamId) {
          return { round: 'CHAMPIONSHIP', roundLabel: `${userTeam.conf} CHAMPIONSHIP`, oppId: m.highId === career.teamId ? m.lowId : m.highId, matchObj: m };
        }
      } else if (po.round === 'SUPERBOWL' && po.superBowl && !po.superBowl.played) {
        const m = po.superBowl;
        if (m.afcId === career.teamId || m.nfcId === career.teamId) {
          return { round: 'SUPERBOWL', roundLabel: 'SUPER BOWL', oppId: m.afcId === career.teamId ? m.nfcId : m.afcId, matchObj: m };
        }
      }
      return null;
    }

    function hasUserFirstRoundBye() {
      if (!career || !career.playoffs || career.playoffs.round !== 'WILDCARD') return false;
      const userTeam = getUserTeam(career.teamId);
      const seedEntry = career.playoffs[userTeam.conf]?.seeds?.find(s => s.teamId === career.teamId);
      return Boolean(seedEntry && seedEntry.hasBye);
    }

    function simulatePlayoffRound(skipUserMatch = false) {
      if (!career || !career.playoffs) return;
      const po = career.playoffs;

      const simMatch = (m) => {
        if (m.played) return;
        if (skipUserMatch && (m.highId === career.teamId || m.lowId === career.teamId)) return;
        const res = simSingleMatchupScore(m.highId, m.lowId);
        m.highScore = res.homeScore;
        m.lowScore = res.awayScore;
        m.played = true;
        m.winnerId = m.highScore >= m.lowScore ? m.highId : m.lowId;
      };

      if (po.round === 'WILDCARD') {
        ['AFC', 'NFC'].forEach(c => po[c].wildCard.forEach(simMatch));
        if (['AFC', 'NFC'].every(c => po[c].wildCard.every(m => m.played))) {
          ['AFC', 'NFC'].forEach(c => {
            const cb = po[c];
            const s1 = cb.seeds[0].teamId;
            const s2 = cb.seeds[1].teamId;
            const w1 = cb.wildCard[0].winnerId;
            const w2 = cb.wildCard[1].winnerId;
            const getSeedNum = (tid) => cb.seeds.find(s => s.teamId === tid)?.seed || 6;
            const winners = [w1, w2].sort((a, b) => getSeedNum(a) - getSeedNum(b));
            cb.divisional = [
              { id: 'DIV1', highSeed: 1, highId: s1, lowSeed: getSeedNum(winners[1]), lowId: winners[1], played: false, highScore: 0, lowScore: 0, winnerId: null },
              { id: 'DIV2', highSeed: 2, highId: s2, lowSeed: getSeedNum(winners[0]), lowId: winners[0], played: false, highScore: 0, lowScore: 0, winnerId: null }
            ];
          });
          po.round = 'DIVISIONAL';
        }
      } else if (po.round === 'DIVISIONAL') {
        ['AFC', 'NFC'].forEach(c => po[c].divisional.forEach(simMatch));
        if (['AFC', 'NFC'].every(c => po[c].divisional.every(m => m.played))) {
          ['AFC', 'NFC'].forEach(c => {
            const cb = po[c];
            const d1 = cb.divisional[0].winnerId;
            const d2 = cb.divisional[1].winnerId;
            const getSeedNum = (tid) => cb.seeds.find(s => s.teamId === tid)?.seed || 4;
            const ordered = [d1, d2].sort((a, b) => getSeedNum(a) - getSeedNum(b));
            cb.championship = {
              id: 'CONF_CHAMP',
              highSeed: getSeedNum(ordered[0]),
              highId: ordered[0],
              lowSeed: getSeedNum(ordered[1]),
              lowId: ordered[1],
              played: false,
              highScore: 0,
              lowScore: 0,
              winnerId: null
            };
          });
          po.round = 'CHAMPIONSHIP';
        }
      } else if (po.round === 'CHAMPIONSHIP') {
        ['AFC', 'NFC'].forEach(c => {
          if (po[c].championship) simMatch(po[c].championship);
        });
        if (po.AFC.championship?.played && po.NFC.championship?.played) {
          po.superBowl = {
            afcId: po.AFC.championship.winnerId,
            nfcId: po.NFC.championship.winnerId,
            played: false,
            afcScore: 0,
            nfcScore: 0,
            winnerId: null
          };
          po.round = 'SUPERBOWL';
        }
      } else if (po.round === 'SUPERBOWL' && po.superBowl) {
        const sb = po.superBowl;
        if (!sb.played) {
          if (skipUserMatch && (sb.afcId === career.teamId || sb.nfcId === career.teamId)) return;
          const res = simSingleMatchupScore(sb.afcId, sb.nfcId);
          sb.afcScore = res.homeScore;
          sb.nfcScore = res.awayScore;
          sb.played = true;
          sb.winnerId = sb.afcScore >= sb.nfcScore ? sb.afcId : sb.nfcId;
        }
        if (sb.played) {
          po.championId = sb.winnerId;
          if (sb.winnerId === career.teamId) {
            career.rings = (career.rings || 0) + 1;
            career.cc += 8;
            showToast('SUPER BOWL CHAMPIONS!! +1 RING & +8 CC! DRAFT UNLOCKED.');
          } else {
            const champ = getUserTeam(sb.winnerId);
            showToast(`${champ.city} ${champ.name} WIN THE SUPER BOWL! NFL DRAFT UNLOCKED.`);
          }
          career.phase = 'OFFSEASON';
          initializeOffseasonDraft();
        }
      }
      saveCareer();
    }

    function progressSingleRosterOffseason(rosterList, teamLabel, historyLog, retiredLog, isUserTeam = false) {
      const retained = [];
      const expiredToFA = [];
      const bonuses = isUserTeam ? getCombinedFacilityBonuses() : { declineMitigation: 0, extraCareerYears: 0 };
      const declineMitigation = bonuses.declineMitigation;
      const extraCareerYears = bonuses.extraCareerYears;

      rosterList.forEach(p => {
        p.age = (p.age || 25) + 1;
        p.contract = (p.contract || 2) - 1;
        p.seasonsPlayed = (p.seasonsPlayed || 0) + 1;

        // Realistic multi-generation player lifecycle: young growth, prime plateau, veteran decline & retirement
        if (p.age <= 25) {
          const growth = Math.random() < (p.isSuperstar ? 0.65 : 0.45) ? (1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 2)) : 0;
          p.ovr = Math.min(97, (p.ovr || 75) + growth);
          if (growth > 0 && Math.random() < 0.4) p.spd = Math.min(10, (p.spd || 7) + 1);
          if (growth > 0 && Math.random() < 0.4) p.acc = Math.min(10, (p.acc || 7) + 1);
        } else if (p.age >= 30 && p.age < (34 + extraCareerYears)) {
          const declineProb = Math.max(0.15, (p.isSuperstar ? 0.35 : 0.55) - declineMitigation);
          const decline = Math.random() < declineProb ? 1 : 0;
          p.ovr = Math.max(64, (p.ovr || 75) - decline);
          if (p.age >= 32 && Math.random() < Math.max(0.12, 0.35 - declineMitigation)) p.spd = Math.max(5, (p.spd || 7) - 1);
        } else if (p.age >= (34 + extraCareerYears)) {
          const retireChance = p.age >= (37 + extraCareerYears) ? 0.78 : (p.isSuperstar ? 0.38 : 0.52);
          if (Math.random() < retireChance) {
            if (isUserTeam || p.isSuperstar || p.ovr >= 86) {
              retiredLog.unshift({
                season: career.season - 1,
                teamId: teamLabel,
                name: p.name,
                pos: p.pos,
                age: p.age,
                ovr: p.ovr,
                isSuperstar: Boolean(p.isSuperstar)
              });
            }
            return; // Player retires permanently from active pool
          }
          p.ovr = Math.max(62, (p.ovr || 74) - (Math.random() < (1 - declineMitigation) ? 1 : 0));
          if (Math.random() < 0.40) p.spd = Math.max(5, (p.spd || 7) - 1);
        }

        if (p.contract <= 0 && retained.length >= 16) {
          p.contract = 2;
          p.isStarter = false;
          p.salary = calculateProportionalSalary(p.pos, p.ovr, p.isSuperstar);
          expiredToFA.push(p);
        } else {
          if (p.contract <= 0) {
            p.contract = 1;
            p.salary = calculateProportionalSalary(p.pos, p.ovr, p.isSuperstar);
          }
          retained.push(p);
        }
      });

      return { retained, expiredToFA };
    }

    function runCpuOffseasonFreeAgency() {
      if (!career || !career.leagueRosters || !Array.isArray(career.freeAgents)) return;
      // Age & prune older free agents so the FA pool stays fresh across 30+ seasons
      career.freeAgents = career.freeAgents.filter(fa => {
        fa.age = (fa.age || 26) + 1;
        if (fa.age >= 35 && Math.random() < 0.65) return false;
        if (fa.age >= 31) fa.ovr = Math.max(64, (fa.ovr || 72) - 1);
        fa.salary = calculateProportionalSalary(fa.pos, fa.ovr, fa.isSuperstar);
        return true;
      });
    }

    function beginNextDynastySeason() {
      if (!career) return;
      const completedSeason = career.season || 1;
      // Multi-Decade Dynasty History & Retired Legends
      const champId = career.playoffs?.championId || null;
      const userRec = getTeamRecordObj(career.teamId);

      if (!Array.isArray(career.seasonHistory)) career.seasonHistory = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(career.retiredLegends)) career.retiredLegends = [];

      career.seasonHistory.unshift({
        season: completedSeason,
        championId: champId || 'TBD',
        userRecord: `${userRec.w}-${userRec.l}`,
        capLimit: career.capLimit || BASE_SALARY_CAP
      });
      if (career.seasonHistory.length > 50) career.seasonHistory.length = 50;

      career.season = completedSeason + 1;
      career.week = 1;
      career.phase = 'REGULAR';
      career.seasonDrillsUsed = 0;
      // Rotate the 5 visible Franchise Facility upgrade departments for the new season
      career.activeFacilityKeys = rotateSeasonFacilities(career.season, career.activeFacilityKeys || []);
      // Increase salary cap by exact 8% proportional rate each season
      const prevCap = career.capLimit || BASE_SALARY_CAP;
      career.capLimit = Math.round(prevCap * (1 + ANNUAL_CAP_GROWTH_RATE));
      career.playoffs = null;

      if (!Array.isArray(career.freeAgents)) career.freeAgents = generateInitialFreeAgents(28);
      if (!career.leagueRosters) career.leagueRosters = {};

      // Progress user roster (aging, development, retirements, expiring contracts)
      const userProg = progressSingleRosterOffseason(
        career.roster, career.teamId, career.seasonHistory, career.retiredLegends, true
      );
      career.roster = userProg.retained;
      career.freeAgents.push(...userProg.expiredToFA);
      autoAssignStarters(career.roster);

      // Progress all 31 CPU franchise rosters identically
      TEAMS.forEach(t => {
        if (t.id === career.teamId) return;
        if (!Array.isArray(career.leagueRosters[t.id]) || !career.leagueRosters[t.id].length) {
          career.leagueRosters[t.id] = generateFullNoLinemanRoster(t);
        }
        const cpuProg = progressSingleRosterOffseason(
          career.leagueRosters[t.id], t.id, career.seasonHistory, career.retiredLegends, false
        );
        career.leagueRosters[t.id] = cpuProg.retained;
        career.freeAgents.push(...cpuProg.expiredToFA);
        autoAssignStarters(career.leagueRosters[t.id]);
      });

      if (career.retiredLegends.length > 60) career.retiredLegends.length = 60;

      // CPU teams replenish rosters via Free Agency & refresh proportional market salaries
      runCpuOffseasonFreeAgency();

      career.standings = {};
      TEAMS.forEach(t => {
        career.standings[t.id] = createEmptyStandingRecord();
      });

      const seasonData = generateSeasonSchedule(career.teamId, career.season);
      career.byeWeeks = seasonData.byeWeeks;
      career.weekGames = seasonData.weekGames;
      career.schedule = seasonData.userSchedule;
      career.draftState = null;
      career.draftClass = [];
      saveCareer();
      showToast(`SEASON ${career.season} KICKOFF! CAP +8% TO $${career.capLimit}M • FACILITIES ROTATED`);
      openHub('office');
    }

    function reconcileStandingsAndSchedule() {
      if (!career) return;
      if (!career.standings) career.standings = {};
      TEAMS.forEach(t => {
        if (!career.standings[t.id]) career.standings[t.id] = createEmptyStandingRecord();
      });

      // Normalize weekGames entries so homeId / awayId are always populated
      if (Array.isArray(career.weekGames)) {
        for (let w = 0; w < 18; w++) {
          if (!Array.isArray(career.weekGames[w])) career.weekGames[w] = [];
          career.weekGames[w].forEach(m => {
            if (!m.homeId && m.home) m.homeId = m.home;
            if (!m.awayId && m.away) m.awayId = m.away;
          });
        }
      }

      // Keep user's schedule entries and weekGames entries 100% synchronized by exact week & opponent
      if (Array.isArray(career.schedule) && Array.isArray(career.weekGames)) {
        career.schedule.forEach(ug => {
          const wkIdx = (ug.week || 1) - 1;
          if (!Array.isArray(career.weekGames[wkIdx])) career.weekGames[wkIdx] = [];

          if (ug.isBye) {
            career.weekGames[wkIdx] = career.weekGames[wkIdx].filter(
              m => (m.homeId || m.home) !== career.teamId && (m.awayId || m.away) !== career.teamId
            );
            return;
          }

          let wg = career.weekGames[wkIdx].find(
            m => (m.homeId || m.home) === career.teamId || (m.awayId || m.away) === career.teamId
          );

          if (!wg) {
            wg = {
              week: ug.week,
              homeId: ug.isHome ? career.teamId : ug.oppId,
              awayId: ug.isHome ? ug.oppId : career.teamId,
              played: Boolean(ug.played),
              homeScore: ug.isHome ? (ug.userScore || 0) : (ug.oppScore || 0),
              awayScore: ug.isHome ? (ug.oppScore || 0) : (ug.userScore || 0)
            };
            career.weekGames[wkIdx].push(wg);
          }

          if (ug.played && !wg.played) {
            wg.played = true;
            wg.homeScore = ug.isHome ? ug.userScore : ug.oppScore;
            wg.awayScore = ug.isHome ? ug.oppScore : ug.userScore;
          } else if (wg.played && !ug.played) {
            ug.played = true;
            ug.userScore = ug.isHome ? wg.homeScore : wg.awayScore;
            ug.oppScore = ug.isHome ? wg.awayScore : wg.homeScore;
          }
        });
      }

      // Rebuild standings directly from played weekGames during REGULAR season so record never desyncs
      if (career.phase === 'REGULAR' && Array.isArray(career.weekGames)) {
        const fresh = {};
        TEAMS.forEach(t => { fresh[t.id] = createEmptyStandingRecord(); });
        career.standings = fresh;
        for (let w = 0; w < 18; w++) {
          (career.weekGames[w] || []).forEach(g => {
            if (g.played) {
              applyGameResultToStandings(g.homeId || g.home, g.awayId || g.away, g.homeScore, g.awayScore);
            }
          });
        }
      }
    }

    function openHub(tab = 'office') {
      if (!career) return;
      ensureCareerSeasonIntegrity();
      reconcileStandingsAndSchedule();
      currentHubTab = tab;

      // Sync career.week to the current active unplayed week so header & PLAY WEEK button always match
      if (career.phase === 'REGULAR' && Array.isArray(career.schedule)) {
        const nextUnplayed = career.schedule.find(g => !g.played);
        if (nextUnplayed) {
          career.week = nextUnplayed.week;
        } else {
          career.week = 19;
          checkRegularSeasonComplete();
        }
      }

      const team = getUserTeam(career.teamId);
      const rec = getTeamRecordObj(career.teamId);
      const capStatus = getSoftCapStatus();
      const badge = document.getElementById('hub-team-badge');
      if (badge) {
        badge.textContent = team.id;
        badge.style.background = team.primary;
      }
      document.getElementById('hub-team-name').textContent = `${team.city} ${team.name} (${career.rings || 0} RINGS)`;
      document.getElementById('hub-season-info').textContent =
        `SEASON ${career.season} • ${career.phase === 'REGULAR' ? `WEEK ${Math.min(18, career.week)}` : career.phase} • RECORD: ${rec.w}-${rec.l}`;
      document.getElementById('hub-cc').textContent = career.cc;

      const capEl = document.getElementById('hub-cap');
      const capBadge = document.getElementById('hub-cap-badge');
      if (capEl && capBadge) {
        if (capStatus.overBy > 0) {
          capEl.textContent = `-${capStatus.overBy}`;
          capBadge.className = 'pixel-box px-2.5 py-1.5 text-rose-400';
        } else {
          capEl.textContent = `${capStatus.capSpace}`;
          capBadge.className = 'pixel-box px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sky-300';
        }
      }

      const rosCountEl = document.getElementById('hub-roster-count');
      const rosBadge = document.getElementById('hub-roster-badge');
      if (rosCountEl && rosBadge) {
        rosCountEl.textContent = `${career.roster.length}/${capStatus.maxRoster}`;
        rosBadge.className = career.roster.length > capStatus.maxRoster
          ? 'pixel-box px-2.5 py-1.5 text-rose-400'
          : (capStatus.penaltySpots > 0 ? 'pixel-box px-2.5 py-1.5 text-amber-300' : 'pixel-box px-2.5 py-1.5 text-yellow-300');
      }

      document.querySelectorAll('.hub-tab').forEach(btn => {
        const active = btn.getAttribute('data-hubtab') === tab;
        btn.classList.toggle('btn-gold', active);
        btn.classList.toggle('btn-slate', !active);
      });

      switchScreen('screen-hub');
      const body = document.getElementById('hub-content');
      if (!body) return;

      if (tab === 'office') renderHubOffice(body, team);
      else if (tab === 'roster') renderHubRoster(body, team);
      else if (tab === 'freeagents') renderHubFreeAgents(body, team);
      else if (tab === 'standings') renderHubStandings(body);
      else if (tab === 'draft') renderHubDraft(body);
    }

    function renderHubRoster(body, team) {
      const capStatus = getSoftCapStatus();
      const fmtOvr = (v) => (v > 12 ? `${v} OVR` : `${v}/10`);
      const posOrder = { QB: 1, RB: 2, WR: 3, TE: 4, LB: 5, CB: 6, S: 7 };
      const drillsUsed = career.seasonDrillsUsed || 0;
      const maxDrills = getSeasonalDrillLimit();
      const drillsLeft = Math.max(0, maxDrills - drillsUsed);
      const nextDrillCost = 10 + drillsUsed * 4;

      const counts = { QB: 0, RB: 0, WR: 0, TE: 0, LB: 0, CB: 0, S: 0 };
      career.roster.forEach(p => {
        const np = normalizePlayerPosition(p.pos);
        counts[np] = (counts[np] || 0) + 1;
      });

      const filteredRoster = career.roster
        .map((p, origIdx) => ({ p, origIdx }))
        .filter(({ p }) => {
          if (rosterUnitFilter === 'OFF') return ['QB', 'RB', 'WR', 'TE'].includes(p.pos);
          if (rosterUnitFilter === 'DEF') return ['LB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(p.pos);
          return true;
        })
        .sort((a, b) => (posOrder[a.p.pos] || 9) - (posOrder[b.p.pos] || 9) || (b.p.isStarter ? 1 : 0) - (a.p.isStarter ? 1 : 0) || (b.p.ovr || 0) - (a.p.ovr || 0));

      const overLimitWarning = career.roster.length > capStatus.maxRoster
        ? `<div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-rose-950 border-rose-500 text-rose-200 text-[10px]">
             WARNING: ROSTER OVER SOFT-CAP LIMIT (${career.roster.length}/${capStatus.maxRoster})! RELEASE ${career.roster.length - capStatus.maxRoster} PLAYER(S) OR REDUCE PAYROLL TO RECOVER ROSTER SPOTS.
           </div>`
        : '';

      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-4">
          ${overLimitWarning}

          <!-- Roster & Soft Salary Cap Summary Banner -->
          <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900 space-y-3">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <div class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-yellow-400">
                  ${team.city} ${team.name} ROSTER &amp; DEPTH CHART (${career.roster.length}/${capStatus.maxRoster} ACTIVE)
                </div>
                <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
                  WEIGHT ROOM LVL ${getFacilityLevel('training')} &bull; SEASON DRILLS: <span class="${drillsLeft > 0 ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}">${drillsLeft}/${maxDrills} LEFT</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <button id="btn-squad-random-drill" ${drillsLeft <= 0 ? 'disabled' : ''} class="pixel-btn btn-gold px-3.5 py-2 text-[9px] sm:text-[10px]">
                  ${drillsLeft > 0 ? `TEAM DRILL (${nextDrillCost} CC)` : 'SEASON DRILLS MAXED'}
                </button>
                <div class="text-right text-[9px] sm:text-[10px]">
                  <div class="${capStatus.overBy > 0 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}">
                    PAYROLL: $${capStatus.payroll}M / $${capStatus.capLimit}M CAP (${capStatus.tierLabel})
                  </div>
                  <div class="text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    SOFT-CAP RULES: $1–$100M OVER = -1 SPOT (33) &bull; $101M+ OVER = -2 SPOTS (32)
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Position Breakdown & Unit Filters -->
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-slate-800 pt-2.5">
              <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 text-[8px] sm:text-[9px]">
                <span class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-amber-300">QB: ${counts.QB}</span>
                <span class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-amber-300">RB: ${counts.RB}</span>
                <span class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-amber-300">WR: ${counts.WR}</span>
                <span class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-700 text-amber-300">TE: ${counts.TE}</span>
                <span class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-sky-700 text-sky-300">LB: ${counts.LB}</span>
                <span class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-sky-700 text-sky-300">CB: ${counts.CB}</span>
                <span class="px-2 py-1 bg-slate-950 border border-sky-700 text-sky-300">S: ${counts.S}</span>
              </div>

              <div class="flex gap-1.5">
                <button data-ros-filter="ALL" class="pixel-btn ${rosterUnitFilter === 'ALL' ? 'btn-gold' : 'btn-slate'} px-2.5 py-1 text-[8px]">ALL (${career.roster.length})</button>
                <button data-ros-filter="OFF" class="pixel-btn ${rosterUnitFilter === 'OFF' ? 'btn-gold' : 'btn-slate'} px-2.5 py-1 text-[8px]">OFFENSE (${counts.QB + counts.RB + counts.WR + counts.TE})</button>
                <button data-ros-filter="DEF" class="pixel-btn ${rosterUnitFilter === 'DEF' ? 'btn-gold' : 'btn-slate'} px-2.5 py-1 text-[8px]">LB &amp; SECONDARY (${counts.LB + counts.CB + counts.S})</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Depth Chart & Active Roster Grid -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            ${filteredRoster.map(({ p, origIdx }) => {
              const isDef = ['LB', 'CB', 'S'].includes(p.pos);
              const softMax = getPlayerSoftMaxOvr(p);
              const isMaxOvr = p.ovr > 12 ? p.ovr >= softMax : p.ovr >= 10;
              return `
                <div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-slate-900 flex items-center justify-between gap-2.5 ${p.isSuperstar ? 'border-yellow-400' : (p.isStarter ? 'border-emerald-500/70' : '')}">
                  <div class="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <div class="text-xs text-yellow-300 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                      <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[8px] ${p.isStarter ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-black' : 'bg-slate-700 text-slate-200'}">
                        ${p.isStarter ? 'STARTER' : 'BACKUP'}
                      </span>
                      ${p.isSuperstar ? '<span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-yellow-400 text-black text-[8px]">★ SUPERSTAR</span>' : ''}
                      <span class="truncate">${p.name}</span>
                      <span class="${isDef ? 'text-sky-400' : 'text-amber-400'}">(${p.pos})</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-300 mt-1">
                      OVR: <span class="text-emerald-400">${fmtOvr(p.ovr)}</span> <span class="text-slate-400">(CAP ${softMax})</span> &bull; SPD: ${p.spd} &bull; ${p.pos === 'QB' ? `ARM: ${p.arm} &bull; ` : ''}ACC: ${p.acc}
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                      AGE: ${p.age || 25} &bull; CAP HIT: $${p.salary || 3}M/YR &bull; ${p.contract || 2} YRS LEFT
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div class="flex flex-col gap-1 shrink-0">
                    ${!p.isStarter ? `<button data-promote-idx="${origIdx}" class="pixel-btn btn-blue px-2 py-1 text-[8px]">SET STARTER</button>` : ''}
                    ${(p.pos === 'CB' || p.pos === 'S') ? `<button data-swappos-idx="${origIdx}" class="pixel-btn btn-slate px-2 py-1 text-[8px]">MOVE TO ${p.pos === 'CB' ? 'S' : 'CB'}</button>` : ''}
                    ${(p.contract || 2) <= 1 ? `<button data-resign-idx="${origIdx}" class="pixel-btn btn-gold px-2 py-1 text-[8px]">RE-SIGN ($${calculateProportionalSalary(p.pos, p.ovr, p.isSuperstar)}M)</button>` : ''}
                    <button data-train-idx="${origIdx}" ${(isMaxOvr || drillsLeft <= 0) ? 'disabled' : ''} class="pixel-btn btn-green px-2 py-1 text-[8px]">
                      ${isMaxOvr ? 'SEASON CAP' : (drillsLeft <= 0 ? 'NO DRILLS LEFT' : `DRILL (${nextDrillCost} CC)`)}
                    </button>
                    <button data-cut-idx="${origIdx}" ${career.roster.length <= 14 ? 'disabled' : ''} class="pixel-btn btn-red px-2 py-1 text-[8px]">
                      CUT TO FA
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      const squadDrillBtn = document.getElementById('btn-squad-random-drill');
      if (squadDrillBtn) {
        squadDrillBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const res = runBalancedSquadTraining(null);
          if (res === 'SEASON_CAP_REACHED') {
            showToast(`ALL ${maxDrills} TEAM DRILLS USED THIS SEASON!`);
            return;
          }
          if (res === null) {
            showToast(`NEED ${nextDrillCost} COACHING CREDITS FOR TEAM DRILL!`);
            return;
          }
          if (!res.length) {
            showToast('ALL PLAYERS AT CURRENT AGE/SEASON POTENTIAL CAP!');
            return;
          }
          sfx.playCatch();
          saveCareer();
          showToast(`TEAM DRILL: ${res.join(' | ')}`, 3600);
          openHub('roster');
        });
      }

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-ros-filter]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          rosterUnitFilter = btn.getAttribute('data-ros-filter');
          openHub('roster');
        });
      });

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-promote-idx]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-promote-idx'), 10);
          const pl = career.roster[idx];
          if (!pl) return;
          const slotCap = STARTER_SLOTS_BY_POS[pl.pos] || 1;
          const curStarters = career.roster
            .filter(x => x.pos === pl.pos && x.isStarter)
            .sort((a, b) => (a.ovr || 0) - (b.ovr || 0));
          if (curStarters.length >= slotCap && curStarters[0]) {
            curStarters[0].isStarter = false;
          }
          pl.isStarter = true;
          sfx.playClick();
          saveCareer();
          showToast(`${pl.name} PROMOTED TO STARTING ${pl.pos}!`);
          openHub('roster');
        });
      });

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-swappos-idx]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-swappos-idx'), 10);
          const pl = career.roster[idx];
          if (!pl || (pl.pos !== 'CB' && pl.pos !== 'S')) return;
          pl.pos = pl.pos === 'CB' ? 'S' : 'CB';
          pl.isStarter = false;
          autoAssignStarters(career.roster);
          sfx.playClick();
          saveCareer();
          showToast(`${pl.name} MOVED TO ${pl.pos}!`);
          openHub('roster');
        });
      });

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-resign-idx]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-resign-idx'), 10);
          const pl = career.roster[idx];
          if (!pl) return;
          const newSal = calculateProportionalSalary(pl.pos, pl.ovr, pl.isSuperstar);
          pl.salary = newSal;
          pl.contract = 3;
          sfx.playCatch();
          saveCareer();
          showToast(`EXTENDED ${pl.name} FOR 3 YRS AT $${newSal}M/YR!`);
          openHub('roster');
        });
      });

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-train-idx]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-train-idx'), 10);
          const pl = career.roster[idx];
          if (!pl) return;
          const softCap = getPlayerSoftMaxOvr(pl);
          if (pl.ovr >= softCap) {
            showToast(`${pl.name} REACHED CURRENT SEASON POTENTIAL CAP (${softCap})!`);
            return;
          }
          const res = runBalancedSquadTraining(idx);
          if (res === 'SEASON_CAP_REACHED') {
            showToast(`ALL ${maxDrills} TEAM DRILLS USED THIS SEASON!`);
            return;
          }
          if (res === null) {
            showToast(`NEED ${nextDrillCost} COACHING CREDITS TO RUN DRILL!`);
            return;
          }
          sfx.playCatch();
          saveCareer();
          showToast(`RANDOM DRILL RESULTS: ${res.join(' | ')}`, 3600);
          openHub('roster');
        });
      });

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-cut-idx]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-cut-idx'), 10);
          if (career.roster.length <= 14) {
            showToast('MINIMUM 14 PLAYERS REQUIRED!');
            return;
          }
          const removed = career.roster.splice(idx, 1)[0];
          removed.isStarter = false;
          if (!Array.isArray(career.freeAgents)) career.freeAgents = [];
          career.freeAgents.unshift(removed);
          autoAssignStarters(career.roster);
          sfx.playClick();
          saveCareer();
          const newCap = getSoftCapStatus();
          showToast(`CUT ${removed.name} TO FREE AGENCY • ROSTER LIMIT: ${newCap.maxRoster}`);
          openHub('roster');
        });
      });
    }

    function renderHubFreeAgents(body, team) {
      if (!Array.isArray(career.freeAgents)) career.freeAgents = generateInitialFreeAgents(24);
      const capStatus = getSoftCapStatus();
      const canSignMore = career.roster.length < capStatus.maxRoster;

      const filteredFA = career.freeAgents
        .map((p, idx) => ({ p, idx }))
        .filter(({ p }) => faPosFilter === 'ALL' || normalizePlayerPosition(p.pos) === faPosFilter)
        .sort((a, b) => (b.p.ovr || 0) - (a.p.ovr || 0));

      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-4">
          <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900 space-y-2.5">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <div class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-yellow-400">NFL FREE AGENT POOL (${career.freeAgents.length} AVAILABLE)</div>
                <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
                  ALL RELEASED PLAYERS AUTOMATICALLY ENTER FREE AGENCY &bull; SKILL, LB &amp; SECONDARY ONLY
                </div>
              </div>
              <div class="text-right text-[9px] sm:text-[10px]">
                <div class="text-yellow-300">ACTIVE SPOTS: ${career.roster.length}/${capStatus.maxRoster}</div>
                <div class="${capStatus.overBy > 0 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-emerald-400'}">${capStatus.tierLabel}</div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 border-t border-slate-800 pt-2">
              ${['ALL', ...DRAFT_POSITIONS].map(pos => `
                <button data-fa-pos="${pos}" class="pixel-btn ${faPosFilter === pos ? 'btn-gold' : 'btn-slate'} px-2.5 py-1 text-[8px]">${pos}</button>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            ${filteredFA.map(({ p, idx }) => `
              <div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between gap-2 ${p.isSuperstar ? 'border-yellow-400' : ''}">
                <div>
                  <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                    <span class="text-xs text-yellow-300 truncate">${p.isSuperstar ? '★ ' : ''}${p.name}</span>
                    <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-600 text-sky-300 text-[9px]">${normalizePlayerPosition(p.pos)}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-300 mt-1">
                    OVR: <span class="text-emerald-400">${p.ovr}</span> &bull; SPD: ${p.spd} &bull; ACC: ${p.acc} &bull; AGE: ${p.age || 26}
                  </div>
                  <div class="text-[8px] text-amber-300 mt-0.5">ASKING: $${p.salary || 3}M/YR (${p.contract || 2} YRS)</div>
                </div>
                <button data-sign-fa-idx="${idx}" ${!canSignMore ? 'disabled' : ''} class="pixel-btn btn-green w-full py-2 text-[9px]">
                  ${canSignMore ? `SIGN PLAYER ($${p.salary || 3}M/YR)` : `ROSTER FULL (${career.roster.length}/${capStatus.maxRoster})`}
                </button>
              </div>
            `).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-fa-pos]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          faPosFilter = btn.getAttribute('data-fa-pos');
          openHub('freeagents');
        });
