      });

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-sign-fa-idx]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-sign-fa-idx'), 10);
          const freshCap = getSoftCapStatus();
          if (career.roster.length >= freshCap.maxRoster) {
            showToast(`ROSTER FULL (${career.roster.length}/${freshCap.maxRoster})!`);
            return;
          }
          const fa = career.freeAgents.splice(idx, 1)[0];
          if (!fa) return;
          fa.pos = normalizePlayerPosition(fa.pos);
          fa.isStarter = false;
          career.roster.push(fa);
          autoAssignStarters(career.roster);
          sfx.playCatch();
          saveCareer();
          const afterCap = getSoftCapStatus();
          showToast(`SIGNED ${fa.name} (${fa.pos}) • ROSTER: ${career.roster.length}/${afterCap.maxRoster}`);
          openHub('freeagents');
        });
      });
    }

    function renderHubStandings(body) {
      const afcData = computeConferenceSeeds('AFC');
      const nfcData = computeConferenceSeeds('NFC');
      const po = career.playoffs;

      const getStarterForLeader = (teamId, posList) => {
        const rList = (teamId === career.teamId)
          ? career.roster
          : (career.leagueRosters && career.leagueRosters[teamId]);
        if (!Array.isArray(rList) || !rList.length) return { name: `${teamId} STAR`, pos: posList[0] };
        const found = rList.find(p => p.isStarter && posList.includes(p.pos))
          || rList.find(p => posList.includes(p.pos))
          || rList[0];
        return found;
      };

      const renderLeagueLeadersCard = () => {
        const categories = [
          {
            label: 'PASSING',
            posList: ['QB'],
            statFmt: (rec, idx) => {
              const yds = rec.games > 0 ? Math.max(0, rec.pf * 11 + (5 - idx) * 45) : 0;
              const td = rec.games > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.round(rec.pf * 0.085) + Math.max(0, 3 - idx)) : 0;
              const ints = rec.games > 0 ? Math.max(1, Math.round(rec.l * 0.65) + (idx % 3)) : 0;
              return `${yds} YDS • ${td} TD • ${ints} INT`;
            }
          },
          {
            label: 'RECEIVING',
            posList: ['WR', 'TE'],
            statFmt: (rec, idx) => `${rec.games > 0 ? Math.max(0, rec.pf * 4 + (5 - idx) * 18) : 0} YDS`
          },
          {
            label: 'RUSHING',
            posList: ['RB'],
            statFmt: (rec, idx) => `${rec.games > 0 ? Math.max(0, rec.pf * 5 + (5 - idx) * 22) : 0} YDS`
          },
          {
            label: 'INTERCEPTIONS',
            posList: ['CB', 'S'],
            statFmt: (rec, idx) => `${Math.max(0, Math.floor(rec.w * 0.45) + (rec.games > 0 ? Math.max(0, 4 - idx) : 0))} INT`
          },
          {
            label: 'SACKS',
            posList: ['LB'],
            statFmt: (rec, idx) => `${(Math.max(0, rec.w * 1.1 + (rec.games > 0 ? Math.max(0, 5 - idx) * 1.5 : 0))).toFixed(1)} SCK`
          }
        ];

        const topTeams = [...TEAMS].sort((a, b) => compareTeamsNFL(a, b, false));

        return `
          <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900">
            <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 mb-2">LEAGUE LEADERS (SEASON ${career.season})</div>
            <div class="space-y-1.5 text-[9px]">
              ${categories.map((cat, idx) => {
                const leaderTeam = topTeams[idx % topTeams.length] || TEAMS[0];
                const rec = getTeamRecordObj(leaderTeam.id);
                const player = getStarterForLeader(leaderTeam.id, cat.posList);
                const isUser = leaderTeam.id === career.teamId;
                return `
                  <div class="p-1.5 border ${isUser ? 'border-yellow-400 bg-slate-800' : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950'}">
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-sky-300">
                      <span>${cat.label}</span>
                      <span class="text-emerald-300">${cat.statFmt(rec, idx)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-0.5 text-white">
                      <span class="truncate">${player.name} (${player.pos})</span>
                      <span class="text-slate-400 ml-2">${leaderTeam.id}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                `;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      };

      const renderConfSeedCard = (confName, data) => `
        <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900">
          <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 mb-2">${confName} PLAYOFF PICTURE (TOP 6 QUALIFY)</div>
          <div class="space-y-1 text-[9px]">
            ${[...data.seeds, ...data.inTheHunt.slice(0, 4)].map(s => {
              const r = getTeamRecordObj(s.team.id);
              const isUser = s.team.id === career.teamId;
              const inPlayoffs = s.seed <= 6;
              return `
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-1.5 border ${isUser ? 'border-yellow-400 bg-slate-800' : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950'}">
                  <span class="${inPlayoffs ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-slate-400'}">#${s.seed} ${s.team.id} (${s.tag})</span>
                  <span class="text-white">${r.w}-${r.l} (${r.diff >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${r.diff})</span>
                </div>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      const renderDivisions = (confName) => {
        const divs = ['EAST', 'NORTH', 'SOUTH', 'WEST'];
        return `
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
            ${divs.map(div => {
              const teams = TEAMS.filter(t => t.conf === confName && t.div === div)
                .sort((a, b) => compareTeamsNFL(a, b, true));
              return `
                <div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-slate-900">
                  <div class="text-[10px] text-yellow-400 mb-2">${confName} ${div}</div>
                  <div class="space-y-1 text-[9px]">
                    ${teams.map(t => {
                      const r = getTeamRecordObj(t.id);
                      const isUser = t.id === career.teamId;
                      return `
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between ${isUser ? 'text-yellow-300' : 'text-slate-200'}">
                          <span>${t.id}</span>
                          <span>${r.w}-${r.l} (PF:${r.pf} PA:${r.pa})</span>
                        </div>
                      `;
                    }).join('')}
                  </div>
                </div>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </div>
        `;
      };

      let bracketHTML = '';
      if (po) {
        const fmtMatch = (m) => {
          if (!m) return 'TBD';
          if (m.afcId) {
            return `${m.afcId} ${m.played ? m.afcScore : ''} vs ${m.nfcId} ${m.played ? m.nfcScore : ''}`;
          }
          return `#${m.highSeed} ${m.highId} ${m.played ? m.highScore : ''} vs #${m.lowSeed} ${m.lowId} ${m.played ? m.lowScore : ''}`;
        };
        bracketHTML = `
          <div class="pixel-box-gold p-4 bg-slate-900 space-y-3">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400">NFL PLAYOFF BRACKET &bull; ROUND: ${po.round}</div>
              ${career.phase === 'PLAYOFFS' ? `<button id="btn-bracket-sim" class="pixel-btn btn-blue px-3 py-1.5 text-[9px]">SIM PLAYOFF ROUND</button>` : ''}
            </div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-[9px]">
              <div class="bg-slate-950 p-2.5 border border-slate-800 space-y-1">
                <div class="text-sky-400">WILD CARD ROUND</div>
                <div>AFC: ${po.AFC.wildCard.map(fmtMatch).join(' | ')}</div>
                <div>NFC: ${po.NFC.wildCard.map(fmtMatch).join(' | ')}</div>
              </div>
              <div class="bg-slate-950 p-2.5 border border-slate-800 space-y-1">
                <div class="text-emerald-400">DIVISIONAL ROUND</div>
                <div>AFC: ${po.AFC.divisional.length ? po.AFC.divisional.map(fmtMatch).join(' | ') : 'AWAITING WC'}</div>
                <div>NFC: ${po.NFC.divisional.length ? po.NFC.divisional.map(fmtMatch).join(' | ') : 'AWAITING WC'}</div>
              </div>
              <div class="bg-slate-950 p-2.5 border border-slate-800 space-y-1">
                <div class="text-yellow-300">CONF CHAMPS &amp; SUPER BOWL</div>
                <div>AFC CHAMP: ${fmtMatch(po.AFC.championship)}</div>
                <div>NFC CHAMP: ${fmtMatch(po.NFC.championship)}</div>
                <div class="text-amber-400">SUPER BOWL: ${fmtMatch(po.superBowl)}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-4">
          ${bracketHTML}
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            ${renderConfSeedCard('AFC', afcData)}
            ${renderConfSeedCard('NFC', nfcData)}
            ${renderLeagueLeadersCard()}
          </div>
          <div class="space-y-3">
            <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400">AFC DIVISION STANDINGS</div>
            ${renderDivisions('AFC')}
            <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 pt-2">NFC DIVISION STANDINGS</div>
            ${renderDivisions('NFC')}
          </div>

          <!-- Multi-Decade Dynasty History & Retired Legends -->
          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2">
            <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900">
              <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 mb-2">DYNASTY CHAMPIONSHIP HISTORY (${(career.seasonHistory || []).length} SEASONS)</div>
              <div class="space-y-1 text-[9px] max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
                ${!(career.seasonHistory && career.seasonHistory.length)
                  ? '<div class="text-slate-400">COMPLETE SEASON 1 TO LOG CHAMPIONS &amp; RECORDS.</div>'
                  : career.seasonHistory.map(h => `
                      <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-1.5 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                        <span class="text-yellow-300">SEASON ${h.season} &bull; CHAMP: ${h.championId}</span>
                        <span class="text-slate-300">${career.teamId}: ${h.userRecord} &bull; CAP: $${h.capLimit}M</span>
                      </div>
                    `).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900">
              <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 mb-2">RETIRED STARS &amp; FRANCHISE LEGENDS</div>
              <div class="space-y-1 text-[9px] max-h-48 overflow-y-auto">
                ${!(career.retiredLegends && career.retiredLegends.length)
                  ? '<div class="text-slate-400">VETERANS WILL RETIRE HERE OVER MULTI-SEASON DYNASTIES.</div>'
                  : career.retiredLegends.slice(0, 20).map(r => `
                      <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-1.5 bg-slate-950 border border-slate-800">
                        <span class="text-emerald-300">${r.isSuperstar ? '★ ' : ''}${r.name} (${r.pos} • ${r.teamId})</span>
                        <span class="text-slate-400">S${r.season} &bull; AGE ${r.age} (${r.ovr} OVR)</span>
                      </div>
                    `).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      const brSimBtn = document.getElementById('btn-bracket-sim');
      if (brSimBtn) {
        brSimBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          simulatePlayoffRound(false);
          openHub('standings');
        });
      }
    }

    function renderHubDraft(body) {
      const isOffseason = career.phase === 'OFFSEASON';

      // Enforce strict post-Super Bowl access only
      if (!isOffseason) {
        const orderPreview = computeDraftPickOrder();
        const userProjPick = orderPreview.indexOf(career.teamId) + 1;
        body.innerHTML = `
          <div class="max-w-4xl mx-auto space-y-4">
            <div class="pixel-box-gold p-6 bg-slate-900 text-center space-y-3">
              <div class="text-sm sm:text-base text-yellow-400">NFL DRAFT ROOM &bull; CURRENTLY LOCKED</div>
              <p class="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed max-w-2xl mx-auto">
                THE NFL DRAFT TAKES PLACE IMMEDIATELY AFTER THE SUPER BOWL DURING THE OFFSEASON PERIOD.
                COMPLETE THE REGULAR SEASON AND PLAYOFFS TO ENTER THE ${DRAFT_ROUNDS}-ROUND DRAFT!
              </p>
              <div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-slate-950 inline-block text-[10px] text-sky-300">
                PROJECTED ROUND 1 PICK: <span class="text-yellow-300">#${userProjPick} OVERALL</span> (REVERSE STANDINGS ORDER)
              </div>
              <div class="pt-2">
                <button id="btn-back-to-office" class="pixel-btn btn-blue px-5 py-2.5 text-xs">RETURN TO FRONT OFFICE</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
        const backBtn = document.getElementById('btn-back-to-office');
        if (backBtn) backBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { sfx.playClick(); openHub('office'); });
        return;
      }

      if (!career.draftState || !career.draftState.order) {
        initializeOffseasonDraft();
        saveCareer();
      }

      const ds = career.draftState;
      const totalPicks = ds.order.length * ds.rounds;
      const curRound = Math.min(ds.rounds, Math.floor(ds.pickIndex / ds.order.length) + 1);
      const pickInRound = (ds.pickIndex % ds.order.length) + 1;
      const overallPick = ds.pickIndex + 1;
      const isComplete = ds.complete || ds.pickIndex >= totalPicks;
      const onClockTeamId = !isComplete ? ds.order[ds.pickIndex % ds.order.length] : null;
      const isUserOnClock = onClockTeamId === career.teamId;

      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="max-w-6xl mx-auto space-y-4">
          <div class="pixel-box-gold p-4 bg-slate-900 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div>
              <div class="text-xs sm:text-sm text-yellow-400">
                ${isComplete
                  ? `NFL DRAFT COMPLETE (${ds.rounds} ROUNDS)`
                  : `NFL DRAFT &bull; ROUND ${curRound}/${ds.rounds} &bull; PICK #${overallPick} (RD PICK #${pickInRound})`}
              </div>
              <div class="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-emerald-400 mt-1">
                ${isComplete
                  ? 'ALL 32 FRANCHISES HAVE COMPLETED THEIR SELECTIONS!'
                  : (isUserOnClock
                      ? `YOUR FRANCHISE (${career.teamId}) IS ON THE CLOCK! SELECT ANY PROSPECT BELOW.`
                      : `ON THE CLOCK: ${onClockTeamId}`)}
              </div>
              <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-300 mt-0.5">
                SKILL &amp; BACK-7 POSITIONS ONLY (QB, RB, WR, TE, LB, CB, S) &bull; 1-IN-20 (5%) HIDDEN SUPERSTAR BREAKOUT CHANCE!
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              ${!isComplete && isUserOnClock ? `<button id="btn-skip-user-pick" class="pixel-btn btn-slate px-3.5 py-2 text-[10px]">PASS PICK</button>` : ''}
              <button id="btn-next-season" class="pixel-btn btn-green px-4 py-2.5 text-xs">START SEASON ${career.season + 1}</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <!-- Available Prospects Board -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-2 grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 max-h-[62vh] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
              ${(career.draftClass || []).slice(0, 36).map((rk, idx) => {
                const tierColor = rk.ovr >= 82 ? 'text-yellow-300' : (rk.ovr >= 74 ? 'text-sky-300' : 'text-slate-200');
                return `
                  <div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col justify-between gap-2">
                    <div>
                      <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-1">
                        <span class="text-xs ${tierColor}">#${rk.draftRank} ${rk.name}</span>
                        <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 bg-slate-800 border border-slate-600 text-sky-300 text-[9px]">${rk.pos}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-300 mt-1">
                        OVR: <span class="text-emerald-400">${rk.ovr}</span> &bull; SPD: ${rk.spd} &bull; ARM: ${rk.arm} &bull; ACC: ${rk.acc} &bull; AGE: ${rk.age}
                      </div>
                      <div class="text-[8px] text-amber-300 mt-0.5">ROOKIE CONTRACT: $${rk.salary}M/YR (4 YRS)</div>
                    </div>
                    <button data-draft-idx="${idx}" ${(!isUserOnClock || isComplete) ? 'disabled' : ''} class="pixel-btn btn-gold w-full py-2 text-[9px]">
                      ${isComplete ? 'DRAFT ENDED' : (isUserOnClock ? 'DRAFT PLAYER' : 'WAITING FOR PICK')}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                `;
              }).join('')}
            </div>

            <!-- Live Draft Ticker / Pick History -->
            <div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col max-h-[62vh]">
              <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 border-b-2 border-slate-700 pb-2 mb-2">RECENT DRAFT PICKS</div>
              <div class="space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto flex-1 text-[9px]">
                ${ds.history.length === 0
                  ? '<div class="text-slate-400">NO PICKS MADE YET.</div>'
                  : ds.history.slice(0, 40).map(h => `
                    <div class="p-2 border ${h.teamId === career.teamId ? 'border-yellow-400 bg-slate-800' : 'border-slate-800 bg-slate-950'}">
                      <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-slate-400">
                        <span>RD ${h.round} PK #${h.pickOverall} &bull; ${h.teamId}</span>
                        ${h.isSuperstar ? '<span class="text-yellow-300">★ SUPERSTAR!</span>' : ''}
                      </div>
                      <div class="text-white mt-0.5">${h.pos} ${h.playerName} (${h.ovr} OVR)</div>
                    </div>
                  `).join('')}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      const nextSeasonBtn = document.getElementById('btn-next-season');
      if (nextSeasonBtn) {
        nextSeasonBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          beginNextDynastySeason();
        });
      }

      const skipPickBtn = document.getElementById('btn-skip-user-pick');
      if (skipPickBtn) {
        skipPickBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          ds.history.unshift({
            round: curRound,
            pickOverall: overallPick,
            teamId: career.teamId,
            playerName: 'PASSED PICK',
            pos: '--',
            ovr: '--',
            isSuperstar: false
          });
          ds.pickIndex += 1;
          advanceCpuDraftPicks();
          saveCareer();
          openHub('draft');
        });
      }

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-draft-idx]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (!isUserOnClock || isComplete) return;
          const idx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-draft-idx'), 10);
          const rookie = career.draftClass[idx];
          if (!rookie) return;

          const draftCap = getSoftCapStatus();
          if (career.roster.length >= draftCap.maxRoster) {
            // Auto-cut the lowest-overall player on the user's roster to Free Agency to make room for the draft pick
            const autoCut = autoTrimRosterToTarget(career.roster, draftCap.maxRoster - 1);
            if (autoCut.length > 0) {
              if (!Array.isArray(career.freeAgents)) career.freeAgents = [];
              autoCut.forEach(p => career.freeAgents.unshift(p));
              showToast(`ROSTER FULL: AUTO-CUT ${autoCut[0].name} (${autoCut[0].ovr} OVR) TO FA FOR DRAFT PICK!`, 3400);
            } else {
              showToast(`ROSTER FULL (${career.roster.length}/${draftCap.maxRoster})! CUT A PLAYER OR PASS PICK.`);
              return;
            }
          }

          career.draftClass.splice(idx, 1);
          const isBreakout = applySuperstarRollOnDraft(rookie);
          // Rookie deal stays locked to slotted rookie scale (never inflated beyond proportional cap)
          rookie.salary = Math.min(rookie.salary, calculateProportionalSalary(rookie.pos, rookie.ovr, false));
          rookie.isStarter = false;
          career.roster.push(rookie);
          autoAssignStarters(career.roster);

          ds.history.unshift({
            round: curRound,
            pickOverall: overallPick,
            teamId: career.teamId,
            playerName: rookie.name,
            pos: rookie.pos,
            ovr: rookie.ovr,
            isSuperstar: isBreakout
          });

          ds.pickIndex += 1;
          advanceCpuDraftPicks();
          saveCareer();

          if (isBreakout) {
            sfx.playTouchdown();
            showToast(`★ SUPERSTAR BREAKOUT!! ${rookie.name} (${rookie.pos}) SURGED TO ${rookie.ovr} OVR!`, 3600);
          } else {
            sfx.playCatch();
            showToast(`DRAFTED ${rookie.name} (${rookie.pos} • ${rookie.ovr} OVR)!`);
          }
          openHub('draft');
        });
      });
    }

    /* =========================================================
       5. FRANCHISE SAVE / LOAD & ROSTER MANAGEMENT
       ========================================================= */
    let inMemoryCareerSave = null;

    async function saveCareer(showConfirmToast = false) {
      if (!career) return false;
      try {
        const snapshot = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(career));
        inMemoryCareerSave = snapshot;
        if (window.__cloudSaveBridge && typeof window.__cloudSaveBridge.saveCareerCloud === 'function') {
          const ok = await window.__cloudSaveBridge.saveCareerCloud(snapshot);
          if (showConfirmToast) {
            if (ok) {
              const t = getUserTeam(career.teamId);
              showToast(`DYNASTY SAVED TO CLOUD! (${t.id} • S${career.season} WK ${Math.min(18, career.week)})`);
            } else {
              showToast('DYNASTY SAVED TO CURRENT SESSION!');
            }
          }
          return ok;
        }
        if (showConfirmToast) showToast('DYNASTY SAVED!');
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Save failed:', e);
        if (showConfirmToast) showToast('SAVE FAILED — PLEASE TRY AGAIN');
        return false;
      }
    }

    async function loadCareerAsync() {
      try {
        if (window.__cloudSaveBridge && typeof window.__cloudSaveBridge.loadCareerCloud === 'function') {
          const cloudData = await window.__cloudSaveBridge.loadCareerCloud();
          if (cloudData && cloudData.teamId) {
            inMemoryCareerSave = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(cloudData));
            return inMemoryCareerSave;
          }
        }
        if (window.__cloudSaveBridge && window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave && window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave.teamId) {
          return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave));
        }
        return inMemoryCareerSave ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(inMemoryCareerSave)) : null;
      } catch (e) {
        return inMemoryCareerSave ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(inMemoryCareerSave)) : null;
      }
    }

    function loadCareer() {
      if (window.__cloudSaveBridge && window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave && window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave.teamId) {
        return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(window.__cloudSaveBridge.cachedSave));
      }
      return inMemoryCareerSave ? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(inMemoryCareerSave)) : null;
    }

    function getUserTeam(id) {
      return TEAMS.find(t => t.id === id) || TEAMS[0];
    }

    function createEmptyStandingRecord() {
      return { w: 0, l: 0, pf: 0, pa: 0, divW: 0, divL: 0, confW: 0, confL: 0 };
    }

    function generateSeasonSchedule(userTeamId, seasonNum = 1) {
    // 1. Group the 32 teams into the 8 canonical divisions (4 teams each)
    const divKeys = [
        'AFC EAST', 'AFC NORTH', 'AFC SOUTH', 'AFC WEST',
        'NFC EAST', 'NFC NORTH', 'NFC SOUTH', 'NFC WEST'
    ];
    const divs = {};
    divKeys.forEach((dk, dIdx) => {
        const list = TEAMS.filter(t => `${t.conf} ${t.div}` === dk).map(t => t.id);
        // Rotate team slots each season for fresh opponent ordering
        const shift = (seasonNum + dIdx) % 4;
        divs[dk] = list.map((_, i) => list[(i + shift) % 4]);
    });

    // Helper: 6-round double round-robin inside a 4-team division (2 games per round)
    function intraDivRounds(d) {
        return [
            [{ home: d[0], away: d[1] }, { home: d[2], away: d[3] }],
            [{ home: d[0], away: d[2] }, { home: d[1], away: d[3] }],
            [{ home: d[0], away: d[3] }, { home: d[1], away: d[2] }],
            [{ home: d[1], away: d[0] }, { home: d[3], away: d[2] }],
            [{ home: d[2], away: d[0] }, { home: d[3], away: d[1] }],
            [{ home: d[3], away: d[0] }, { home: d[2], away: d[1] }]
        ];
    }

    // Helper: 4-round complete bipartite matching between two 4-team divisions (4 games per round)
    function crossDivRounds(a, b, swapHome = false) {
        const rounds = [];
        for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
            const games = [];
            for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
                const tA = a[i];
                const tB = b[(i + r) % 4];
                const isAHome = ((i + r + seasonNum) % 2 === 0) !== swapHome;
                games.push(isAHome ? { home: tA, away: tB } : { home: tB, away: tA });
            }
            rounds.push(games);
        }
        return rounds;
    }

    // Build 17 exact rounds where all 32 teams play (16 games per round = 544 team-games = 32 * 17)
    const rounds = Array.from({ length: 17 }, () => []);

    // Rounds 0..5 (Weeks 1..6): Intra-division home & away (6 games per team)
    divKeys.forEach(dk => {
        const rList = intraDivRounds(divs[dk]);
        for (let r = 0; r < 6; r++) {
            rounds[r].push(...rList[r]);
        }
    });

    // Rounds 6..9 (Weeks 7..10): Intra-conference cross-division pair 1 (4 games per team)
    const pair1 = [
        ['AFC EAST', 'AFC NORTH'],
        ['AFC SOUTH', 'AFC WEST'],
        ['NFC EAST', 'NFC NORTH'],
        ['NFC SOUTH', 'NFC WEST']
    ];
    pair1.forEach(([d1, d2], idx) => {
        const rList = crossDivRounds(divs[d1], divs[d2], idx % 2 === 1);
        for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
            rounds[6 + r].push(...rList[r]);
        }
    });

    // Rounds 10..13 (Weeks 11..14): Inter-conference cross-division pair 2 (4 games per team)
    const pair2 = [
        ['AFC EAST', 'NFC EAST'],
        ['AFC NORTH', 'NFC NORTH'],
        ['AFC SOUTH', 'NFC SOUTH'],
        ['AFC WEST', 'NFC WEST']
    ];
    pair2.forEach(([d1, d2], idx) => {
        const rList = crossDivRounds(divs[d1], divs[d2], (idx + 1) % 2 === 1);
        for (let r = 0; r < 4; r++) {
            rounds[10 + r].push(...rList[r]);
        }
    });

    // Rounds 14..16 (Weeks 15..17): 3-game intra-conference cross-division pair 3 (3 games per team)
    const pair3 = [
        ['AFC EAST', 'AFC SOUTH'],
        ['AFC NORTH', 'AFC WEST'],
        ['NFC EAST', 'NFC SOUTH'],
        ['NFC NORTH', 'NFC WEST']
    ];
    pair3.forEach(([d1, d2], idx) => {
        const rList = crossDivRounds(divs[d1], divs[d2], idx % 2 === 0);
        for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) {
            rounds[14 + r].push(...rList[r]);
        }
    });

    // Place Rounds 0..16 into Weeks 1..17, and initialize Week 18 as empty
    const weekGames = Array.from({ length: 18 }, (_, w) =>
        w < 17 ? rounds[w].map(g => ({ homeId: g.home, awayId: g.away, played: false, homeScore: 0, awayScore: 0 })) : []
    );

    // Assign every single one of the 32 teams strictly 1 bye week across Weeks 7..14 (indices 6..13)
    const byeWeeks = {};
    const assignedBye = new Set();

    for (let w = 6; w <= 13; w++) {
        let movedCount = 0;
        const remainingInWeek = [];
        for (const g of weekGames[w]) {
            if (movedCount < 2 && !assignedBye.has(g.homeId) && !assignedBye.has(g.awayId)) {
                assignedBye.add(g.homeId);
                assignedBye.add(g.awayId);
                byeWeeks[g.homeId] = w + 1;
                byeWeeks[g.awayId] = w + 1;
                weekGames[17].push(g);
                movedCount++;
            } else {
                remainingInWeek.push(g);
            }
        }
        weekGames[w] = remainingInWeek;
    }

    // Build the user's 18-week schedule view directly from weekGames
    const schedule = [];
    for (let w = 0; w < 18; w++) {
        const weekNum = w + 1;
        const g = weekGames[w].find(x => x.homeId === userTeamId || x.awayId === userTeamId);
        if (!g) {
            schedule.push({
                week: weekNum,
                oppId: null,
                isHome: true,
                isBye: true,
                played: false,
                result: null
            });
        } else {
            const isHome = g.homeId === userTeamId;
            const oppId = isHome ? g.awayId : g.homeId;
            schedule.push({
                week: weekNum,
                oppId,
                isHome,
                isBye: false,
                played: false,
                result: null
            });
        }
    }

    return { schedule, weekGames, byeWeeks };
}

function rebuildStandingsFromWeekGames(career) {
    const standings = {};
    TEAMS.forEach(t => {
        standings[t.id] = createEmptyStandingRecord();
    });
    if (!Array.isArray(career.weekGames)) return standings;

    for (let w = 0; w < career.weekGames.length; w++) {
        for (const g of career.weekGames[w]) {
            if (!g || !g.played) continue;
            const homeId = g.home || g.homeId;
            const awayId = g.away || g.awayId;
            const homeTeam = TEAMS.find(t => t.id === homeId);
            const awayTeam = TEAMS.find(t => t.id === awayId);
            const isConf = homeTeam && awayTeam && homeTeam.conf === awayTeam.conf;
            const isDiv = isConf && homeTeam.div === awayTeam.div;

            if (standings[homeId]) {
                standings[homeId].pf += g.homeScore || 0;
                standings[homeId].pa += g.awayScore || 0;
                if ((g.homeScore || 0) >= (g.awayScore || 0)) {
                    standings[homeId].w++;
                    if (isConf) standings[homeId].confW++;
                    if (isDiv) standings[homeId].divW++;
                } else {
                    standings[homeId].l++;
                    if (isConf) standings[homeId].confL++;
                    if (isDiv) standings[homeId].divL++;
                }
            }
            if (standings[awayId]) {
                standings[awayId].pf += g.awayScore || 0;
                standings[awayId].pa += g.homeScore || 0;
                if ((g.awayScore || 0) > (g.homeScore || 0)) {
                    standings[awayId].w++;
                    if (isConf) standings[awayId].confW++;
                    if (isDiv) standings[awayId].divW++;
                } else {
                    standings[awayId].l++;
                    if (isConf) standings[awayId].confL++;
                    if (isDiv) standings[awayId].divL++;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return standings;
}

function ensureCareerSeasonIntegrity() {
    if (!career || !career.teamId) return;

    // Validate that schedule has 18 weeks and exactly 17 games + 1 bye (repair any corrupted schedule)
    const byeCount = Array.isArray(career.schedule) ? career.schedule.filter(g => g.isBye).length : -1;
    const gameCount = Array.isArray(career.schedule) ? career.schedule.filter(g => !g.isBye && g.oppId).length : -1;
    const hasValidSchedule = Array.isArray(career.schedule) && career.schedule.length === 18 && byeCount === 1 && gameCount === 17;
    const hasValidWeekGames = Array.isArray(career.weekGames) && career.weekGames.length === 18 && career.weekGames[0]?.length === 16;

    if (!hasValidSchedule || !hasValidWeekGames) {
        const gen = generateSeasonSchedule(career.teamId, career.season || 1);
        career.schedule = gen.schedule;
        career.weekGames = gen.weekGames;
        career.byeWeeks = gen.byeWeeks;
        career.week = 1;
        saveCareer();
    }

    // Keep user schedule entries synced with weekGames
    for (let w = 0; w < 18; w++) {
        const entry = career.schedule[w];
        const wg = career.weekGames[w].find(g => (g.home || g.homeId) === career.teamId || (g.away || g.awayId) === career.teamId);
        if (entry && wg && wg.played && !entry.played) {
            entry.played = true;
            const homeId = wg.home || wg.homeId;
            const userScore = homeId === career.teamId ? wg.homeScore : wg.awayScore;
            const oppScore = homeId === career.teamId ? wg.awayScore : wg.homeScore;
            entry.userScore = userScore;
            entry.oppScore = oppScore;
            entry.result = `${userScore >= oppScore ? 'W' : 'L'} ${userScore}-${oppScore}`;
        }
    }

    // Recompute standings directly from all played games so W-L records always stay in sync
    career.standings = rebuildStandingsFromWeekGames(career);

    // Advance career.week to the first unplayed week (or 19 when regular season finishes)
    const nextUnplayed = career.schedule.find(g => !g.played);
    career.week = nextUnplayed ? nextUnplayed.week : 19;
}

    function createNewCareer(teamId) {
      const team = getUserTeam(teamId);
      const standings = {};
      TEAMS.forEach(t => {
        standings[t.id] = createEmptyStandingRecord();
      });

      const roster = generateFullNoLinemanRoster(team);
      const leagueRosters = {};
      TEAMS.forEach(t => {
        if (t.id !== teamId) {
          leagueRosters[t.id] = generateFullNoLinemanRoster(t);
        }
      });

      const initialFacs = {};
      FACILITY_POOL.forEach(f => { initialFacs[f.key] = 1; });

      const seasonData = generateSeasonSchedule(teamId, 1);
      career = {
        teamId,
        season: 1,
        week: 1,
        phase: 'REGULAR',
        cc: 12,
        seasonDrillsUsed: 0,
        rings: 0,
        capLimit: 175,
        facilities: initialFacs,
        activeFacilityKeys: rotateSeasonFacilities(1, []),
        standings,
        byeWeeks: seasonData.byeWeeks,
        weekGames: seasonData.weekGames,
        schedule: seasonData.schedule,
        playoffs: null,
        roster,
        leagueRosters,
        seasonHistory: [],
        retiredLegends: [],
        freeAgents: generateInitialFreeAgents(28),
        draftClass: generateDraftClass()
      };
      saveCareer();
      return career;
    }

    function getTeamRecordObj(teamId) {
      const r = (career && career.standings && career.standings[teamId])
        ? career.standings[teamId]
        : createEmptyStandingRecord();
      const games = (r.w || 0) + (r.l || 0);
      const pct = games > 0 ? (r.w || 0) / games : 0;
      const diff = (r.pf || 0) - (r.pa || 0);
      return { ...r, games, pct, diff };
    }

    function compareTeamsNFL(teamA, teamB, isDivisionTie = false) {
      const a = getTeamRecordObj(teamA.id);
      const b = getTeamRecordObj(teamB.id);
      if (Math.abs(b.pct - a.pct) > 0.0001) return b.pct - a.pct;
      if (b.w !== a.w) return b.w - a.w;
      if (isDivisionTie) {
        const aDivG = (a.divW || 0) + (a.divL || 0);
        const bDivG = (b.divW || 0) + (b.divL || 0);
        const aDivPct = aDivG > 0 ? (a.divW || 0) / aDivG : 0;
        const bDivPct = bDivG > 0 ? (b.divW || 0) / bDivG : 0;
        if (Math.abs(bDivPct - aDivPct) > 0.0001) return bDivPct - aDivPct;
      }
      const aConfG = (a.confW || 0) + (a.confL || 0);
      const bConfG = (b.confW || 0) + (b.confL || 0);
      const aConfPct = aConfG > 0 ? (a.confW || 0) / aConfG : 0;
      const bConfPct = bConfG > 0 ? (b.confW || 0) / bConfG : 0;
      if (Math.abs(bConfPct - aConfPct) > 0.0001) return bConfPct - aConfPct;
      if (b.diff !== a.diff) return b.diff - a.diff;
      return (b.pf || 0) - (a.pf || 0);
    }

    function computeConferenceSeeds(confName) {
      const divs = ['EAST', 'NORTH', 'SOUTH', 'WEST'];
      const divWinners = [];
      const nonWinners = [];

      divs.forEach(div => {
        const divTeams = TEAMS.filter(t => t.conf === confName && t.div === div)
          .sort((a, b) => compareTeamsNFL(a, b, true));
        if (divTeams.length) {
          divWinners.push(divTeams[0]);
          nonWinners.push(...divTeams.slice(1));
        }
      });

      divWinners.sort((a, b) => compareTeamsNFL(a, b, false));
      nonWinners.sort((a, b) => compareTeamsNFL(a, b, false));

      const wildCards = nonWinners.slice(0, 2);
      const inTheHunt = nonWinners.slice(2).map((t, idx) => ({
        seed: 7 + idx,
        team: t,
        tag: 'IN THE HUNT',
        hasBye: false
      }));

      const seeds = [
        ...divWinners.map((t, idx) => ({
          seed: idx + 1,
          team: t,
          tag: idx < 2 ? `${t.div} CHAMPS • 1ST RD BYE` : `${t.div} CHAMPS`,
          hasBye: idx < 2
        })),
        ...wildCards.map((t, idx) => ({
          seed: 5 + idx,
          team: t,
          tag: 'WILD CARD',
          hasBye: false
        }))
      ];

      return { seeds, inTheHunt };
    }

    function buildPlayoffBracket() {
      const makeConfBracket = (confName) => {
        const { seeds } = computeConferenceSeeds(confName);
        const s1 = seeds[0], s2 = seeds[1], s3 = seeds[2], s4 = seeds[3], s5 = seeds[4], s6 = seeds[5];
        return {
          seeds: seeds.map(s => ({ seed: s.seed, teamId: s.team.id, hasBye: s.hasBye, tag: s.tag })),
          wildCard: [
            { id: 'WC1', highSeed: 3, highId: s3.team.id, lowSeed: 6, lowId: s6.team.id, played: false, highScore: 0, lowScore: 0, winnerId: null },
            { id: 'WC2', highSeed: 4, highId: s4.team.id, lowSeed: 5, lowId: s5.team.id, played: false, highScore: 0, lowScore: 0, winnerId: null }
          ],
          divisional: [],
          championship: null
        };
      };

      return {
        round: 'WILDCARD',
        AFC: makeConfBracket('AFC'),
        NFC: makeConfBracket('NFC'),
        superBowl: null,
        championId: null
      };
    }

    function simulateEntireRegularSeason() {
      if (!career || career.phase !== 'REGULAR') return;
      for (let w = 1; w <= 18; w++) {
        simulateLeagueWeek(w, false);
        const uGame = career.schedule.find(g => g.week === w);
        if (uGame && !uGame.played) {
          uGame.played = true;
          if (!uGame.isBye) {
            const lg = career.weekGames?.[w - 1]?.find(
              m => (m.homeId === career.teamId && m.awayId === uGame.oppId) || (m.awayId === career.teamId && m.homeId === uGame.oppId)
            );
            if (lg) {
              uGame.userScore = lg.homeId === career.teamId ? lg.homeScore : lg.awayScore;
              uGame.oppScore = lg.homeId === career.teamId ? lg.awayScore : lg.homeScore;
            }
          }
        }
      }
      career.week = 19;
      checkRegularSeasonComplete();
      saveCareer();
    }

    function renderHubOffice(body, team) {
      const ratings = getTeamRatings(team.id);
      const capStatus = getSoftCapStatus();
      if (career.phase === 'REGULAR' && Array.isArray(career.schedule)) {
        const unplayed = career.schedule.find(g => !g.played);
        if (unplayed) career.week = unplayed.week;
      }
      const nextRegGame = career.phase === 'REGULAR'
        ? (career.schedule.find(g => g.week === career.week && !g.played) || career.schedule.find(g => !g.played))
        : null;
      const poMatch = career.phase === 'PLAYOFFS' ? getUserActivePlayoffMatchup() : null;
      const userHasBye = hasUserFirstRoundBye();

      let actionCardHTML = '';
      if (career.phase === 'REGULAR' && nextRegGame) {
        if (nextRegGame.isBye) {
          actionCardHTML = `
            <div class="pixel-box-gold p-4 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400">WEEK ${nextRegGame.week} &bull; SCHEDULED BYE WEEK</div>
                <div class="text-sm text-emerald-400 mt-1">REST &amp; RECOVERY WEEK (+1 CC BONUS)</div>
                <div class="text-[9px] text-slate-300 mt-1">ADVANCE WEEK TO SIMULATE LEAGUE MATCHUPS</div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <button id="btn-advance-bye" class="pixel-btn btn-green px-4 py-3 text-xs">ADVANCE BYE WEEK</button>
                <button id="btn-sim-season" class="pixel-btn btn-slate px-3 py-3 text-[10px]">SIM SEASON</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        } else {
          const opp = getUserTeam(nextRegGame.oppId);
          actionCardHTML = `
            <div class="pixel-box-gold p-4 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400">WEEK ${nextRegGame.week} OF 18 &bull; ${nextRegGame.isHome ? 'HOME' : 'AWAY'} MATCHUP</div>
                <div class="text-sm sm:text-base text-white mt-1">${nextRegGame.isHome ? 'VS' : '@'} ${opp.city} ${opp.name}</div>
                <div class="text-[9px] text-emerald-400 mt-1">OPP OFF: ${'★'.repeat(opp.off)} &bull; OPP DEF: ${'★'.repeat(opp.def)}</div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2.5">
                <button id="btn-play-week" class="pixel-btn btn-green px-5 py-3 text-xs">PLAY WEEK ${nextRegGame.week}</button>
                <button id="btn-sim-week" class="pixel-btn btn-blue px-4 py-3 text-xs">SIM GAME</button>
                <button id="btn-sim-season" class="pixel-btn btn-slate px-3 py-3 text-[10px]">SIM SEASON</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }
      } else if (career.phase === 'PLAYOFFS') {
        if (poMatch) {
          const opp = getUserTeam(poMatch.oppId);
          actionCardHTML = `
            <div class="pixel-box-gold p-4 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400">NFL POSTSEASON &bull; ${poMatch.roundLabel}</div>
                <div class="text-sm sm:text-base text-white mt-1">VS ${opp.city} ${opp.name}</div>
                <div class="text-[9px] text-emerald-400 mt-1">WIN OR GO HOME!</div>
              </div>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5">
                <button id="btn-play-po" class="pixel-btn btn-green px-5 py-3 text-xs">PLAY ${poMatch.roundLabel}</button>
                <button id="btn-sim-po" class="pixel-btn btn-blue px-4 py-3 text-xs">SIM ROUND</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        } else {
          actionCardHTML = `
            <div class="pixel-box-gold p-4 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400">NFL PLAYOFFS &bull; ${career.playoffs?.round || ''}</div>
                <div class="text-sm text-sky-300 mt-1">
                  ${userHasBye ? 'YOU EARNED A #1/#2 FIRST-ROUND BYE! SIMULATE WILD CARD ROUND.' : 'SIMULATE REMAINING PLAYOFF ROUND TO REACH OFFSEASON.'}
                </div>
              </div>
              <button id="btn-sim-po" class="pixel-btn btn-gold px-5 py-3 text-xs">SIM PLAYOFF ROUND</button>
            </div>
          `;
        }
      } else {
        const champ = career.playoffs?.championId ? getUserTeam(career.playoffs.championId) : null;
        actionCardHTML = `
          <div class="pixel-box-gold p-4 bg-slate-900 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <div>
              <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400">SEASON ${career.season} COMPLETE!</div>
              <div class="text-sm text-emerald-400 mt-1">${champ ? `SUPER BOWL CHAMPIONS: ${champ.city} ${champ.name}` : 'OFFSEASON DRAFT OPEN'}</div>
            </div>
            <button id="btn-go-draft" class="pixel-btn btn-gold px-5 py-3 text-xs">OPEN ROOKIE DRAFT</button>
          </div>
        `;
      }

      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="w-full max-w-[1680px] mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-12 gap-4 items-start">
          <!-- Left Column: Matchup, Uniform, Overview & Facility Upgrades -->
          <div class="xl:col-span-7 space-y-4">
            ${actionCardHTML}

            <!-- Uniform Selector Bar -->
            <div class="pixel-box p-3 bg-slate-900 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-4 text-center">
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span class="text-[10px] sm:text-xs text-yellow-400">GAME UNIFORM:</span>
                <span class="text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] ${userUniformChoice ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400'}">
                  ${userUniformChoice === 'HOME' ? 'HOME COLORED' : (userUniformChoice === 'AWAY' ? 'AWAY WHITE' : 'SELECT BEFORE KICKOFF')}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <button data-hub-uni="HOME" class="pixel-btn ${userUniformChoice === 'HOME' ? 'btn-gold' : 'btn-slate'} px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[10px]">HOME (COLORED)</button>
                <button data-hub-uni="AWAY" class="pixel-btn ${userUniformChoice === 'AWAY' ? 'btn-gold' : 'btn-slate'} px-3.5 py-1.5 text-[10px]">AWAY (WHITE)</button>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Team Ratings & Facilities -->
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
              <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900">
                <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 mb-2">FRANCHISE OVERVIEW</div>
                <div class="space-y-1.5 text-[10px] text-slate-200">
                  <div>OFFENSE RATING: <span class="text-emerald-400">${'★'.repeat(ratings.off)}</span></div>
                  <div>DEFENSE RATING: <span class="text-sky-400">${'★'.repeat(ratings.def)}</span></div>
                  <div>ACTIVE ROSTER: <span class="${career.roster.length > capStatus.maxRoster ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-yellow-300'}">${career.roster.length}/${capStatus.maxRoster} PLAYERS</span></div>
                  <div>PAYROLL / CAP: <span class="${capStatus.overBy > 0 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-emerald-300'}">$${capStatus.payroll}M / $${capStatus.capLimit}M</span></div>
                  <div class="text-[8px] ${capStatus.overBy > 0 ? 'text-amber-300' : 'text-slate-400'}">${capStatus.tierLabel}</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div class="pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                  <span class="text-xs text-yellow-400">SEASON ${career.season} FACILITY UPGRADES</span>
                  <span class="text-[8px] text-emerald-400">ROTATES ANNUALLY • PERKS PERMANENT</span>
                </div>
                <div class="space-y-2 text-[9px]">
                  ${getActiveSeasonFacilities().map(fac => {
                    const lvl = getFacilityLevel(fac.key);
                    const cost = fac.baseCost + (lvl - 1) * 10;
                    const maxed = lvl >= fac.maxLvl;
                    return `
                      <div class="bg-slate-950 p-2 border border-slate-800 space-y-1">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <span class="uppercase text-yellow-300">${fac.name} (LVL ${lvl}/${fac.maxLvl})</span>
                          <button data-upgrade-fac="${fac.key}" ${maxed ? 'disabled' : ''} class="pixel-btn btn-blue px-2.5 py-1 text-[8px] shrink-0">
                            ${maxed ? 'MAXED' : `UPGRADE (${cost} CC)`}
                          </button>
                        </div>
                        <div class="text-[8px] text-sky-300">${fac.bonusDesc(lvl)}</div>
                      </div>
                    `;
                  }).join('')}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Right Column: 18-Week Regular Season Schedule -->
          <div class="xl:col-span-5 pixel-box p-4 bg-slate-900">
            <div class="text-xs text-yellow-400 mb-3">17-GAME REGULAR SEASON SCHEDULE (18 WEEKS)</div>
            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 text-[8px]">
              ${career.schedule.map(g => {
                if (g.isBye) {
                  return `
                    <div class="p-2.5 border ${g.played ? 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-950/60 text-slate-400' : 'border-yellow-500/60 bg-slate-950 text-yellow-300'}">
                      <div>WK ${g.week}: BYE</div>
                      <div class="mt-0.5">${g.played ? 'COMPLETED' : 'OFF WEEK'}</div>
                    </div>
                  `;
                }
                const resStr = g.played
                  ? (g.userScore >= g.oppScore ? `W ${g.userScore}-${g.oppScore}` : `L ${g.userScore}-${g.oppScore}`)
                  : 'UPCOMING';
                return `
                  <div class="p-2.5 border ${g.played ? 'border-slate-700 bg-slate-950/60' : 'border-sky-500/50 bg-slate-950'}">
                    <div class="text-slate-300">WK ${g.week} ${g.isHome ? 'VS' : '@'} ${g.oppId}</div>
                    <div class="mt-0.5 ${g.played ? (g.userScore >= g.oppScore ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400') : 'text-sky-300'}">${resStr}</div>
                  </div>
                `;
              }).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-hub-uni]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          setUserUniformChoice(btn.getAttribute('data-hub-uni'));
        });
      });

      body.querySelectorAll('[data-upgrade-fac]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const facKey = btn.getAttribute('data-upgrade-fac');
          const facMeta = FACILITY_POOL.find(f => f.key === facKey);
          if (!facMeta) return;
          const lvl = getFacilityLevel(facKey);
          const cost = facMeta.baseCost + (lvl - 1) * 10;
          if (lvl >= facMeta.maxLvl) return;
          if (career.cc < cost) {
            showToast(`NEED ${cost} COACHING CREDITS!`);
            return;
          }
          sfx.playCatch();
          career.cc -= cost;
          career.facilities[facKey] = lvl + 1;
          saveCareer();
          showToast(`${facMeta.name} UPGRADED TO LVL ${lvl + 1}!`);
          openHub('office');
        });
      });

      const playWeekBtn = document.getElementById('btn-play-week');
      if (playWeekBtn && nextRegGame) {
        const activeWeekNum = nextRegGame.week;
        const activeOppId = nextRegGame.oppId;
        const activeIsHome = nextRegGame.isHome;
        playWeekBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (!userUniformChoice) {
            showToast('SELECT HOME OR AWAY UNIFORM FIRST!');
            return;
          }
          const curCap = getSoftCapStatus();
          if (career.roster.length > curCap.maxRoster) {
            showToast(`ROSTER OVER SOFT-CAP LIMIT (${career.roster.length}/${curCap.maxRoster})! CUT ${career.roster.length - curCap.maxRoster} PLAYER(S) FIRST.`);
            openHub('roster');
            return;
          }
          sfx.playClick();
          startMatch(career.teamId, activeOppId, false, activeWeekNum, null, activeIsHome);
        });
      }

      const simWeekBtn = document.getElementById('btn-sim-week');
      if (simWeekBtn && nextRegGame) {
        const activeWeekNum = nextRegGame.week;
        const activeOppId = nextRegGame.oppId;
        const activeIsHome = nextRegGame.isHome;
        simWeekBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          const targetGame = career.schedule.find(g => g.week === activeWeekNum) || nextRegGame;
          const res = activeIsHome
            ? simSingleMatchupScore(career.teamId, activeOppId)
            : simSingleMatchupScore(activeOppId, career.teamId);
          const userScore = activeIsHome ? res.homeScore : res.awayScore;
          const oppScore = activeIsHome ? res.awayScore : res.homeScore;
          targetGame.played = true;
          targetGame.userScore = userScore;
          targetGame.oppScore = oppScore;

          const wkGames = career.weekGames?.[activeWeekNum - 1];
          let lg = Array.isArray(wkGames)
            ? wkGames.find(m => (m.homeId || m.home) === career.teamId || (m.awayId || m.away) === career.teamId)
            : null;
          if (lg) {
            lg.homeId = lg.homeId || lg.home;
            lg.awayId = lg.awayId || lg.away;
            lg.played = true;
            lg.homeScore = res.homeScore;
            lg.awayScore = res.awayScore;
          } else if (Array.isArray(wkGames)) {
            wkGames.push({
              week: activeWeekNum,
              homeId: activeIsHome ? career.teamId : activeOppId,
              awayId: activeIsHome ? activeOppId : career.teamId,
              played: true,
              homeScore: res.homeScore,
              awayScore: res.awayScore
            });
          }
          applyGameResultToStandings(
            activeIsHome ? career.teamId : activeOppId,
            activeIsHome ? activeOppId : career.teamId,
            res.homeScore,
            res.awayScore
          );
          simulateLeagueWeek(activeWeekNum, true);
          const nextOpen = career.schedule.find(g => !g.played);
          career.week = nextOpen ? nextOpen.week : 19;
          if (userScore >= oppScore) {
            career.cc += 2 + getCombinedFacilityBonuses().winCcBonus;
            showToast(`WK ${activeWeekNum} VICTORY! ${userScore}-${oppScore}`);
          } else {
            career.cc += 1;
            showToast(`WK ${activeWeekNum} DEFEAT: ${userScore}-${oppScore}`);
          }
          checkRegularSeasonComplete();
          saveCareer();
          openHub('office');
        });
      }

      const byeBtn = document.getElementById('btn-advance-bye');
      if (byeBtn && nextRegGame && nextRegGame.isBye) {
        const activeByeWeek = nextRegGame.week;
        byeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          const targetBye = career.schedule.find(g => g.week === activeByeWeek) || nextRegGame;
          targetBye.played = true;
          simulateLeagueWeek(activeByeWeek, true);
          const nextOpen = career.schedule.find(g => !g.played);
          career.week = nextOpen ? nextOpen.week : 19;
          career.cc += 1;
          showToast(`WEEK ${activeByeWeek} BYE COMPLETE (+1 CC)!`);
          checkRegularSeasonComplete();
          saveCareer();
          openHub('office');
        });
      }

      const simSeasonBtn = document.getElementById('btn-sim-season');
      if (simSeasonBtn) {
        simSeasonBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          simulateEntireRegularSeason();
          showToast('REGULAR SEASON SIMULATED! PLAYOFFS BEGIN.');
          openHub('standings');
        });
      }

      const playPoBtn = document.getElementById('btn-play-po');
      if (playPoBtn && poMatch) {
        playPoBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          if (!userUniformChoice) {
            showToast('SELECT HOME OR AWAY UNIFORM FIRST!');
            return;
          }
          const curCap = getSoftCapStatus();
          if (career.roster.length > curCap.maxRoster) {
            showToast(`ROSTER OVER SOFT-CAP LIMIT (${career.roster.length}/${curCap.maxRoster})! CUT ${career.roster.length - curCap.maxRoster} PLAYER(S) FIRST.`);
            openHub('roster');
            return;
          }
          sfx.playClick();
          startMatch(career.teamId, poMatch.oppId, false, 19, poMatch);
        });
      }

      const simPoBtn = document.getElementById('btn-sim-po');
      if (simPoBtn) {
        simPoBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          simulatePlayoffRound(false);
          openHub(career.phase === 'OFFSEASON' ? 'draft' : 'standings');
        });
      }

      const draftBtn = document.getElementById('btn-go-draft');
      if (draftBtn) {
        draftBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          sfx.playClick();
          openHub('draft');
        });
      }
    }

    function recordMatchResultToCareer(homeScore, awayScore, oppId, weekNum = null, poMeta = null, userIsHome = true) {
      if (!career) return;
      if (poMeta && career.phase === 'PLAYOFFS' && career.playoffs) {
        const m = poMeta.matchObj;
        if (poMeta.round === 'SUPERBOWL') {
          const userIsAfc = m.afcId === career.teamId;
          m.afcScore = userIsAfc ? homeScore : awayScore;
          m.nfcScore = userIsAfc ? awayScore : homeScore;
          m.played = true;
          m.winnerId = homeScore >= awayScore ? career.teamId : oppId;
        } else {
          const userIsHigh = m.highId === career.teamId;
          m.highScore = userIsHigh ? homeScore : awayScore;
          m.lowScore = userIsHigh ? awayScore : homeScore;
          m.played = true;
          m.winnerId = homeScore >= awayScore ? career.teamId : oppId;
        }
        simulatePlayoffRound(false);
        return;
      }

      const nextUnplayed = Array.isArray(career.schedule) ? career.schedule.find(x => !x.played && !x.isBye) : null;
      const targetWeek = weekNum || (nextUnplayed ? nextUnplayed.week : career.week);
      const g = career.schedule.find(x => x.week === targetWeek && !x.isBye)
        || career.schedule.find(x => !x.played && !x.isBye && x.oppId === oppId);
      const actualWeek = g ? g.week : targetWeek;
      const isHome = g ? g.isHome : userIsHome;
      if (g) {
        g.played = true;
        g.userScore = homeScore;
        g.oppScore = awayScore;
      }
      const wkGames = career.weekGames?.[actualWeek - 1];
      const lg = Array.isArray(wkGames)
        ? wkGames.find(m => m.homeId === career.teamId || m.awayId === career.teamId)
        : null;
      if (lg) {
        lg.played = true;
        lg.homeScore = isHome ? homeScore : awayScore;
        lg.awayScore = isHome ? awayScore : homeScore;
      } else if (Array.isArray(wkGames)) {
        wkGames.push({
          week: actualWeek,
          homeId: isHome ? career.teamId : oppId,
          awayId: isHome ? oppId : career.teamId,
          played: true,
          homeScore: isHome ? homeScore : awayScore,
          awayScore: isHome ? awayScore : homeScore
        });
      }
      applyGameResultToStandings(
        isHome ? career.teamId : oppId,
        isHome ? oppId : career.teamId,
        isHome ? homeScore : awayScore,
        isHome ? awayScore : homeScore
      );
      simulateLeagueWeek(actualWeek, true);
      const nextOpen = career.schedule.find(x => !x.played);
      career.week = nextOpen ? nextOpen.week : 19;
      checkRegularSeasonComplete();
      saveCareer();
    }

    function openBoxScoreModal() {
      const modal = document.getElementById('overlay-boxscore');
      const body = document.getElementById('boxscore-body');
      if (!modal || !body || !match) return;
      const s = match.stats;
      const uName = match.userTeam.id;
      const cName = match.cpuTeam.id;
      const hTeam = match.userIsHome ? match.userTeam : match.cpuTeam;
      const aTeam = match.userIsHome ? match.cpuTeam : match.userTeam;
      const hScore = match.userIsHome ? match.userScore : match.cpuScore;
      const aScore = match.userIsHome ? match.cpuScore : match.userScore;
      const hStats = match.userIsHome ? s.user : s.cpu;
      const aStats = match.userIsHome ? s.cpu : s.user;

      const uComp = s.user.passComp || 0;
      const uAtt = s.user.passAtt || 0;
      const uPassYds = s.user.passYds || 0;
      const uRushAtt = s.user.rushAtt || 0;
      const uRushYds = s.user.rushYds || 0;
      const uTds = s.user.tds || 0;
      const uTurnovers = (s.user.ints || 0) + (s.user.fumbles || 0);
      const uSacks = s.user.sacks || 0;

      const cComp = s.cpu.passComp || 0;
      const cAtt = s.cpu.passAtt || 0;
      const cPassYds = s.cpu.passYds || 0;
      const cRushAtt = s.cpu.rushAtt || 0;
      const cRushYds = s.cpu.rushYds || 0;
      const cTds = s.cpu.tds || 0;
      const cTurnovers = (s.cpu.ints || 0) + (s.cpu.fumbles || 0);
      const cSacks = s.cpu.sacks || 0;

      const uTotalYds = uPassYds + uRushYds;
      const cTotalYds = cPassYds + cRushYds;

      const renderPlayerList = (title, mapObj, formatter, emptyLabel = 'NO STATS RECORDED YET') => {
        const entries = Object.entries(mapObj || {});
        if (!entries.length) {
          return `
            <div class="bg-slate-950 p-3 sm:p-4 border-2 border-slate-800">
              <div class="text-sm sm:text-base text-yellow-400 mb-1.5">${title}</div>
              <div class="text-slate-400 text-xs sm:text-sm">${emptyLabel}</div>
            </div>
          `;
        }
        return `
          <div class="bg-slate-950 p-3 sm:p-4 border-2 border-slate-800">
            <div class="text-sm sm:text-base text-yellow-400 mb-2">${title}</div>
            <div class="space-y-1.5 text-xs sm:text-base">
              ${entries.map(([name, val]) => `
                <div class="flex justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-800/80 pb-1 last:border-b-0">
                  <span class="text-white truncate">${name}</span>
                  <span class="text-emerald-300 shrink-0">${formatter(val)}</span>
                </div>
              `).join('')}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      };

      body.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-slate-950 p-4 border-2 border-slate-700">
          <div class="grid grid-cols-6 text-center text-yellow-300 border-b-2 border-slate-800 pb-2 mb-2 text-xs sm:text-base">
            <div class="text-left">TEAM</div><div>Q1</div><div>Q2</div><div>Q3</div><div>Q4</div><div>TOT</div>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-6 text-center text-white mb-2 text-sm sm:text-lg">
            <div class="text-left text-slate-300">${aTeam.id} (AWAY)</div>
            <div>${aStats.q[0]}</div><div>${aStats.q[1]}</div><div>${aStats.q[2]}</div><div>${aStats.q[3]}</div>
            <div class="text-yellow-400">${aScore}</div>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-6 text-center text-white text-sm sm:text-lg">
            <div class="text-left text-emerald-300">${hTeam.id} (HOME)</div>
            <div>${hStats.q[0]}</div><div>${hStats.q[1]}</div><div>${hStats.q[2]}</div><div>${hStats.q[3]}</div>
            <div class="text-yellow-400">${hScore}</div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="bg-slate-950 p-4 border-2 border-slate-800 space-y-2 text-xs sm:text-base">
          <div class="text-yellow-400 text-sm sm:text-base border-b border-slate-800 pb-1.5 mb-2">TEAM COMPARISON (${uName} VS ${cName})</div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>TOTAL YARDS</span><span class="text-emerald-300">${uName}: ${uTotalYds} &bull; ${cName}: ${cTotalYds}</span></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>PASSING</span><span class="text-sky-300">${uName}: ${uComp}/${uAtt}, ${uPassYds} YDS &bull; ${cName}: ${cComp}/${cAtt}, ${cPassYds} YDS</span></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>RUSHING</span><span class="text-amber-300">${uName}: ${uRushAtt} ATT, ${uRushYds} YDS &bull; ${cName}: ${cRushAtt} ATT, ${cRushYds} YDS</span></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>TOUCHDOWNS</span><span class="text-yellow-300">${uName}: ${uTds} &bull; ${cName}: ${cTds}</span></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between"><span>SACKS / TURNOVERS</span><span class="text-rose-300">${uName}: ${uSacks} SCK, ${uTurnovers} TO &bull; ${cName}: ${cSacks} SCK, ${cTurnovers} TO</span></div>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
          ${renderPlayerList(`${uName} PASSING`, s.players.pass, p => `${p.comp}/${p.att}, ${p.yds} YDS, ${p.td} TD, ${p.int} INT`)}
          ${renderPlayerList(`${uName} RUSHING`, s.players.rush, p => `${p.att} ATT, ${p.yds} YDS, ${p.td} TD`)}
          ${renderPlayerList(`${uName} RECEIVING`, s.players.rec, p => `${p.catches} REC, ${p.yds} YDS, ${p.td} TD`)}
          ${renderPlayerList(`${uName} DEFENSE`, s.players.def, p => `${p.tkl} TKL, ${p.sck} SCK, ${p.int} INT`)}
        </div>
      `;
      modal.classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    /* =========================================================
       6. LIVE 2D ARCADE MATCH ENGINE (FIELD, PHYSICS, RENDERING)
       ========================================================= */
    const canvas = document.getElementById('game-canvas');
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');

    const FIELD_W = 120; // 0..10 left EZ, 10..110 playing field, 110..120 right EZ
    const FIELD_H = 53.3;

    function resizeCanvas() {
      const wrap = document.getElementById('canvas-wrap');
      if (!wrap) return;
      canvas.width = wrap.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
      canvas.height = wrap.clientHeight || (window.innerHeight - 56);
    }

    function startMatch(userTeamId, cpuTeamId, isQuickPlay = false, weekNum = null, poMeta = null, userIsHome = true) {
      if (!userUniformChoice) {
        showToast('SELECT HOME OR AWAY UNIFORM FIRST!');
        return;
      }
      const userTeam = getUserTeam(userTeamId);
      const cpuTeam = getUserTeam(cpuTeamId);
      const diff = DIFFICULTIES[selectedDifficulty] || DIFFICULTIES.PRO;

      const userUni = buildTeamUniform(userTeam, userUniformChoice);
      // Ensure high-contrast uniform separation for CPU opponent
      let cpuMode = userUniformChoice === 'HOME' ? 'AWAY' : 'HOME';
      if (userUniformChoice === 'HOME' && userTeam.primary === cpuTeam.primary) {
        cpuMode = 'AWAY';
      }
      const cpuUni = buildTeamUniform(cpuTeam, cpuMode);

      let roster = [];
      if (!isQuickPlay && career && career.teamId === userTeamId) {
        roster = career.roster;
      } else {
        roster = generateFullNoLinemanRoster(userTeam);
      }

      const pickStarterOrBest = (pos, idx = 0) => {
        const starters = roster.filter(p => p.pos === pos && p.isStarter).sort((a, b) => (b.ovr || 0) - (a.ovr || 0));
        if (starters[idx]) return starters[idx];
        const allAtPos = roster.filter(p => p.pos === pos).sort((a, b) => (b.ovr || 0) - (a.ovr || 0));
        return allAtPos[idx] || allAtPos[0] || null;
      };

      const qb = pickStarterOrBest('QB', 0) || { name: `${userTeam.id} QB`, spd: 7, arm: 8, acc: 8, ovr: 82 };
      const rb = pickStarterOrBest('RB', 0) || { name: `${userTeam.id} RB`, spd: 8, arm: 4, acc: 8, ovr: 82 };
      const wr1 = pickStarterOrBest('WR', 0) || { name: `${userTeam.id} WR1`, spd: 9, arm: 3, acc: 9, ovr: 84 };
      const wr2 = pickStarterOrBest('WR', 1) || { name: `${userTeam.id} WR2`, spd: 8, arm: 3, acc: 8, ovr: 80 };
      const wr3 = pickStarterOrBest('WR', 2) || { name: `${userTeam.id} WR3`, spd: 8, arm: 3, acc: 8, ovr: 78 };
      const te = pickStarterOrBest('TE', 0) || { name: `${userTeam.id} TE`, spd: 7, arm: 4, acc: 8, ovr: 80 };
      const lb1 = pickStarterOrBest('LB', 0) || { name: `${userTeam.id} MLB`, spd: 8, arm: 4, acc: 8, ovr: 82 };
      const lb2 = pickStarterOrBest('LB', 1) || { name: `${userTeam.id} OLB`, spd: 8, arm: 4, acc: 8, ovr: 80 };
      const cb1 = pickStarterOrBest('CB', 0) || { name: `${userTeam.id} CB1`, spd: 9, arm: 3, acc: 9, ovr: 83 };
      const cb2 = pickStarterOrBest('CB', 1) || { name: `${userTeam.id} CB2`, spd: 9, arm: 3, acc: 8, ovr: 80 };
      const fs = pickStarterOrBest('S', 0) || { name: `${userTeam.id} FS`, spd: 9, arm: 3, acc: 8, ovr: 81 };
      const ss = pickStarterOrBest('S', 1) || { name: `${userTeam.id} SS`, spd: 8, arm: 3, acc: 8, ovr: 80 };

      const userRatings = getTeamRatings(userTeamId);
      const cpuRatings = getTeamRatings(cpuTeamId);

      // Build dynamic 32-team turf theme (each stadium has authentic primary/secondary field colors)
      const stadiumPalette = {
        primary: userIsHome ? userTeam.primary : cpuTeam.primary,
        secondary: userIsHome ? userTeam.secondary : cpuTeam.secondary,
        homeId: userIsHome ? userTeam.id : cpuTeam.id,
        homeCity: userIsHome ? userTeam.city : cpuTeam.city,
        homeName: userIsHome ? userTeam.name : cpuTeam.name
      };

      match = {
        isQuickPlay,
        weekNum,
        poMeta,
        userIsHome,
        stadiumPalette,
        userTeam,
        cpuTeam,
        userUni,
        cpuUni,
        userRatings,
        cpuRatings,
        diff,
        qb, rb, wr1, wr2, wr3, te, lb1, lb2, cb1, cb2, fs, ss,
        userScore: 0,
        cpuScore: 0,
        quarter: 1,
        clock: 120,
        possession: 'USER',
        driveDir: 1, // 1 = driving left-to-right toward x=110; -1 = driving right-to-left toward x=10
        los: 35,
        firstDownLine: 45,
        down: 1,
        ydLineNum: 25,
        stamina: 100,
        phase: 'PLAYCALL',
        currentPlay: null,
        selectedDefenderId: 'LB_1',
        defConceptKey: 'COVER_2',
        cpuPreSnapShell: 'COVER_2',
        playClock: 0,
        players: [],
        ball: null,
        kick: { pos: 50, dir: 1, speed: 115, dist: 35, isPat: false },
        stats: {
          user: { q: [0, 0, 0, 0], passAtt: 0, passComp: 0, passYds: 0, rushAtt: 0, rushYds: 0, tds: 0, ints: 0, fumbles: 0, sacks: 0 },
          cpu:  { q: [0, 0, 0, 0], passAtt: 0, passComp: 0, passYds: 0, rushAtt: 0, rushYds: 0, tds: 0, ints: 0, fumbles: 0, sacks: 0 },
          players: { pass: {}, rush: {}, rec: {}, def: {} },
          cpuPlayers: { pass: {}, rush: {}, rec: {}, def: {} }
        }
      };

      ['overlay-playcall', 'overlay-def-playcall', 'overlay-presnap', 'overlay-def-presnap', 'overlay-kick', 'overlay-sim', 'overlay-postgame', 'overlay-boxscore'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
      });

      const homeT = userIsHome ? userTeam : cpuTeam;
      const awayT = userIsHome ? cpuTeam : userTeam;
      const homeU = userIsHome ? userUni : cpuUni;
      const awayU = userIsHome ? cpuUni : userUni;

      document.getElementById('hud-away-name').textContent = awayT.id;
      document.getElementById('hud-away-color').style.background = awayU.jersey;
      document.getElementById('hud-home-name').textContent = homeT.id;
      document.getElementById('hud-home-color').style.background = homeU.jersey;

      switchScreen('screen-game');
      updateGameHUD();
      openPlaycallOverlay();
    }

    function updateGameHUD() {
      if (!match) return;
      const hScore = match.userIsHome ? match.userScore : match.cpuScore;
      const aScore = match.userIsHome ? match.cpuScore : match.userScore;
      document.getElementById('hud-home-score').textContent = hScore;
      document.getElementById('hud-away-score').textContent = aScore;

      const downStr = ['1ST', '2ND', '3RD', '4TH'][Math.min(3, match.down - 1)];
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const targetEz = dir === 1 ? 110 : 10;
      const ownEz = dir === 1 ? 10 : 110;
      const toGo = Math.max(1, Math.round((match.firstDownLine - match.los) * dir));
      const goalDist = Math.max(1, Math.round((targetEz - match.los) * dir));
      const isGoalToGo = dir === 1 ? match.firstDownLine >= 110 : match.firstDownLine <= 10;
      const distText = isGoalToGo ? `GOAL (${goalDist})` : toGo;

      let spotText = '';
      const distFromOwn = Math.round((match.los - ownEz) * dir);
      if (distFromOwn <= 50) {
        spotText = `OWN ${Math.max(1, distFromOwn)}`;
      } else {
        spotText = `OPP ${Math.max(1, 100 - distFromOwn)}`;
      }

      document.getElementById('hud-down-dist').textContent = `${downStr} & ${distText} • ${spotText}`;

      const mins = Math.floor(match.clock / 60);
      const secs = Math.floor(match.clock % 60);
      document.getElementById('hud-clock').textContent =
        `Q${match.quarter} • ${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`;

      updateLiveHudStatTrackers();
    }

    function formatTopLeaderText(statMap, defaultName, fallbackYds = 0, includePassTdInt = false) {
      const entries = Object.entries(statMap || {});
      if (!entries.length) {
        return { name: defaultName, yds: fallbackYds, td: 0, int: 0 };
      }
      entries.sort((a, b) => (b[1].yds || 0) - (a[1].yds || 0));
      const [fullName, data] = entries[0];
      const parts = fullName.trim().split(' ');
      const shortName = parts.length > 1 ? `${parts[0][0]}. ${parts.slice(1).join(' ')}` : fullName;
      return {
        name: shortName,
        yds: data.yds || 0,
        td: includePassTdInt ? (data.td || 0) : 0,
        int: includePassTdInt ? (data.int || 0) : 0
      };
    }

    function updateLiveHudStatTrackers() {
      if (!match || !match.stats) return;
      const awayEl = document.getElementById('hud-away-leaders');
      const homeEl = document.getElementById('hud-home-leaders');
      if (!awayEl || !homeEl) return;

      const userPass = formatTopLeaderText(match.stats.players.pass, match.qb.name.split(' ').pop(), match.stats.user.passYds || 0, true);
      if (!Object.keys(match.stats.players.pass || {}).length) {
        userPass.int = match.stats.user.ints || 0;
      }
      const userRec  = formatTopLeaderText(match.stats.players.rec, match.wr1.name.split(' ').pop(), 0);
      const userRush = formatTopLeaderText(match.stats.players.rush, match.rb.name.split(' ').pop(), match.stats.user.rushYds || 0);

      const cpuQbStar = (match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'QB') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} QB` };
      const cpuWrStar = (match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'WR' || s.pos === 'TE') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} WR` };
      const cpuRbStar = (match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'RB') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} RB` };

      const cpuPass = formatTopLeaderText(match.stats.cpuPlayers?.pass, cpuQbStar.name.split(' ').pop(), match.stats.cpu.passYds || 0, true);
      if (!Object.keys(match.stats.cpuPlayers?.pass || {}).length) {
        cpuPass.int = match.stats.cpu.ints || 0;
      }
      const cpuRec  = formatTopLeaderText(match.stats.cpuPlayers?.rec, cpuWrStar.name.split(' ').pop(), 0);
      const cpuRush = formatTopLeaderText(match.stats.cpuPlayers?.rush, cpuRbStar.name.split(' ').pop(), match.stats.cpu.rushYds || 0);

      const awayPack = match.userIsHome
        ? { pass: cpuPass, rec: cpuRec, rush: cpuRush }
        : { pass: userPass, rec: userRec, rush: userRush };
      const homePack = match.userIsHome
        ? { pass: userPass, rec: userRec, rush: userRush }
        : { pass: cpuPass, rec: cpuRec, rush: cpuRush };

      awayEl.innerHTML = `
        <span class="text-sky-300 truncate">PASS: ${awayPack.pass.name} ${awayPack.pass.yds} YDS, ${awayPack.pass.td} TD, ${awayPack.pass.int} INT</span>
        <span class="text-emerald-300 truncate">REC: ${awayPack.rec.name} ${awayPack.rec.yds} YDS &bull; RUSH: ${awayPack.rush.name} ${awayPack.rush.yds} YDS</span>
      `;
      homeEl.innerHTML = `
        <span class="text-sky-300 truncate">PASS: ${homePack.pass.name} ${homePack.pass.yds} YDS, ${homePack.pass.td} TD, ${homePack.pass.int} INT</span>
        <span class="text-emerald-300 truncate">REC: ${homePack.rec.name} ${homePack.rec.yds} YDS &bull; RUSH: ${homePack.rush.name} ${homePack.rush.yds} YDS</span>
      `;
    }

    function triggerBanner(text, duration = 1250) {
      const box = document.getElementById('field-banner');
      const txt = document.getElementById('field-banner-text');
      if (!box || !txt) return;
      txt.textContent = text;
      box.classList.remove('hidden');
      clearTimeout(box._bannerTimer);
      box._bannerTimer = setTimeout(() => box.classList.add('hidden'), duration);
    }

    function renderOffensivePlayDiagramSVG(play) {
      const baseGrid = `
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="160" height="72" rx="4" fill="#0f2b1d" stroke="#1e293b" stroke-width="2"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="20" x2="160" y2="20" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.14" stroke-width="1"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="38" x2="160" y2="38" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.14" stroke-width="1"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="54" x2="160" y2="54" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="3,2"/>
      `;
      const olDots = [66, 73, 80, 87, 94].map(x => `<rect x="${x - 2}" y="52" width="4" height="4" fill="#94a3b8"/>`).join('');
      let routesSvg = '';

      if (play.type === 'RUN') {
        const runOffset = (play.runDir || 0) * 34;
        routesSvg = `
          <circle cx="80" cy="60" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="67" r="3.2" fill="#f97316"/>
          <polyline points="80,67 ${80 + runOffset * 0.5},54 ${80 + runOffset},24" fill="none" stroke="#f97316" stroke-width="2.6"/>
          <polygon points="${80 + runOffset},20 ${76 + runOffset},27 ${84 + runOffset},27" fill="#f97316"/>
        `;
      } else if (play.routes) {
        const startMap = {
          WR1: { x: 22, y: 54 },
          WR2: { x: 138, y: 54 },
          WR3: { x: 44, y: 56 },
          TE:  { x: 103, y: 54 },
          RB:  { x: 74, y: 63 }
        };
        Object.entries(play.routes).forEach(([slot, pts]) => {
          const st = startMap[slot] || { x: 80, y: 56 };
          const strokeColor = slot === 'WR1' ? '#facc15' : (slot === 'RB' ? '#34d399' : '#38bdf8');
          let curX = st.x;
          let curY = st.y;
          const polyPts = [`${curX},${curY}`];
          pts.forEach(pt => {
            curX = Math.max(8, Math.min(152, st.x + pt.dy * 1.8));
            curY = Math.max(8, Math.min(68, st.y - pt.dx * 1.45));
            polyPts.push(`${curX.toFixed(1)},${curY.toFixed(1)}`);
          });
          routesSvg += `
            <circle cx="${st.x}" cy="${st.y}" r="2.6" fill="${strokeColor}"/>
            <polyline points="${polyPts.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="${strokeColor}" stroke-width="2"/>
            <circle cx="${curX.toFixed(1)}" cy="${curY.toFixed(1)}" r="2.2" fill="${strokeColor}"/>
          `;
        });
      }
      return `<svg viewBox="0 0 160 72" class="w-full h-16 sm:h-20 block my-0.5">${baseGrid}${olDots}${routesSvg}</svg>`;
    }

    function renderOffFormationDiagramSVG(formId) {
      const baseGrid = `
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="160" height="72" rx="4" fill="#0f2b1d" stroke="#1e293b" stroke-width="2"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="20" x2="160" y2="20" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.14" stroke-width="1"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="38" x2="160" y2="38" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.14" stroke-width="1"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="54" x2="160" y2="54" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="3,2"/>
      `;
      const olDots = [66, 73, 80, 87, 94].map(x => `<rect x="${x - 2}" y="52" width="4" height="4" fill="#94a3b8"/>`).join('');
      let skillNodes = '';

      if (formId === 'SINGLEBACK') {
        skillNodes = `
          <circle cx="80" cy="60" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="67" r="3.2" fill="#34d399"/>
          <circle cx="103" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="22" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="138" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="44" cy="56" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      } else if (formId === 'I_FORM') {
        skillNodes = `
          <circle cx="80" cy="59" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
