          <circle cx="80" cy="64" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="69" r="3.2" fill="#34d399"/>
          <circle cx="103" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="22" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="138" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      } else if (formId === 'PISTOL') {
        skillNodes = `
          <circle cx="80" cy="61" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="68" r="3.2" fill="#34d399"/>
          <circle cx="103" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="22" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="138" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="54" cy="56" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      } else if (formId === 'SHOTGUN') {
        skillNodes = `
          <circle cx="80" cy="64" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="71" cy="64" r="3.2" fill="#34d399"/>
          <circle cx="103" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="20" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="140" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="44" cy="56" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      } else {
        // GOAL_LINE (3 TE + FB + RB)
        skillNodes = `
          <circle cx="80" cy="59" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="64" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="69" r="3.2" fill="#34d399"/>
          <circle cx="57" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="103" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="111" cy="54" r="2.8" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      }

      return `<svg viewBox="0 0 160 72" class="w-full h-16 sm:h-20 block my-0.5">${baseGrid}${olDots}${skillNodes}</svg>`;
    }

    function openPlaycallOverlay() {
      if (!match) return;
      match.possession = 'USER';
      match.phase = 'PLAYCALL';
      match.ball = null;
      match.ballCarrier = null;
      match.qbScrambleMode = false;

      const shellKeys = Object.keys(DEFENSIVE_CONCEPTS);
      match.cpuPreSnapShell = shellKeys[Math.floor(Math.random() * shellKeys.length)];

      ['overlay-def-playcall', 'overlay-presnap', 'overlay-def-presnap', 'overlay-kick', 'overlay-sim', 'mobile-controls'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
      });

      offPlayStep = 'FORMATION';
      offCarouselIdx = 0;
      renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
      document.getElementById('overlay-playcall').classList.remove('hidden');
      updateGameHUD();
    }

    function renderOffensivePlaybookUI() {
      if (!match) return;
      const sitEl = document.getElementById('playcall-situation');
      const hintEl = document.getElementById('playcall-def-hint');
      const footerEl = document.getElementById('playcall-footer');
      const optsEl = document.getElementById('playcall-options');
      const backBtn = document.getElementById('btn-off-back-formations');
      const fourthActions = document.getElementById('fourth-down-actions');
      const leftBtn = document.getElementById('btn-off-scroll-left');
      const rightBtn = document.getElementById('btn-off-scroll-right');
      if (!optsEl) return;

      const downStr = ['1ST', '2ND', '3RD', '4TH'][Math.min(3, match.down - 1)];
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const toGo = Math.max(1, Math.round((match.firstDownLine - match.los) * dir));
      const shellObj = DEFENSIVE_CONCEPTS[match.cpuPreSnapShell] || DEFENSIVE_CONCEPTS.COVER_2;

      if (hintEl) hintEl.textContent = `DEF LOOK: ${shellObj.shell}`;
      if (fourthActions) fourthActions.classList.toggle('hidden', match.down !== 4);

      if (offPlayStep === 'FORMATION') {
        if (sitEl) sitEl.textContent = `${downStr} & ${toGo} • SELECT FORMATION:`;
        if (backBtn) backBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        if (leftBtn) leftBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        if (rightBtn) rightBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        if (footerEl) footerEl.textContent = 'PRESS [1-5] OR CLICK A FORMATION TO VIEW PLAYS';

        optsEl.innerHTML = OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.map((f, i) => {
          const playCount = PLAYBOOK.filter(f.match).length;
          return `
            <button data-off-form="${f.id}" class="pixel-box p-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:border-yellow-400 text-left flex flex-col justify-between gap-1">
              <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] text-yellow-400">
                <span>[${i + 1}] ${f.name}</span>
                <span class="text-sky-300">${playCount} PLAYS</span>
              </div>
              ${renderOffFormationDiagramSVG(f.id)}
              <div class="text-[8px] text-emerald-300">${f.personnel}</div>
              <div class="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] text-slate-300 leading-tight">${f.desc}</div>
            </button>
          `;
        }).join('');

        optsEl.querySelectorAll('[data-off-form]').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            sfx.playClick();
            selectedOffFormationId = btn.getAttribute('data-off-form');
            offPlayStep = 'PLAY';
            offCarouselIdx = 0;
            renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
          });
        });
      } else {
        const formObj = OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedOffFormationId) || OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[0];
        const allPlays = PLAYBOOK.filter(formObj.match);
        const visiblePlays = allPlays.slice(offCarouselIdx, offCarouselIdx + 5);

        if (sitEl) sitEl.textContent = `${downStr} & ${toGo} • ${formObj.name}:`;
        if (backBtn) backBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (leftBtn) leftBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', offCarouselIdx <= 0);
        if (rightBtn) rightBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', offCarouselIdx + 5 >= allPlays.length);
        if (footerEl) footerEl.textContent = `PRESS [1-5] TO CALL PLAY • [A/D] TO SCROLL (${offCarouselIdx + 1}-${Math.min(allPlays.length, offCarouselIdx + 5)} OF ${allPlays.length})`;

        optsEl.innerHTML = visiblePlays.map((p, i) => `
          <button data-off-play-idx="${offCarouselIdx + i}" class="pixel-box p-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:border-yellow-400 text-left flex flex-col justify-between gap-1">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[9px] sm:text-[10px]">
              <span class="text-yellow-300 truncate">[${i + 1}] ${p.name}</span>
              <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[8px] ${p.type === 'RUN' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-black' : 'bg-sky-500 text-black'}">${p.tag}</span>
            </div>
            ${renderOffensivePlayDiagramSVG(p)}
            <div class="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] text-slate-300 leading-tight line-clamp-2">${p.desc}</div>
          </button>
        `).join('');

        optsEl.querySelectorAll('[data-off-play-idx]').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const pIdx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-off-play-idx'), 10);
            const chosen = allPlays[pIdx];
            if (chosen) {
              sfx.playClick();
              selectOffensivePlay(chosen);
            }
          });
        });
      }
    }

    function openDefensivePlaycallOverlay() {
      if (!match) return;
      match.possession = 'CPU';
      match.phase = 'DEF_PLAYCALL';
      match.ball = null;
      match.ballCarrier = null;

      ['overlay-playcall', 'overlay-presnap', 'overlay-def-presnap', 'overlay-kick', 'overlay-sim', 'mobile-controls'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
      });

      defPlayStep = 'FORMATION';
      defCarouselIdx = 0;
      renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
      document.getElementById('overlay-def-playcall').classList.remove('hidden');
      updateGameHUD();
    }

    function renderDefensivePlaybookUI() {
      if (!match) return;
      const sitEl = document.getElementById('def-playcall-situation');
      const footerEl = document.getElementById('def-playcall-footer');
      const optsEl = document.getElementById('def-playcall-options');
      const backBtn = document.getElementById('btn-def-back-formations');
      const leftBtn = document.getElementById('btn-def-scroll-left');
      const rightBtn = document.getElementById('btn-def-scroll-right');
      if (!optsEl) return;

      const downStr = ['1ST', '2ND', '3RD', '4TH'][Math.min(3, match.down - 1)];
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const toGo = Math.max(1, Math.round((match.firstDownLine - match.los) * dir));

      if (defPlayStep === 'FORMATION') {
        if (sitEl) sitEl.textContent = `DEFENSE • ${downStr} & ${toGo} • SELECT FORMATION:`;
        if (backBtn) backBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        if (leftBtn) leftBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        if (rightBtn) rightBtn.classList.add('hidden');
        if (footerEl) footerEl.textContent = 'PRESS [1-5] OR CLICK A DEFENSIVE FORMATION';

        optsEl.innerHTML = DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.map((f, i) => `
          <button data-def-form="${f.id}" class="pixel-box p-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:border-sky-400 text-left flex flex-col justify-between gap-1">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] text-sky-300">
              <span>[${i + 1}] ${f.name}</span>
              <span class="text-yellow-300">${f.plays.length} PLAYS</span>
            </div>
            ${renderDefFormationDiagramSVG(f.id)}
            <div class="text-[8px] text-emerald-300">${f.personnel}</div>
            <div class="text-[8px] text-slate-300 leading-tight">${f.desc}</div>
          </button>
        `).join('');

        optsEl.querySelectorAll('[data-def-form]').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            sfx.playClick();
            selectedDefFormationId = btn.getAttribute('data-def-form');
            defPlayStep = 'PLAY';
            defCarouselIdx = 0;
            renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
          });
        });
      } else {
        const formObj = DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedDefFormationId) || DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[1];
        const visiblePlays = formObj.plays.slice(defCarouselIdx, defCarouselIdx + 5);

        if (sitEl) sitEl.textContent = `DEFENSE • ${downStr} & ${toGo} • ${formObj.name}:`;
        if (backBtn) backBtn.classList.remove('hidden');
        if (leftBtn) leftBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', defCarouselIdx <= 0);
        if (rightBtn) rightBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', defCarouselIdx + 5 >= formObj.plays.length);
        if (footerEl) footerEl.textContent = 'PRESS [1-5] OR CLICK A COVERAGE/BLITZ CALL';

        optsEl.innerHTML = visiblePlays.map((dp, i) => `
          <button data-def-play-idx="${defCarouselIdx + i}" class="pixel-box p-2.5 bg-slate-900 hover:border-sky-400 text-left flex flex-col justify-between gap-1">
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between text-[9px] sm:text-[10px]">
              <span class="text-sky-300 truncate">[${i + 1}] ${dp.title}</span>
              <span class="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[8px] bg-sky-500 text-black">${dp.tag}</span>
            </div>
            ${renderDefensivePlayDiagramSVG(dp.key, formObj.id)}
            <div class="text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] text-slate-300 leading-tight">${dp.desc}</div>
          </button>
        `).join('');

        optsEl.querySelectorAll('[data-def-play-idx]').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const pIdx = parseInt(btn.getAttribute('data-def-play-idx'), 10);
            const chosenDef = formObj.plays[pIdx];
            if (chosenDef) {
              sfx.playClick();
              startUserDefensivePlay(chosenDef.key, formObj.id, chosenDef.title);
            }
          });
        });
      }
    }

    function renderDefFormationDiagramSVG(formId) {
      const baseGrid = `
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="160" height="72" rx="4" fill="#0f2537" stroke="#1e293b" stroke-width="2"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="20" x2="160" y2="20" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.14" stroke-width="1"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="38" x2="160" y2="38" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.14" stroke-width="1"/>
        <line x1="0" y1="56" x2="160" y2="56" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="3,2"/>
      `;
      let nodes = '';
      if (formId === '3-4') {
        nodes = `
          <rect x="64" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="78" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="92" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/>
          <circle cx="52" cy="46" r="3" fill="#facc15"/><circle cx="71" cy="40" r="3" fill="#facc15"/><circle cx="89" cy="40" r="3" fill="#facc15"/><circle cx="108" cy="46" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="20" cy="42" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="140" cy="42" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="56" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="104" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>

      } else if (formId === 'NICKEL') {
        nodes = `
          <rect x="60" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="72" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="84" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="96" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/>

          <circle cx="56" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="104" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      } else if (formId === 'DIME') {
        nodes = `
          <rect x="60" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="72" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="84" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="96" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="39" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="44" cy="41" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="116" cy="41" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="20" cy="38" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="140" cy="38" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="54" cy="15" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="106" cy="15" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      } else if (formId === 'PREVENT') {
        nodes = `
          <rect x="65" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="78" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="91" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/>
          <circle cx="80" cy="38" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="50" cy="26" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="80" cy="24" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="110" cy="26" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="20" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="140" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="58" cy="11" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="102" cy="11" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      } else {
        nodes = `
          <rect x="60" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="72" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="84" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/><rect x="96" y="50" width="5" height="5" fill="#f87171"/>
          <circle cx="56" cy="40" r="3" fill="#facc15"/><circle cx="80" cy="39" r="3" fill="#facc15"/><circle cx="104" cy="40" r="3" fill="#facc15"/>
          <circle cx="20" cy="42" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="140" cy="42" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
          <circle cx="56" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/><circle cx="104" cy="18" r="3" fill="#38bdf8"/>
        `;
      }
      return `<svg viewBox="0 0 160 72" class="w-full h-16 sm:h-20 block my-0.5">${baseGrid}${nodes}</svg>`;
    }

    function renderDefensivePlayDiagramSVG(conceptKey, formId) {
      let zones = '';
      let arrows = '';
      if (conceptKey === 'COVER_2') {
        zones = `
          <ellipse cx="46" cy="16" rx="34" ry="11" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="114" cy="16" rx="34" ry="11" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="22" cy="41" rx="16" ry="8" fill="#facc15" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="80" cy="37" rx="22" ry="8" fill="#facc15" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="138" cy="41" rx="16" ry="8" fill="#facc15" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.2"/>
        `;
        arrows = `<line x1="68" y1="51" x2="72" y2="62" stroke="#ef4444" stroke-width="2"/><line x1="92" y1="51" x2="88" y2="62" stroke="#ef4444" stroke-width="2"/>`;
      } else if (conceptKey === 'COVER_3') {
        zones = `
          <ellipse cx="28" cy="16" rx="22" ry="11" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="80" cy="14" rx="24" ry="11" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="132" cy="16" rx="22" ry="11" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="52" cy="39" rx="20" ry="8" fill="#facc15" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="108" cy="39" rx="20" ry="8" fill="#facc15" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.2"/>
        `;
        arrows = `<line x1="104" y1="22" x2="96" y2="43" stroke="#f97316" stroke-width="2" stroke-dasharray="3,2"/>`;
      } else if (conceptKey === 'MAN_2_UNDER') {
        zones = `
          <ellipse cx="46" cy="15" rx="32" ry="10" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="114" cy="15" rx="32" ry="10" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
        `;
        arrows = `
          <line x1="20" y1="43" x2="20" y2="54" stroke="#a78bfa" stroke-width="2.2"/>
          <line x1="54" y1="43" x2="54" y2="54" stroke="#a78bfa" stroke-width="2.2"/>
          <line x1="106" y1="43" x2="106" y2="54" stroke="#a78bfa" stroke-width="2.2"/>
          <line x1="140" y1="43" x2="140" y2="54" stroke="#a78bfa" stroke-width="2.2"/>
        `;
      } else if (conceptKey === 'BLITZ') {
        zones = `
          <ellipse cx="28" cy="16" rx="22" ry="10" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="80" cy="14" rx="24" ry="10" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="132" cy="16" rx="22" ry="10" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.22" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
        `;
        arrows = `
          <polyline points="56,41 66,53 74,64" fill="none" stroke="#ef4444" stroke-width="2.4"/>
          <polyline points="72,49 77,64" fill="none" stroke="#ef4444" stroke-width="2.4"/>
          <polyline points="88,49 83,64" fill="none" stroke="#ef4444" stroke-width="2.4"/>
          <polyline points="104,41 94,53 86,64" fill="none" stroke="#ef4444" stroke-width="2.4"/>
        `;
      } else if (conceptKey === 'COVER_4') {
        zones = [24, 61, 99, 136].map(cx =>
          `<ellipse cx="${cx}" cy="16" rx="16" ry="11" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.26" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>`
        ).join('') + `
          <ellipse cx="56" cy="39" rx="22" ry="7" fill="#facc15" fill-opacity="0.2" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.1"/>
          <ellipse cx="104" cy="39" rx="22" ry="7" fill="#facc15" fill-opacity="0.2" stroke="#facc15" stroke-width="1.1"/>
        `;
      } else {
        // COVER_2_CONTAIN
        zones = `
          <ellipse cx="46" cy="15" rx="32" ry="10" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <ellipse cx="114" cy="15" rx="32" ry="10" fill="#38bdf8" fill-opacity="0.24" stroke="#38bdf8" stroke-width="1.2"/>
          <rect x="64" y="34" width="32" height="10" rx="4" fill="#34d399" fill-opacity="0.25" stroke="#34d399" stroke-width="1.2"/>
        `;
        arrows = `
          <polyline points="56,49 52,58 64,63" fill="none" stroke="#f97316" stroke-width="2.2"/>

      return `
        <svg viewBox="0 0 160 72" class="w-full h-16 sm:h-20 block my-0.5">
          <rect x="0" y="0" width="160" height="72" rx="4" fill="#0f2537" stroke="#1e293b" stroke-width="2"/>
          <line x1="0" y1="22" x2="160" y2="22" stroke="#ffffff" stroke-opacity="0.14" stroke-width="1"/>

          ${zones}
          ${arrows}
        </svg>
      `;
    }

    function drawDetailedPlayerSprite(ctx, p, px, py, r, animClock) {
      // Determine movement delta for realistic leg stride & facing orientation
      const prevX = p._prevX !== undefined ? p._prevX : p.x;
      const prevY = p._prevY !== undefined ? p._prevY : p.y;
      const moveDist = Math.hypot(p.x - prevX, p.y - prevY);
      p._prevX = p.x;
      p._prevY = p.y;

      const isMoving = moveDist > 0.008;
      if (isMoving) {
        p._stridePhase = (p._stridePhase || 0) + moveDist * 3.4;
      } else {
        p._stridePhase = 0;
      }
      const dir = (p.side === 'OFF' ? 1 : -1) * (match ? (match.driveDir || 1) : 1);
      const stride = isMoving ? Math.sin(p._stridePhase) * (r * 0.45) : 0;
      const bob = isMoving ? Math.abs(Math.cos(p._stridePhase)) * (r * 0.16) : 0;
      const isLineman = p.role === 'OL' || p.role === 'DL';

      // 1. Controlled Player Gold Halo & Directional Chevron
      const isControlled = (match.possession === 'USER' && p === match.ballCarrier) ||
                           (match.possession === 'CPU' && p.id === match.selectedDefenderId);
      if (isControlled) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#facc15';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.4;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(px, py + r * 0.96, r + 4.5, (r + 4.5) * 0.48, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();

        // Floating yellow indicator arrow above controlled player's helmet
        const arrowY = py - r * 1.75 - bob;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(px, arrowY + 5);
        ctx.lineTo(px - 4.5, arrowY - 2);
        ctx.lineTo(px + 4.5, arrowY - 2);
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.restore();
      }

      // 2. Ground Drop Shadow
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.42)';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse(px, py + r * 1.0, r * 0.78, r * 0.34, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // 3. Slimmer Athletic Legs & Cleats
      const legY = py + r * 0.46 - bob * 0.4;
      const pantsColor = p.color === '#f8fafc' ? (p.secondary || '#1e293b') : '#f1f5f9';
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2.1, r * 0.25);
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';

      // Back leg
      ctx.strokeStyle = pantsColor;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(px - r * 0.14, legY);
      ctx.lineTo(px - r * 0.18 - stride, py + r * 1.0);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#090d16';
      ctx.fillRect(px - r * 0.30 - stride, py + r * 0.92, r * 0.34, r * 0.18);

      // Front leg
      ctx.strokeStyle = pantsColor;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(px + r * 0.14, legY);
      ctx.lineTo(px + r * 0.18 + stride, py + r * 1.0);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#090d16';
      ctx.fillRect(px + r * 0.08 + stride, py + r * 0.92, r * 0.34, r * 0.18);

      // 4. Lean Tapered Jersey Torso & Trim Shoulder Pads
      const torsoY = py - r * 0.08 - bob;
      const shoulderW = isLineman ? r * 0.86 : r * 0.68;
      const waistW = isLineman ? r * 0.68 : r * 0.48;
      const torsoTop = torsoY - r * 0.56;
      const torsoBot = torsoY + r * 0.52;

      ctx.fillStyle = p.color;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#090d16';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.8;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(px - shoulderW, torsoTop + r * 0.12);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(px, torsoTop - r * 0.06, px + shoulderW, torsoTop + r * 0.12);
      ctx.lineTo(px + waistW, torsoBot);
      ctx.lineTo(px - waistW, torsoBot);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.stroke();

      // Subtle shoulder pad trim stripes
      ctx.fillStyle = p.secondary || '#facc15';
      ctx.fillRect(px - shoulderW * 0.85, torsoTop + r * 0.12, shoulderW * 0.32, r * 0.22);
      ctx.fillRect(px + shoulderW * 0.53, torsoTop + r * 0.12, shoulderW * 0.32, r * 0.22);

      // 5. Sleeker Arms & Gloves
      const gloveColor = p.secondary || '#ffffff';
      ctx.strokeStyle = p.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(2.0, r * 0.22);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(px - shoulderW * 0.78, torsoTop + r * 0.22);
      ctx.lineTo(px - shoulderW * 0.86 + dir * (r * 0.32) - stride * 0.4, torsoY + r * 0.34);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = gloveColor;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(px - shoulderW * 0.86 + dir * (r * 0.32) - stride * 0.4, torsoY + r * 0.34, r * 0.16, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // Lead arm (stiff-arm pose when juking or diving tackle)
      const leadArmX = p.jukeTimer > 0 || p.diveTimer > 0
        ? px + dir * (shoulderW + r * 0.58)
        : px + shoulderW * 0.78 + dir * (r * 0.32) + stride * 0.4;
      const leadArmY = p.jukeTimer > 0 ? torsoY - r * 0.12 : torsoY + r * 0.32;
      ctx.strokeStyle = p.color;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(px + shoulderW * 0.78, torsoTop + r * 0.22);
      ctx.lineTo(leadArmX, leadArmY);
      ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle = gloveColor;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(leadArmX, leadArmY, r * 0.17, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();

      // 6. Proportioned Helmet, Center Stripe, Dark Visor & Facemask
      const headY = torsoTop - r * 0.38;
      const headR = r * 0.46;
      const helmetFill = p.helmetColor || p.secondary || '#ffffff';

      ctx.fillStyle = helmetFill;
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#090d16';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(px, headY, headR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.stroke();

      // Helmet center stripe
      ctx.fillStyle = p.color === helmetFill ? '#ffffff' : p.color;
      ctx.fillRect(px - headR * 0.2, headY - headR * 0.92, headR * 0.4, headR * 0.95);

      // Tinted Visor & Metallic Facemask pointing in facing direction
      const faceX = px + dir * (headR * 0.42);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f172a';
      ctx.fillRect(faceX - headR * 0.30, headY - headR * 0.24, headR * 0.64, headR * 0.32);

      ctx.strokeStyle = '#cbd5e1';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.3;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(faceX - headR * 0.20, headY + headR * 0.16);
      ctx.lineTo(faceX + dir * headR * 0.46, headY + headR * 0.16);
      ctx.moveTo(faceX - headR * 0.20, headY + headR * 0.38);
      ctx.lineTo(faceX + dir * headR * 0.42, headY + headR * 0.38);
      ctx.stroke();

      // 7. Laced Leather Football Tucked Under Arm
      if (p.hasBall) {
        const bx = px + dir * (shoulderW * 0.62);
        const by = torsoY + r * 0.08;
        ctx.save();
        ctx.translate(bx, by);
        ctx.rotate(dir * 0.35);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#854d0e';
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(0, 0, r * 0.50, r * 0.30, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.stroke();
        // White laces
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.1;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(-r * 0.18, 0);
        ctx.lineTo(r * 0.18, 0);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();
      }

      // 8. Crisp Receiver Pass Button Badge [1-5]
      if (match.possession === 'USER' && (match.phase === 'PRESNAP' || match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK') && p.side === 'OFF' && p.isEligible && p.num > 1) {
        const btnNum = p.num - 1;
        const badgeY = headY - headR - 14;
        ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
        ctx.fillRect(px - 9, badgeY, 18, 14);
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#090d16';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.6;
        ctx.strokeRect(px - 9, badgeY, 18, 14);
        ctx.fillStyle = '#090d16';
        ctx.font = 'bold 10px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
        ctx.textAlign = 'center';
        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        ctx.fillText(String(btnNum), px, badgeY + 7.5);
      }
    }

    function selectOffensivePlay(play) {
      if (!match) return;
      match.currentPlay = play;
      match.phase = 'PRESNAP';
      match.ball = null;
      setupPlayersForPlay(play, 'USER', match.cpuPreSnapShell || 'COVER_2');

      document.getElementById('overlay-playcall').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('presnap-play-name').textContent = `${play.formation} • ${play.name}`;
      document.getElementById('overlay-presnap').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function hikeBall() {
      if (!match || match.phase !== 'PRESNAP') return;
      sfx.playWhistle();
      document.getElementById('overlay-presnap').classList.add('hidden');

      const play = match.currentPlay;
      const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
      const rb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'RB');

      if (play.type === 'RUN') {
        match.phase = 'RUN_PLAY';
        match.playClock = 0;
        if (play.id === 'EM_QB_DRAW' || play.id === 'GL_TUSH_PUSH') {
          qb.hasBall = true;
          match.ballCarrier = qb;
        } else {
          rb.hasBall = true;
          match.ballCarrier = rb;
          match.stats.user.rushAtt += 1;
          const rName = match.rb.name;
          if (!match.stats.players.rush[rName]) match.stats.players.rush[rName] = { att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0 };
          match.stats.players.rush[rName].att += 1;
        }
      } else {
        match.phase = 'PASS_DROPBACK';
        match.playClock = 0;
        match.qbScrambleMode = false;
        qb.hasBall = true;
        match.ballCarrier = qb;
        match.stats.user.passAtt += 1;
        const qName = match.qb.name;
        if (!match.stats.players.pass[qName]) match.stats.players.pass[qName] = { comp: 0, att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0, int: 0 };
        match.stats.players.pass[qName].att += 1;
      }
      updateMobileControls();
    }

    function activateQbScramble() {
      if (!match || match.possession !== 'USER' || match.phase !== 'PASS_DROPBACK') return;
      const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
      if (!qb || !qb.hasBall) return;

      match.phase = 'RUN_PLAY';
      match.qbScrambleMode = true;
      match.ballCarrier = qb;

      // Convert remaining eligible receivers into downfield blockers for the scrambling QB
      match.players.forEach(p => {
        if (p.side === 'OFF' && p.isEligible) {
          p.isEligible = false;
          p.role = 'OL';
        }
      });

      // Undo the pre-counted pass attempt and log a rushing attempt instead
      match.stats.user.passAtt = Math.max(0, (match.stats.user.passAtt || 1) - 1);
      const qName = match.qb.name;
      if (match.stats.players.pass[qName] && match.stats.players.pass[qName].att > 0) {
        match.stats.players.pass[qName].att -= 1;
      }
      match.stats.user.rushAtt += 1;
      if (!match.stats.players.rush[qName]) match.stats.players.rush[qName] = { att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0 };
      match.stats.players.rush[qName].att += 1;

      sfx.playClick();
      triggerBanner('QB SCRAMBLE!', 1100);
      updateMobileControls();
    }

    function startUserDefensivePlay(conceptKey, defFormationId = '4-3', customTitle = null) {
      if (!match) return;
      match.defConceptKey = conceptKey;
      match.defFormationId = defFormationId;
      match.defPlayTitle = customTitle || (DEFENSIVE_CONCEPTS[conceptKey]?.name || conceptKey);
      // CPU selects an offensive play based on down & distance
      const cpuPlayPool = match.down >= 3 && (match.firstDownLine - match.los) >= 7
        ? PLAYBOOK.filter(p => p.type === 'PASS')
        : PLAYBOOK;
      const cpuPlay = cpuPlayPool[Math.floor(Math.random() * cpuPlayPool.length)];
      match.currentPlay = cpuPlay;
      match.phase = 'DEF_PRESNAP';
      match.playClock = 0;
      match.ball = null;

      setupPlayersForPlay(cpuPlay, 'CPU', conceptKey, defFormationId);
      // Default user control to middle linebacker
      match.selectedDefenderId = 'LB_1';

      document.getElementById('overlay-def-playcall').classList.add('hidden');
      const labelEl = document.getElementById('def-presnap-play-name');
      if (labelEl) labelEl.textContent = `${match.defPlayTitle} • ALIGN & SNAP`;
      document.getElementById('overlay-def-presnap').classList.remove('hidden');
      updateMobileControls();
    }

    function snapCpuOffenseBall() {
      if (!match || match.phase !== 'DEF_PRESNAP') return;
      sfx.playWhistle();
      document.getElementById('overlay-def-presnap').classList.add('hidden');
      match.phase = 'DEF_LIVE';
      match.playClock = 0;

      const cpuPlay = match.currentPlay || PLAYBOOK[0];
      const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
      const rb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'RB');
      if (cpuPlay.type === 'RUN') {
        rb.hasBall = true;
        match.ballCarrier = rb;
        match.stats.cpu.rushAtt += 1;
      } else {
        qb.hasBall = true;
        match.ballCarrier = qb;
        match.cpuThrowTimer = 1.45 + Math.random() * 1.35;
        match.stats.cpu.passAtt += 1;
      }
      updateMobileControls();
    }

    function updateMobileControls() {
      const box = document.getElementById('mobile-controls');
      const scrambleBtn = document.getElementById('btn-qb-scramble');
      const jukeBtn = document.getElementById('btn-juke');
      const swBtn = document.getElementById('btn-def-switch');
      const tkBtn = document.getElementById('btn-def-tackle');
      if (!box || !match) return;

      const isUserLive = match.possession === 'USER' && (match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK' || match.phase === 'RUN_PLAY');
      const isDefLive = match.possession === 'CPU' && match.phase === 'DEF_LIVE';

      box.classList.toggle('hidden', !isUserLive && !isDefLive);
      if (scrambleBtn) scrambleBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !(match.possession === 'USER' && match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK'));
      if (jukeBtn) jukeBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !(match.possession === 'USER' && match.phase === 'RUN_PLAY'));
      if (swBtn) swBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !isDefLive);
      if (tkBtn) tkBtn.classList.toggle('hidden', !isDefLive);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       8. 11-ON-11 FORMATION SETUP & ROUTE/DEFENSE ASSIGNMENTS
       ========================================================= */
    function setupPlayersForPlay(play, offSide = 'USER', defConceptKey = 'COVER_2', defFormationId = '4-3') {
      const los = match.los;
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const midY = FIELD_H / 2;
      const offTeam = offSide === 'USER' ? match.userTeam : match.cpuTeam;
      const defTeam = offSide === 'USER' ? match.cpuTeam : match.userTeam;
      const offUni = offSide === 'USER' ? match.userUni : match.cpuUni;
      const defUni = offSide === 'USER' ? match.cpuUni : match.userUni;
      const offStar = offSide === 'USER' ? match.userRatings.off : match.cpuRatings.off;
      const defStar = offSide === 'USER' ? match.cpuRatings.def : match.userRatings.def;

      const qbSpd = offSide === 'USER' ? match.qb.spd : (6 + offStar * 0.7);
      const rbSpd = offSide === 'USER' ? match.rb.spd : (7 + offStar * 0.6);
      const wr1Spd = offSide === 'USER' ? match.wr1.spd : (7 + offStar * 0.65);
      const wr2Spd = offSide === 'USER' ? match.wr2.spd : (7 + offStar * 0.6);
      const wr3Spd = offSide === 'USER' ? match.wr3.spd : (7 + offStar * 0.55);
      const teSpd = offSide === 'USER' ? match.te.spd : (6 + offStar * 0.55);

      const qbLabel = offSide === 'USER' ? match.qb.name.split(' ').pop() : 'QB';
      const rbLabel = offSide === 'USER' ? match.rb.name.split(' ').pop() : 'RB';
      const wr1Label = offSide === 'USER' ? match.wr1.name.split(' ').pop() : 'WR1';
      const wr2Label = offSide === 'USER' ? match.wr2.name.split(' ').pop() : 'WR2';
      const wr3Label = offSide === 'USER' ? match.wr3.name.split(' ').pop() : 'WR3';
      const teLabel = offSide === 'USER' ? match.te.name.split(' ').pop() : 'TE';

      const form = play.formation || '';
      const isShotgun = form.includes('SHOTGUN') || form.includes('SPREAD') || form.includes('TRIPS');
      const isIForm = form.includes('I-FORM');
      const isPistol = form.includes('PISTOL');
      const isGoalLine = form.includes('GOAL LINE');
      const isTrips = form.includes('TRIPS');
      const isBunch = form.includes('BUNCH');
      const isDoubleTight = form.includes('DOUBLE TIGHT');

      const qbX = los - dir * (isShotgun ? 5.2 : (isPistol ? 3.6 : (isGoalLine ? 1.5 : 2.2)));
      const rbX = los - dir * (isShotgun ? 5.2 : (isPistol ? 6.6 : (isIForm ? 6.8 : (isGoalLine ? 5.5 : 5.6))));
      const rbY = isShotgun ? midY - 3.2 : midY;

      let wr1Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY - 19 };
      let wr2Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY + 19 };
      let wr3Pos = { x: los - dir * 1.4, y: midY - 11 };
      let tePos  = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY + 7.2 };

      if (isTrips) {
        wr1Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY + 20 };
        wr2Pos = { x: los - dir * 1.3, y: midY + 14.5 };
        wr3Pos = { x: los - dir * 1.3, y: midY + 9.5 };
        tePos  = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY - 7.5 };
      } else if (isBunch) {
        wr1Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY + 15 };
        wr2Pos = { x: los - dir * 1.5, y: midY + 12.2 };
        wr3Pos = { x: los - dir * 1.5, y: midY + 17.5 };
        tePos  = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY - 7.5 };
      } else if (isIForm) {
        wr1Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY - 18.5 };
        wr2Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY + 18.5 };
        // Fullback aligned directly in front of RB in I-Formation
        wr3Pos = { x: los - dir * 4.4, y: midY };
        tePos  = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY + 7.2 };
      } else if (isGoalLine) {
        // Heavy 3-TE + Fullback Goal Line formation (no wideouts)
        wr1Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.9, y: midY - 7.4 };
        wr2Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.9, y: midY + 9.6 };

        wr1Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY - 18.5 };
        wr2Pos = { x: los - dir * 0.8, y: midY + 18.5 };

        tePos  = { x: los - dir * 0.9, y: midY + 7.2 };
      }

      const offPlayers = [
        { id: 'QB',  role: 'QB', num: 1, name: qbLabel,  x: qbX,      y: midY,      spd: qbSpd,  hasBall: false },
        { id: 'RB',  role: 'RB', num: 5, name: rbLabel,  x: rbX,      y: rbY,       spd: rbSpd,  hasBall: false, isEligible: true },

          const r = play.routes[p.id];
          if (r) {
            p.waypoints = r.map(pt => ({
              x: Math.min(116, Math.max(4, p.startX + dir * pt.dx)),
              y: Math.min(FIELD_H - 2, Math.max(2, p.startY + pt.dy))
            }));
          }
        });
      }

      const defBaseSpd = 6.75 + defStar * 0.46;
      const concept = DEFENSIVE_CONCEPTS[defConceptKey] || DEFENSIVE_CONCEPTS.COVER_2;

      // Distinct pre-snap alignments & personnel groupings for 3-4, 4-3, Nickel, Dime & Prevent
      let defRosterTemplate;
      if (defFormationId === '3-4') {

      } else if (defFormationId === 'NICKEL') {

          { id: 'FS',     role: 'DB', name: 'FS',  x: los + dir * 12.8,y: midY - 9.2, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.05 },
          { id: 'SS',     role: 'DB', name: 'SS',  x: los + dir * 12.8,y: midY + 9.2, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.04 }

      } else if (defFormationId === 'DIME') {
        defRosterTemplate = [

          { id: 'FS',     role: 'DB', name: 'FS',  x: los + dir * 14.5,y: midY - 9.5, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.06 },
          { id: 'SS',     role: 'DB', name: 'SS',  x: los + dir * 14.5,y: midY + 9.5, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.05 }
        ];
      } else if (defFormationId === 'PREVENT') {
        defRosterTemplate = [
          { id: 'DL_L',   role: 'DL', name: 'LE',  x: los + dir * 1.0, y: midY - 4.4, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.92 },

          { id: 'DL_R',   role: 'DL', name: 'RE',  x: los + dir * 1.0, y: midY + 4.4, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.92 },
          { id: 'LB_1',   role: 'LB', name: 'SPY', x: los + dir * 6.5, y: midY,       spd: defBaseSpd * 1.02 },
          { id: 'DL_DT2', role: 'DB', name: 'DB5', x: los + dir * 9.5, y: midY - 7.5, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.05 },
          { id: 'LB_2',   role: 'DB', name: 'DB6', x: los + dir * 9.5, y: midY + 7.5, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.05 },
          { id: 'NICKEL', role: 'DB', name: 'DB7', x: los + dir * 11.0,y: midY,       spd: defBaseSpd * 1.06 },
          { id: 'CB_1',   role: 'DB', name: 'CB1', x: los + dir * 13.0,y: midY - 17.5,spd: defBaseSpd * 1.08 },
          { id: 'CB_2',   role: 'DB', name: 'CB2', x: los + dir * 13.0,y: midY + 17.5,spd: defBaseSpd * 1.08 },
          { id: 'FS',     role: 'DB', name: 'FS',  x: los + dir * 18.0,y: midY - 8.5, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.06 },
          { id: 'SS',     role: 'DB', name: 'SS',  x: los + dir * 18.0,y: midY + 8.5, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.06 }
        ];
      } else {
        // Default 4-3 Base
        defRosterTemplate = [
          { id: 'DL_L',   role: 'DL', name: 'LE',  x: los + dir * 1.0, y: midY - 4.8, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.92 },
          { id: 'DL_DT1', role: 'DL', name: 'DT',  x: los + dir * 1.0, y: midY - 1.6, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.88 },
          { id: 'DL_DT2', role: 'DL', name: 'DT',  x: los + dir * 1.0, y: midY + 1.6, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.88 },
          { id: 'DL_R',   role: 'DL', name: 'RE',  x: los + dir * 1.0, y: midY + 4.8, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.92 },
          { id: 'LB_1',   role: 'LB', name: 'MLB', x: los + dir * 4.4, y: midY - 3.6, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.98 },
          { id: 'LB_2',   role: 'LB', name: 'OLB', x: los + dir * 4.4, y: midY + 3.6, spd: defBaseSpd * 0.98 },
          { id: 'NICKEL', role: 'LB', name: 'SLB', x: los + dir * 4.2, y: wr3Pos.y,   spd: defBaseSpd * 1.02 },
          { id: 'CB_1',   role: 'DB', name: 'CB1', x: los + dir * 4.2, y: wr1Pos.y,   spd: defBaseSpd * 1.08 },
          { id: 'CB_2',   role: 'DB', name: 'CB2', x: los + dir * 4.2, y: wr2Pos.y,   spd: defBaseSpd * 1.08 },
          { id: 'FS',     role: 'DB', name: 'FS',  x: los + dir * 12.5,y: midY - 9.0, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.05 },
          { id: 'SS',     role: 'DB', name: 'SS',  x: los + dir * 12.5,y: midY + 9.0, spd: defBaseSpd * 1.04 }
        ];
      }

      const defPlayers = defRosterTemplate.map(p => ({
        ...p,
        x: Math.max(4, Math.min(116, p.x)),
        side: 'DEF',
        color: defUni.jersey,
        secondary: defUni.trim,
        helmetColor: defUni.helmet,
        assignment: concept.assignments[p.id] || 'HOOK_MID',
        diveTimer: 0
      }));

      match.players = [...offPlayers, ...defPlayers];
      match.ballCarrier = null;
    }

    function passToReceiver(recPlayer) {
      if (!match || match.phase !== 'PASS_DROPBACK') return;
      const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
      if (!qb || !qb.hasBall) return;
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;

      // Prevent illegal forward pass once QB scrambles past the line of scrimmage
      if ((qb.x - match.los) * dir > 0.5) {
        triggerBanner('ILLEGAL FORWARD PASS — PAST LOS!', 1200);
        return;
      }

      sfx.playThrow();
      qb.hasBall = false;
      match.ballCarrier = null;
      match.phase = 'PASS_FLIGHT';
      updateMobileControls();

      // Lead receiver slightly along their route
      const leadX = Math.max(2, Math.min(118, recPlayer.x + dir * 2.4));
      const leadY = Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.5, Math.max(1.5, recPlayer.y));

      const dist = Math.hypot(leadX - qb.x, leadY - qb.y);
      const ballSpd = 25 + (match.qb.arm || 8) * 1.7;
      const flightTime = Math.max(0.24, Math.min(1.35, dist / ballSpd));

      match.ball = {
        startX: qb.x,
        startY: qb.y,
        x: qb.x,
        y: qb.y,
        targetX: leadX,
        targetY: leadY,
        targetId: recPlayer.id,
        elapsed: 0,
        duration: flightTime,
        arcHeight: Math.min(4.8, dist * 0.13)
      };
    }

    function throwToFieldPoint(fieldX, fieldY) {
      if (!match || match.phase !== 'PASS_DROPBACK') return;
      const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
      if (!qb || !qb.hasBall) return;
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;

      // Prevent illegal forward pass once QB scrambles past the line of scrimmage
      if ((qb.x - match.los) * dir > 0.5) {
        triggerBanner('ILLEGAL FORWARD PASS — PAST LOS!', 1200);
        return;
      }

      const eligibles = match.players.filter(p => p.side === 'OFF' && p.isEligible);
      if (!eligibles.length) return;

      // Throw directly to the exact spot the user clicked on the field so they lead the receiver!
      const targetX = Math.min(118, Math.max(4, fieldX));
      const targetY = Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.5, Math.max(1.5, fieldY));

      const dist = Math.hypot(targetX - qb.x, targetY - qb.y);
      const ballSpd = 25 + (match.qb.arm || 8) * 1.7;
      const flightTime = Math.max(0.24, Math.min(1.45, dist / ballSpd));

      // Pick the receiver who can best converge on the thrown spot (considering current pos + projected route)
      let bestRec = eligibles[0];
      let bestScore = Infinity;
      eligibles.forEach(r => {
        const curDist = Math.hypot(r.x - targetX, r.y - targetY);
        const fut = predictReceiverFuturePos(r, flightTime * 0.75);
        const futDist = Math.hypot(fut.x - targetX, fut.y - targetY);
        const score = Math.min(curDist, futDist * 0.85);
        if (score < bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          bestRec = r;
        }
      });

      sfx.playThrow();
      qb.hasBall = false;
      match.ballCarrier = null;
      match.phase = 'PASS_FLIGHT';
      updateMobileControls();

      match.ball = {
        startX: qb.x,
        startY: qb.y,
        x: qb.x,
        y: qb.y,
        targetX,
        targetY,
        targetId: bestRec.id,
        elapsed: 0,
        duration: flightTime,
        arcHeight: Math.min(5.2, dist * 0.14)
      };
    }

    function predictReceiverFuturePos(rec, lookaheadSec) {
      let simX = rec.x;
      let simY = rec.y;
      let rIdx = rec.routeIdx || 0;
      const spd = 7.2 + (rec.spd || 8) * 0.44;
      let rem = lookaheadSec;
      const step = 0.05;

      while (rem > 0) {
        const dt = Math.min(step, rem);
        rem -= dt;
        if (rec.waypoints && rec.waypoints.length) {
          const wp = rec.waypoints[Math.min(rIdx, rec.waypoints.length - 1)];
          const dx = wp.x - simX;
          const dy = wp.y - simY;
          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          if (dist > 0.6) {
            simX += (dx / dist) * spd * dt;
            simY += (dy / dist) * spd * dt;
          } else if (rIdx < rec.waypoints.length - 1) {
            rIdx += 1;
          } else {
            const dir = match ? (match.driveDir || 1) : 1;
            simX = Math.max(3, Math.min(117, simX + dir * spd * 0.65 * dt));
          }
        } else {
          const dir = match ? (match.driveDir || 1) : 1;
          simX = Math.max(5, Math.min(115, simX + dir * 5.2 * dt));
        }
      }
      return { x: simX, y: simY };
    }

    function openKickOverlay(isPat = false) {
      if (!match) return;
      match.phase = 'KICKING';
      document.getElementById('overlay-playcall').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('overlay-presnap').classList.add('hidden');
      document.getElementById('mobile-controls').classList.add('hidden');

      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const targetEz = dir === 1 ? 110 : 10;
      const dist = isPat ? 33 : Math.max(20, Math.round((targetEz - match.los) * dir + 17));
      match.kick = {
        pos: 10,
        dir: 1,
        speed: isPat ? 95 : Math.min(165, 95 + dist * 1.05),
        dist,
        isPat
      };

      document.getElementById('kick-title').textContent = isPat ? 'EXTRA POINT KICK' : 'FIELD GOAL ATTEMPT';
      document.getElementById('kick-subtitle').textContent = `DISTANCE: ${dist} YARDS • STOP IN GREEN ZONE`;
      document.getElementById('overlay-kick').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function lockKickAttempt() {
      if (!match || match.phase !== 'KICKING') return;
      document.getElementById('overlay-kick').classList.add('hidden');
      const k = match.kick;
      const offFromCenter = Math.abs(k.pos - 50);
      const made = offFromCenter <= 12;

      if (k.isPat) {
        if (made) {
          sfx.playCatch();
          addPoints('USER', 1);
          triggerBanner('EXTRA POINT GOOD! +1');
        } else {
          sfx.playTurnover();
          triggerBanner('EXTRA POINT MISSED!');
        }
        setTimeout(() => beginDefensiveSeries(), 1300);
      } else {
        if (made) {
          sfx.playTouchdown();
          addPoints('USER', 3);
          triggerBanner(`IT'S GOOD! ${k.dist} YD FG!`);
          setTimeout(() => beginDefensiveSeries(), 1400);
        } else {
          sfx.playTurnover();
          triggerBanner('FIELD GOAL NO GOOD!');
          // Missed FG: opponent takes over at spot of kick (7 yds behind LOS) driving the opposite direction
          const prevDir = match.driveDir || 1;
          const kickSpot = match.los - prevDir * 7;
          match.driveDir = -prevDir;
          match.los = Math.max(15, Math.min(105, kickSpot));
          match.firstDownLine = Math.max(10, Math.min(110, match.los + match.driveDir * 10));
          match.down = 1;
          setTimeout(() => beginDefensiveSeries(true), 1400);
        }
      }
    }

    function addPoints(side, pts) {
      if (!match) return;
      const qIdx = Math.min(3, match.quarter - 1);
      if (side === 'USER') {
        match.userScore += pts;
        match.stats.user.q[qIdx] += pts;
      } else {
        match.cpuScore += pts;
        match.stats.cpu.q[qIdx] += pts;
      }
      updateGameHUD();
    }

    function beginDefensiveSeries(keepSpot = false) {
      if (!match) return;
      if (checkQuarterEnd()) return;
      if (!keepSpot) {
        match.driveDir = -1; // CPU starts at right-hand 25 (x = 85) and drives left toward USER's end zone (x = 10)
        match.los = 85;
        match.firstDownLine = 75;
        match.down = 1;
      }
      openDefensivePlaycallOverlay();
    }

    function simulateCpuDrive() {
      if (!match) return;
      document.getElementById('overlay-def-playcall').classList.add('hidden');
      match.phase = 'SIM_DRIVE';

      const cpuOff = match.cpuRatings.off * match.diff.cpuOffMult;
      const userDef = match.userRatings.def;
      const diffEdge = cpuOff - userDef;
      const roll = Math.random() * 10 + diffEdge * 1.1;

      const simLog = document.getElementById('sim-log');
      const simTitle = document.getElementById('sim-title');
      simTitle.textContent = `${match.cpuTeam.city} ${match.cpuTeam.name} DRIVE`;

      match.clock = Math.max(0, match.clock - (28 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 26)));

      const cpuQbName = ((match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'QB') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} QB` }).name;
      const cpuWrName = ((match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'WR' || s.pos === 'TE') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} WR` }).name;
      const cpuRbName = ((match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'RB') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} RB` }).name;
      if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers) match.stats.cpuPlayers = { pass: {}, rush: {}, rec: {}, def: {} };
      const addCpuSimYds = (pYds, rYds, td = 0) => {
        if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers.pass[cpuQbName]) match.stats.cpuPlayers.pass[cpuQbName] = { comp: 0, att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0, int: 0 };
        if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers.rec[cpuWrName]) match.stats.cpuPlayers.rec[cpuWrName] = { catches: 0, yds: 0, td: 0 };
        if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers.rush[cpuRbName]) match.stats.cpuPlayers.rush[cpuRbName] = { att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0 };
        match.stats.cpuPlayers.pass[cpuQbName].yds += pYds;
        match.stats.cpuPlayers.pass[cpuQbName].td += td;
        match.stats.cpuPlayers.rec[cpuWrName].yds += Math.round(pYds * 0.65);
        match.stats.cpuPlayers.rush[cpuRbName].yds += rYds;
      };

      if (roll > 6.8) {
        addPoints('CPU', 7);
        match.stats.cpu.tds += 1;
        match.stats.cpu.passYds += 58;
        match.stats.cpu.rushYds += 22;
        addCpuSimYds(58, 22, 1);
        simLog.innerHTML = `<p class="text-rose-400">CPU MARCHES 75 YARDS FOR A TOUCHDOWN (+7)!</p><p>YOUR OFFENSE TAKES OVER AT THE 25.</p>`;
      } else if (roll > 4.2) {
        addPoints('CPU', 3);
        match.stats.cpu.passYds += 36;
        match.stats.cpu.rushYds += 14;
        addCpuSimYds(36, 14, 0);
        simLog.innerHTML = `<p class="text-amber-300">DEFENSE HOLDS IN THE RED ZONE! CPU KICKS FIELD GOAL (+3).</p>`;
      } else if (roll < 1.3) {
        match.stats.cpu.ints += 1;
        simLog.innerHTML = `<p class="text-emerald-400">INTERCEPTION BY YOUR SECONDARY! GREAT FIELD POSITION!</p>`;
      } else {
        match.stats.cpu.passYds += 14;
        addCpuSimYds(14, 0, 0);
        simLog.innerHTML = `<p class="text-sky-300">3-AND-OUT! DEFENSE FORCES A CPU PUNT.</p>`;
      }

      updateGameHUD();
      document.getElementById('overlay-sim').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function checkQuarterEnd() {
      if (!match) return false;
      if (match.clock > 0) return false;

      if (match.quarter < 4) {
        const endedQ = match.quarter;
        match.quarter += 1;
        match.clock = 120;
        updateGameHUD();
        if (endedQ === 2) {
          match.phase = 'HALFTIME';
          document.getElementById('sim-title').textContent = 'HALFTIME REPORT';
          document.getElementById('sim-log').innerHTML = `
            <p class="text-yellow-300">${match.userTeam.id} ${match.userScore} — ${match.cpuScore} ${match.cpuTeam.id}</p>
            <p class="text-emerald-400">2ND HALF KICKOFF COMING UP!</p>
          `;
          document.getElementById('overlay-sim').classList.remove('hidden');
          return true;
        }
        triggerBanner(`END OF Q${endedQ}`);
        return false;
      }

      finishMatch();
      return true;
    }

    function finishMatch() {
      if (!match) return;
      match.phase = 'POSTGAME';
      ['overlay-playcall', 'overlay-def-playcall', 'overlay-presnap', 'overlay-def-presnap', 'overlay-kick', 'overlay-sim', 'mobile-controls'].forEach(id => {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) el.classList.add('hidden');
      });

      const won = match.userScore >= match.cpuScore;
      if (won) sfx.playTouchdown();
      else sfx.playTurnover();

      document.getElementById('postgame-result').textContent = won ? 'VICTORY!' : 'FINAL SCORE';
      document.getElementById('postgame-score').textContent =
        `${match.userTeam.id} ${match.userScore} — ${match.cpuScore} ${match.cpuTeam.id}`;

      const s = match.stats;
      const qbStats = (s.players.pass && s.players.pass[match.qb.name]) || { td: 0, int: s.user.ints || 0 };
      document.getElementById('postgame-boxscore').innerHTML = `
        <div class="text-yellow-300">FINAL STATS SUMMARY</div>
        <div>PASSING: ${s.user.passComp}/${s.user.passAtt}, ${s.user.passYds} YDS, ${qbStats.td || 0} TD, ${qbStats.int ?? (s.user.ints || 0)} INT</div>
        <div>RUSHING: ${s.user.rushAtt} ATT, ${s.user.rushYds} YDS</div>
        <div>DEFENSE: ${s.user.sacks} SACKS, ${s.cpu.ints} INTS</div>
      `;

      const rewardsEl = document.getElementById('postgame-rewards');
      if (!match.isQuickPlay && career) {
        const ccEarned = won ? (2 + getCombinedFacilityBonuses().winCcBonus) : 1;
        career.cc += ccEarned;
        recordMatchResultToCareer(match.userScore, match.cpuScore, match.cpuTeam.id, match.weekNum, match.poMeta, match.userIsHome);
        rewardsEl.innerHTML = `
          <div class="text-emerald-400">FRANCHISE PROGRESS SAVED!</div>
          <div>COACHING CREDITS EARNED: <span class="text-yellow-300">+${ccEarned} CC</span></div>
          <div>SEASON RECORD UPDATED IN STANDINGS</div>
        `;
      } else {
        rewardsEl.innerHTML = `<div class="text-sky-300">EXHIBITION QUICK PLAY COMPLETE!</div>`;
      }

      document.getElementById('overlay-postgame').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function resolveEndOfPlay(endX, reason = 'TACKLED') {
      if (!match || match.phase === 'PLAY_OVER') return;
      match.phase = 'PLAY_OVER';
      match.ball = null;
      updateMobileControls();

      const offSide = match.possession;
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const clampedX = Math.max(5, Math.min(115, endX));
      const gained = Math.round((clampedX - match.los) * dir);

      // Update yardage stats
      if (reason !== 'INCOMPLETE' && reason !== 'INTERCEPTION' && reason !== 'FUMBLE') {
        if (match.currentPlay && match.currentPlay.type === 'PASS' && !match.qbScrambleMode) {
          if (offSide === 'USER') {
            match.stats.user.passYds += gained;
            const qName = match.qb.name;
            if (match.stats.players.pass[qName]) match.stats.players.pass[qName].yds += gained;
            const recName = match.lastReceiverName || match.wr1.name;
            if (match.stats.players.rec[recName]) match.stats.players.rec[recName].yds += gained;
          } else {
            match.stats.cpu.passYds += gained;
            if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers) match.stats.cpuPlayers = { pass: {}, rush: {}, rec: {}, def: {} };
            const cpuQbName = ((match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'QB') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} QB` }).name;
            const cpuWrName = ((match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'WR' || s.pos === 'TE') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} WR` }).name;
            if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers.pass[cpuQbName]) match.stats.cpuPlayers.pass[cpuQbName] = { comp: 0, att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0, int: 0 };
            if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers.rec[cpuWrName]) match.stats.cpuPlayers.rec[cpuWrName] = { catches: 0, yds: 0, td: 0 };
            match.stats.cpuPlayers.pass[cpuQbName].yds += gained;
            match.stats.cpuPlayers.rec[cpuWrName].yds += gained;
          }
        } else {
          if (offSide === 'USER') {
            match.stats.user.rushYds += gained;
            const rName = match.qbScrambleMode ? match.qb.name : match.rb.name;
            if (match.stats.players.rush[rName]) match.stats.players.rush[rName].yds += gained;
          } else {
            match.stats.cpu.rushYds += gained;
            if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers) match.stats.cpuPlayers = { pass: {}, rush: {}, rec: {}, def: {} };
            const cpuRbName = ((match.cpuTeam.stars || []).find(s => s.pos === 'RB') || { name: `${match.cpuTeam.id} RB` }).name;
            if (!match.stats.cpuPlayers.rush[cpuRbName]) match.stats.cpuPlayers.rush[cpuRbName] = { att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0 };
            match.stats.cpuPlayers.rush[cpuRbName].yds += gained;
          }
        }
        updateLiveHudStatTrackers();
      }

      if (reason === 'SACK') {
        if (offSide === 'USER') match.stats.cpu.sacks += 1;
        else match.stats.user.sacks += 1;
      }

      // Check Touchdown
      const reachedEz = dir === 1 ? clampedX >= 110 : clampedX <= 10;
      if (reachedEz && reason !== 'INCOMPLETE' && reason !== 'INTERCEPTION' && reason !== 'FUMBLE') {
        sfx.playTouchdown();
        if (offSide === 'USER') {
          addPoints('USER', 6);
          match.stats.user.tds += 1;
          if (match.currentPlay && match.currentPlay.type === 'PASS' && !match.qbScrambleMode) {
            const qName = match.qb.name;
            if (match.stats.players.pass[qName]) match.stats.players.pass[qName].td += 1;
            const recName = match.lastReceiverName || match.wr1.name;
            if (match.stats.players.rec[recName]) match.stats.players.rec[recName].td += 1;
          } else {
            const rName = match.qbScrambleMode ? match.qb.name : match.rb.name;
            if (match.stats.players.rush[rName]) match.stats.players.rush[rName].td += 1;
          }
          triggerBanner('TOUCHDOWN!! +6');
          setTimeout(() => openKickOverlay(true), 1300);
        } else {
          addPoints('CPU', 7);
          match.stats.cpu.tds += 1;
          triggerBanner('CPU TOUCHDOWN!');
          setTimeout(() => {
            if (checkQuarterEnd()) return;
            match.driveDir = 1;
            match.los = 35;
            match.firstDownLine = 45;
            match.down = 1;
            openPlaycallOverlay();
          }, 1500);
        }
        return;
      }

      // Check Interception or Fumble — keep exact field spot and reverse driving direction!
      if (reason === 'INTERCEPTION' || reason === 'FUMBLE') {
        sfx.playTurnover();
        const spot = Math.max(15, Math.min(105, clampedX));
        const nextDir = -dir;
        if (offSide === 'USER') {
          if (reason === 'INTERCEPTION') {
            match.stats.user.ints += 1;
            const qName = match.qb.name;
            if (match.stats.players.pass[qName]) match.stats.players.pass[qName].int += 1;
          } else {
            match.stats.user.fumbles += 1;
          }
          triggerBanner(reason === 'INTERCEPTION' ? 'INTERCEPTED!' : 'FUMBLE LOST!');
          match.driveDir = nextDir;
          match.los = spot;
          match.firstDownLine = Math.max(10, Math.min(110, match.los + nextDir * 10));
          match.down = 1;
          setTimeout(() => beginDefensiveSeries(true), 1400);
        } else {
          if (reason === 'INTERCEPTION') match.stats.cpu.ints += 1;
          else match.stats.cpu.fumbles += 1;
          triggerBanner(reason === 'INTERCEPTION' ? 'INTERCEPTION! YOUR BALL!' : 'FUMBLE RECOVERED!');
          match.driveDir = nextDir;
          match.los = spot;
          match.firstDownLine = Math.max(10, Math.min(110, match.los + nextDir * 10));
          match.down = 1;
          setTimeout(() => openPlaycallOverlay(), 1400);
        }
        return;
      }

      // Advance spot & check first down
      if (reason !== 'INCOMPLETE') {
        match.los = clampedX;
      }

      const gotFirstDown = dir === 1 ? match.los >= match.firstDownLine : match.los <= match.firstDownLine;
      if (gotFirstDown && reason !== 'INCOMPLETE') {
        match.down = 1;
        match.firstDownLine = Math.max(10, Math.min(110, match.los + dir * 10));
        triggerBanner('FIRST DOWN!', 1100);
      } else {
        match.down += 1;
        if (reason === 'INCOMPLETE') triggerBanner('INCOMPLETE PASS', 950);
        else if (reason === 'SACK') triggerBanner(`SACKED! (${gained} YDS)`, 1050);
        else triggerBanner(`${gained >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${gained} YARDS`, 900);
      }

      // Check Turnover on Downs — keep exact spot and flip driving direction!
      if (match.down > 4) {
        sfx.playTurnover();
        triggerBanner('TURNOVER ON DOWNS!');
        const nextDir = -dir;
        match.driveDir = nextDir;
        match.los = Math.max(15, Math.min(105, match.los));
        match.firstDownLine = Math.max(10, Math.min(110, match.los + nextDir * 10));
        match.down = 1;
        setTimeout(() => {
          if (checkQuarterEnd()) return;
          if (offSide === 'USER') beginDefensiveSeries(true);
          else openPlaycallOverlay();
        }, 1350);
        return;
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        if (checkQuarterEnd()) return;
        if (offSide === 'USER') openPlaycallOverlay();
        else openDefensivePlaycallOverlay();
      }, 1150);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       9. LIVE SIMULATION STEP (AI, ROUTES, PASSING, TACKLING)
       ========================================================= */
    function updateMatch(dt) {
      if (!match) return;

      // Kick meter oscillation
      if (match.phase === 'KICKING') {
        const k = match.kick;
        k.pos += k.dir * k.speed * dt;
        if (k.pos >= 94) { k.pos = 94; k.dir = -1; }
        if (k.pos <= 6)  { k.pos = 6;  k.dir = 1; }
        const curEl = document.getElementById('kick-cursor');
        if (curEl) curEl.style.left = `${k.pos}%`;
        return;
      }

      const activePhases = ['PASS_DROPBACK', 'PASS_FLIGHT', 'RUN_PLAY', 'DEF_LIVE'];
      if (!activePhases.includes(match.phase)) return;

      // Tick game clock
      match.clock = Math.max(0, match.clock - dt * 1.45);
      match.playClock += dt;
      updateGameHUD();

      const turboActive = Boolean(keys['ShiftLeft'] || keys['ShiftRight'] || keys['TURBO']);
      const staminaCostMult = Math.max(0.75, 1 - getCombinedFacilityBonuses().staminaReduction);
      if (turboActive && match.stamina > 0) {
        match.stamina = Math.max(0, match.stamina - 22 * staminaCostMult * dt);
      } else {
        match.stamina = Math.min(100, match.stamina + 14 * dt);
      }
      const hasTurbo = turboActive && match.stamina > 3;

      // Determine user-controlled player
      let userCtrl = null;
      if (match.possession === 'USER') {
        userCtrl = match.ballCarrier;
      } else if (match.possession === 'CPU') {
        userCtrl = match.players.find(p => p.id === match.selectedDefenderId) || match.players.find(p => p.side === 'DEF');
      }

      // Move user-controlled player via WASD / Arrows
      if (userCtrl) {
        let vx = 0, vy = 0;
        if (keys['KeyW'] || keys['ArrowUp']) vy -= 1;
        if (keys['KeyS'] || keys['ArrowDown']) vy += 1;
        if (keys['KeyA'] || keys['ArrowLeft']) vx -= 1;
        if (keys['KeyD'] || keys['ArrowRight']) vx += 1;

        if (vx !== 0 || vy !== 0) {
          const len = Math.hypot(vx, vy);
          vx /= len; vy /= len;
          const mult = hasTurbo ? 1.28 : 1.0;
          const spd = (6.8 + (userCtrl.spd || 7) * 0.42) * mult;
          userCtrl.x = Math.max(2, Math.min(118, userCtrl.x + vx * spd * dt));
          userCtrl.y = Math.max(1.2, Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.2, userCtrl.y + vy * spd * dt));
        }
        if (userCtrl.jukeTimer > 0) userCtrl.jukeTimer = Math.max(0, userCtrl.jukeTimer - dt);
      }

      // Update Offensive AI players
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      match.players.forEach(p => {
        if (p.side !== 'OFF') return;
        if (p === userCtrl) return;

        // CPU QB dropback & pass decision when user is on Defense
        if (match.possession === 'CPU' && p.id === 'QB' && p.hasBall) {
          if ((match.los - p.x) * dir < 6.2) {
            p.x -= dir * 4.2 * dt;
          }
          match.cpuThrowTimer -= dt;
          if (match.cpuThrowTimer <= 0) {
            const elig = match.players.filter(r => r.side === 'OFF' && r.isEligible);
            const target = elig[Math.floor(Math.random() * elig.length)];
            if (target) {
              sfx.playThrow();
              p.hasBall = false;
              match.ballCarrier = null;
              match.phase = 'PASS_FLIGHT';
              const dist = Math.hypot(target.x + dir * 2 - p.x, target.y - p.y);
              match.ball = {
                startX: p.x, startY: p.y, x: p.x, y: p.y,
                targetX: Math.max(3, Math.min(117, target.x + dir * 2.2)),
                targetY: target.y,
                targetId: target.id,
                elapsed: 0,
                duration: Math.max(0.28, Math.min(1.25, dist / 28)),
                arcHeight: Math.min(4.2, dist * 0.12)
              };
            }
          }
          return;
        }

        // CPU RB running with the ball
        if (match.possession === 'CPU' && p.hasBall) {
          const runDir = (match.currentPlay && match.currentPlay.runDir) || 0;
          const targetY = Math.max(4, Math.min(FIELD_H - 4, FIELD_H / 2 + runDir * 14));
          const dy = targetY - p.y;
          const vy = Math.abs(dy) > 1 ? Math.sign(dy) * 4.5 : 0;
          const spd = 7.4 + match.cpuRatings.off * 0.48;
          p.x = Math.max(4, Math.min(116, p.x + dir * spd * dt));
          p.y = Math.max(1.5, Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.5, p.y + vy * dt));
          return;
        }

        // Offensive Line blocking nearest pass rusher
        if (p.role === 'OL') {
          let nearestDef = null;
          let minD = 999;
          match.players.forEach(d => {
            if (d.side === 'DEF' && (d.role === 'DL' || d.assignment === 'BLITZ' || d.assignment === 'RUSH' || d.assignment === 'CONTAIN_EDGE')) {
              const dist = Math.hypot(d.x - p.x, d.y - p.y);
              if (dist < minD) {
                minD = dist;
                nearestDef = d;
              }
            }
          });
          if (nearestDef && minD < 10) {
            const dx = nearestDef.x - p.x;
            const dy = nearestDef.y - p.y;
            const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
            const qb = match.players.find(q => q.id === 'QB');
            // Form a curved pass-protection pocket cup 2.6-3.4 yards in front of the QB
            const targetPocketX = qb
              ? (dir === 1
                  ? Math.min(match.los + 0.2, Math.max(qb.x + 2.75, match.los - 1.6))
                  : Math.max(match.los - 0.2, Math.min(qb.x - 2.75, match.los + 1.6)))
              : p.startX - dir * 0.8;
            if (dist > 0.95) {
              // Kick-slide smoothly into pass-pro set while mirroring rusher's lateral path
              p.x += (targetPocketX - p.x) * 4.2 * dt;
              p.y += (dy / dist) * p.spd * 1.2 * dt;
            } else {
              // Active trench battle: OL holds the pocket wall cleanly for the first 1.8s, then gives ground naturally
              const trenchOsc = Math.sin(match.playClock * 8.0 + p.startY) * 0.22;
              const holdWindow = 1.85;
              if (match.playClock < holdWindow) {
                // Anchor the pocket wall at least 2.6 yards ahead of the QB so rushers engage the OL, not the QB
                const wallX = qb ? (dir === 1 ? Math.max(p.x, qb.x + 2.65) : Math.min(p.x, qb.x - 2.65)) : p.x;
                p.x += (wallX - p.x) * 6.0 * dt;
                const targetY = qb ? p.y + (qb.y - p.y) * 0.08 + trenchOsc * 0.12 : p.y;
                p.y += (targetY - p.y) * 3.5 * dt;
                nearestDef.x = dir === 1 ? Math.max(nearestDef.x, p.x + 0.78) : Math.min(nearestDef.x, p.x - 0.78);
                nearestDef.y += (p.y + trenchOsc * 0.25 - nearestDef.y) * 5.0 * dt;
              } else if (match.playClock < 2.65) {
                // Gradual bull-rush pushback between 1.85s and 2.65s
                const pushBackSpeed = 1.35 * (match.possession === 'USER' ? match.diff.passRushMult : 1.0);
                p.x -= dir * pushBackSpeed * dt;
                nearestDef.x = p.x + dir * 0.75;
                nearestDef.y += (p.y + trenchOsc * 0.3 - nearestDef.y) * 5.0 * dt;
              } else {
                // Rusher sheds block cleanly after 2.65s and swims past the blocker
                const swimDir = nearestDef.y >= p.y ? 1 : -1;
                nearestDef.y += swimDir * 2.5 * dt;
              }
            }
          }
          return;
        }

        // Downfield & perimeter lead blocking whenever a ball carrier is advancing (Run, Scramble, or After Catch)
        const activeCarrier = match.ballCarrier;
        const isLiveBallAdvance = activeCarrier && (
          match.phase === 'RUN_PLAY' ||
          (match.phase === 'DEF_LIVE' && match.currentPlay && match.currentPlay.type === 'RUN') ||
          (activeCarrier.id !== 'QB')
        );

        if (isLiveBallAdvance && p !== activeCarrier && p.id !== 'QB') {
          let threatDef = null;
          let bestThreatScore = Infinity;

          match.players.forEach(d => {
            if (d.side !== 'DEF') return;
            const distToCarrier = Math.hypot(d.x - activeCarrier.x, d.y - activeCarrier.y);
            const distToBlocker = Math.hypot(d.x - p.x, d.y - p.y);
            // Target defenders within 16 yards of the ball carrier (prioritizing defenders in front of the carrier)
            const inFrontBonus = (d.x - activeCarrier.x) * dir >= -2.5 ? -3.0 : 1.5;
            if (distToCarrier < 16.5 && distToBlocker < 18.0) {
              const score = distToCarrier * 0.65 + distToBlocker * 0.45 + inFrontBonus;
              if (score < bestThreatScore) {
                bestThreatScore = score;
                threatDef = d;
              }
            }
          });

          if (threatDef) {
            const dx = threatDef.x - p.x;
            const dy = threatDef.y - p.y;
            const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
            const blockSpd = (7.4 + (p.spd || 7) * 0.45) * 1.12;

            if (dist > 1.05) {
              // Sprint into blocking position slightly on the ball carrier's side of the defender
              const shieldX = threatDef.x - dir * 0.45;
              const shieldY = threatDef.y + Math.sign(activeCarrier.y - threatDef.y) * 0.25;
              const sdx = shieldX - p.x;
              const sdy = shieldY - p.y;
              const sdist = Math.max(0.1, Math.hypot(sdx, sdy));
              p.x = Math.max(2, Math.min(118, p.x + (sdx / sdist) * blockSpd * dt));
              p.y = Math.max(1.2, Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.2, p.y + (sdy / sdist) * blockSpd * dt));
            } else {
              // Engaged downfield block: seal & drive defender away from the ball carrier's lane
              const pushX = dir * 1.65;
              const awayY = Math.sign(threatDef.y - activeCarrier.y || 1) * 1.35;
              threatDef.x = Math.max(2, Math.min(118, threatDef.x + pushX * dt));
              threatDef.y = Math.max(1.2, Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.2, threatDef.y + awayY * dt));
              p.x = Math.max(2, Math.min(118, threatDef.x - dir * 0.82));
              p.y += (threatDef.y - p.y) * 6.0 * dt;
            }
            return;
          } else {
            // Escort ahead of the ball carrier downfield looking for incoming pursuit
            const leadSpotX = Math.max(4, Math.min(116, activeCarrier.x + dir * 4.5));
            const dx = leadSpotX - p.x;
            const dy = (activeCarrier.y + (p.startY - FIELD_H / 2) * 0.35) - p.y;
            const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
            if (dist > 0.8) {
              const escortSpd = 7.4 + (p.spd || 7) * 0.42;
              p.x = Math.max(3, Math.min(117, p.x + (dx / dist) * escortSpd * dt));
              p.y = Math.max(1.2, Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.2, p.y + (dy / dist) * escortSpd * dt));
            }
            return;
          }
        }

        // Eligible Receivers running routes (or tracking thrown pass spot in mid-air!)
        if (match.phase === 'PASS_FLIGHT' && match.ball && p.isEligible) {
          const distToSpot = Math.hypot(match.ball.targetX - p.x, match.ball.targetY - p.y);
          // Target receiver (or any nearby receiver within 11 yds) breaks toward the thrown spot
          if (p.id === match.ball.targetId || distToSpot < 11) {
            const dx = match.ball.targetX - p.x;
            const dy = match.ball.targetY - p.y;
            const spd = (7.4 + (p.spd || 8) * 0.46) * (p.id === match.ball.targetId ? 1.08 : 1.0);
            if (distToSpot > 0.35) {
              p.x = Math.min(118, Math.max(2, p.x + (dx / distToSpot) * spd * dt));
              p.y = Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.2, Math.max(1.2, p.y + (dy / distToSpot) * spd * dt));
            }
            return;
          }
        }

        if (match.currentPlay && match.currentPlay.type === 'PASS' && p.waypoints && p.waypoints.length) {
          const wp = p.waypoints[Math.min(p.routeIdx, p.waypoints.length - 1)];
          const dx = wp.x - p.x;
          const dy = wp.y - p.y;
          const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
          const spd = 7.2 + (p.spd || 8) * 0.44;
          if (dist > 0.6) {
            p.x += (dx / dist) * spd * dt;
            p.y += (dy / dist) * spd * dt;
          } else if (p.routeIdx < p.waypoints.length - 1) {
            p.routeIdx += 1;
          } else {
            p.x = Math.max(3, Math.min(117, p.x + dir * spd * 0.65 * dt));
          }
        } else {
          // Run-play downfield stalk blocking
          p.x = Math.max(5, Math.min(115, p.x + dir * 5.2 * dt));
        }
      });

      // Update Ball Flight & Catch / Interception Resolution
      if (match.phase === 'PASS_FLIGHT' && match.ball) {
        const b = match.ball;
        b.elapsed += dt;
        const t = Math.min(1, b.elapsed / b.duration);
        b.x = b.startX + (b.targetX - b.startX) * t;
        b.y = b.startY + (b.targetY - b.startY) * t;

        if (t >= 1) {
          // Check defenders near landing spot for INT or PBU
          let closestDef = null;
          let defDist = 999;
          match.players.forEach(d => {
            if (d.side === 'DEF') {
              const dist = Math.hypot(d.x - b.x, d.y - b.y);
              if (dist < defDist) { defDist = dist; closestDef = d; }
            }
          });

          // Check nearest eligible receiver near landing spot
          let closestRec = null;
          let recDist = 999;
          match.players.forEach(r => {
            if (r.side === 'OFF' && r.isEligible) {
              const dist = Math.hypot(r.x - b.x, r.y - b.y);
              if (dist < recDist) { recDist = dist; closestRec = r; }
            }
          });

          const intOdds = (defDist < 1.65 && defDist < recDist + 0.15)
            ? 0.24 * match.diff.intChanceMult
            : 0;

          if (closestDef && Math.random() < intOdds) {
            if (match.possession === 'CPU') {
              const dName = closestDef.name || 'DB';
              if (!match.stats.players.def[dName]) match.stats.players.def[dName] = { tkl: 0, sck: 0, int: 0 };
              match.stats.players.def[dName].int += 1;
            } else {
              const qName = match.qb.name;
              if (!match.stats.players.pass[qName]) match.stats.players.pass[qName] = { comp: 0, att: 0, yds: 0, td: 0, int: 0 };
              match.stats.players.pass[qName].int += 1;
            }
            resolveEndOfPlay(b.x, 'INTERCEPTION');
            return;
          }

          if (closestRec && recDist <= 3.35 && !(defDist < 1.35 && Math.random() < 0.34)) {
            sfx.playCatch();
            closestRec.hasBall = true;
            match.ballCarrier = closestRec;
            match.ball = null;
            match.phase = match.possession === 'USER' ? 'RUN_PLAY' : 'DEF_LIVE';
            if (match.possession === 'USER') {
              match.stats.user.passComp += 1;
              const qName = match.qb.name;
              if (match.stats.players.pass[qName]) match.stats.players.pass[qName].comp += 1;
              const recName = closestRec.name;
              match.lastReceiverName = recName;
              if (!match.stats.players.rec[recName]) match.stats.players.rec[recName] = { catches: 0, yds: 0, td: 0 };
              match.stats.players.rec[recName].catches += 1;
            } else {
              match.stats.cpu.passComp += 1;
            }
            updateMobileControls();
          } else {
            resolveEndOfPlay(match.los, 'INCOMPLETE');
            return;
          }
        }
      }

      // Update Defensive AI players
      const carrier = match.ballCarrier;
      match.players.forEach(d => {
        if (d.side !== 'DEF') return;
        if (d === userCtrl) return;
        if (d.diveTimer > 0) d.diveTimer = Math.max(0, d.diveTimer - dt);

        const spdMult = match.possession === 'USER' ? match.diff.defSpeedMult : 1.05;
        const rushMult = match.possession === 'USER' ? match.diff.passRushMult : 1.05;
        let spd = (d.spd || 7.2) * spdMult;
        let tx = d.x;
        let ty = d.y;
        const isBallPastLos = carrier && (carrier.x - match.los) * dir > 0.8;
        const isRunOrAfterCatch = match.phase === 'RUN_PLAY' || (match.phase === 'DEF_LIVE' && match.currentPlay?.type === 'RUN') || isBallPastLos;

        if (isRunOrAfterCatch && carrier) {
          // Calculate realistic pursuit angle ahead of the ball carrier's path
          const carrierLeadX = (carrier.x - d.x) * dir > 0
            ? Math.max(10, Math.min(110, carrier.x + dir * 3.6))
            : carrier.x;
          const lateralPull = (carrier.y - d.y) * 0.22;
          tx = carrierLeadX;
          ty = Math.max(1.2, Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.2, carrier.y + lateralPull));

          // Open-field pursuit burst for DBs/LBs chasing a breakaway runner downfield
          const distToCarrier = Math.hypot(carrier.x - d.x, carrier.y - d.y);
