          const isOpenFieldChase = (carrier.x - match.los) * dir > 3.0 && distToCarrier > 2.6;
          if (isOpenFieldChase) {
            const roleChaseMult = d.role === 'DB' ? 1.29 : d.role === 'LB' ? 1.23 : 1.12;
            spd *= roleChaseMult;
          } else {
            spd *= 1.13;
          }
        } else if (d.assignment === 'RUSH' || d.assignment === 'BLITZ') {
          const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
          if (qb) {
            // Dynamic rush lane: wide edge rushers arc around the tackle before closing on QB
            const isEdge = d.id === 'DL_L' || d.id === 'DL_R';
            const edgeArcY = isEdge && match.playClock < 1.55 ? (d.id === 'DL_L' ? -2.8 : 2.8) : 0;
            const swimJitter = Math.sin(match.playClock * 9.5 + (d.y || 0)) * 0.45;
            // Aim at the pocket perimeter (~2.8 yds ahead of QB) during the first 1.6s so rushers engage OL cleanly
            const pocketBarrierX = qb.x + dir * 2.85;
            tx = match.playClock < 1.6
              ? (dir === 1 ? Math.max(qb.x + 2.5, pocketBarrierX) : Math.min(qb.x - 2.5, pocketBarrierX))
              : qb.x;
            ty = Math.max(1.5, Math.min(FIELD_H - 1.5, qb.y + edgeArcY + swimJitter));
            // Controlled rush pacing so defenders cannot reach the QB within the first ~1.8 seconds
            const burstMult = match.playClock < 1.6 ? 0.84 : 1.02;
            spd *= burstMult * rushMult;
          }
        } else if (d.assignment === 'CONTAIN_EDGE') {
          const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
          if (qb) {
            const edgeOffsetY = d.id === 'DL_L' ? -5.0 : 5.0;
            tx = dir === 1 ? Math.max(qb.x - 0.3, match.los - 2.4) : Math.min(qb.x + 0.3, match.los + 2.4);
            ty = Math.max(2, Math.min(FIELD_H - 2, qb.y + edgeOffsetY));
            spd *= 1.02 * rushMult;
          }
        } else if (d.assignment === 'SPY_QB') {
          const qb = match.players.find(p => p.id === 'QB');
          if (qb) {
            tx = match.los + dir * 3.2;
            ty = qb.y;
            spd *= 1.08;
          }
        } else if (d.assignment && d.assignment.startsWith('MAN_')) {
          const targetId = d.assignment.replace('MAN_', '');
          const rec = match.players.find(p => p.id === targetId);
          if (rec) {
            // Mirror receiver's vertical momentum & maintain a deep over-top cushion against streaks
            const isDeepStem = (rec.x - match.los) * dir > 6.5;
            const cushionX = isDeepStem ? 1.85 : 0.95;
            tx = Math.max(3, Math.min(117, rec.x + dir * cushionX));
            ty = rec.y + (FIELD_H / 2 - rec.y) * 0.06;
            spd *= isDeepStem ? 1.18 : 1.09;
          }
        } else {
          // Zone coverage drops + pattern-matching vertical threats + ball-hawking break on passes
          const midY = FIELD_H / 2;
          const zoneMap = {
            HOOK_MID:       { x: match.los + dir * 6.5,  y: midY - 3 },
            HOOK_WEAK:      { x: match.los + dir * 6.5,  y: midY + 5 },
            CURL_FLAT:      { x: match.los + dir * 5.5,  y: midY + 13 },
            FLAT_TOP:       { x: match.los + dir * 4.5,  y: 7 },
            FLAT_BOT:       { x: match.los + dir * 4.5,  y: FIELD_H - 7 },
            BOX_RUN:        { x: match.los + dir * 4.0,  y: midY + 2 },
            DEEP_HALF_TOP:  { x: match.los + dir * 18.0, y: FIELD_H * 0.26 },
            DEEP_HALF_BOT:  { x: match.los + dir * 18.0, y: FIELD_H * 0.74 },
            DEEP_THIRD_TOP: { x: match.los + dir * 18.0, y: FIELD_H * 0.18 },
            DEEP_THIRD_MID: { x: match.los + dir * 19.5, y: midY },
            DEEP_THIRD_BOT: { x: match.los + dir * 18.0, y: FIELD_H * 0.82 },
            DEEP_QTR_1:     { x: match.los + dir * 18.5, y: FIELD_H * 0.14 },
            DEEP_QTR_2:     { x: match.los + dir * 19.5, y: FIELD_H * 0.38 },
            DEEP_QTR_3:     { x: match.los + dir * 19.5, y: FIELD_H * 0.62 },
            DEEP_QTR_4:     { x: match.los + dir * 18.5, y: FIELD_H * 0.86 }
          };
          const z = zoneMap[d.assignment] || { x: match.los + dir * 8, y: d.y };
          tx = Math.max(3, Math.min(117, z.x));
          ty = z.y;

          // Deep-zone DBs & safeties carry vertical receivers pushing past their landmark
          const isDeepZone = d.assignment && d.assignment.startsWith('DEEP_');
          if (isDeepZone) {
            let deepestThreat = null;
            let threatScore = Infinity;
            match.players.forEach(r => {
              if (r.side === 'OFF' && r.isEligible && (r.x - match.los) * dir > 7.5) {
                const latDiff = Math.abs(r.y - z.y);
                const maxLateralSpan = d.assignment.startsWith('DEEP_HALF') ? 16.5 : 13.0;
                if (latDiff < maxLateralSpan && latDiff < threatScore) {
                  threatScore = latDiff;
                  deepestThreat = r;
                }
              }
            });
            if (deepestThreat) {
              const carryX = deepestThreat.x + dir * 2.2;
              tx = Math.max(3, Math.min(117, dir === 1 ? Math.max(tx, carryX) : Math.min(tx, carryX)));
              ty = deepestThreat.y + (z.y - deepestThreat.y) * 0.25;
              spd *= 1.16;
            }
          }

          // Zone defenders react & break on the ball once thrown (balanced reaction delay & radius)
          if (match.phase === 'PASS_FLIGHT' && match.ball && match.ball.elapsed > 0.16) {
            const distToTarget = Math.hypot(match.ball.targetX - d.x, match.ball.targetY - d.y);
            const reactRadius = isDeepZone ? 16.5 : 10.5;
            if (distToTarget < reactRadius) {
              tx = match.ball.targetX;
              ty = match.ball.targetY;
              spd *= isDeepZone ? 1.02 : 0.96;
            }
          }
        }

        // Also allow man-coverage DBs to break toward the ball's landing spot in flight after brief reaction delay
        if (match.phase === 'PASS_FLIGHT' && match.ball && match.ball.elapsed > 0.14 && d.assignment && d.assignment.startsWith('MAN_')) {
          const distToTarget = Math.hypot(match.ball.targetX - d.x, match.ball.targetY - d.y);
          if (distToTarget < 12.0) {
            tx = match.ball.targetX;
            ty = match.ball.targetY;
            spd *= 0.98;
          }
        }

        const dx = tx - d.x;
        const dy = ty - d.y;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (dist > 0.35) {
          d.x += (dx / dist) * spd * dt;
          d.y += (dy / dist) * spd * dt;
        }
      });

      // Check Sideline Out-of-Bounds or End Zone Touchdown for Ball Carrier
      if (carrier) {
        if (carrier.y <= 0.7 || carrier.y >= FIELD_H - 0.7) {
          sfx.playWhistle();
          resolveEndOfPlay(carrier.x, 'OUT_OF_BOUNDS');
          return;
        }
        const carrierReachedEz = dir === 1 ? carrier.x >= 110 : carrier.x <= 10;
        if (carrierReachedEz) {
          resolveEndOfPlay(dir === 1 ? 110 : 10, 'TOUCHDOWN');
          return;
        }

        // Check Tackle Collisions
        for (const d of match.players) {
          if (d.side !== 'DEF') continue;
          const dist = Math.hypot(d.x - carrier.x, d.y - carrier.y);
          const tackleRadius = d.diveTimer > 0 ? 1.85 : 1.18;
          if (dist <= tackleRadius) {
            const isBehindLos = (carrier.x - match.los) * dir < 0;
            const isPotentialSack = (match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK' || (match.possession === 'CPU' && carrier.id === 'QB')) && isBehindLos;
            // Smooth pocket barrier during the first 1.65s so unblocked blitzers engage at pocket depth instead of jittering on the QB
            if (isPotentialSack && match.playClock < 1.65) {
              const pushOutX = carrier.x + dir * (tackleRadius + 1.35);
              d.x += (pushOutX - d.x) * 0.35;
              continue;
            }
            if (carrier.jukeTimer > 0) {
              // Broken tackle via juke!
              carrier.jukeTimer = 0;
              d.x -= dir * 1.6;
              triggerBanner('BROKEN TACKLE!', 850);
              continue;
            }
            sfx.playTackle();
            if (match.possession === 'CPU') {
              const dName = d.name || 'DEF';
              if (!match.stats.players.def[dName]) match.stats.players.def[dName] = { tkl: 0, sck: 0, int: 0 };
              match.stats.players.def[dName].tkl += 1;
              if (isBehindLos) match.stats.players.def[dName].sck += 1;
            }
            resolveEndOfPlay(carrier.x, isPotentialSack ? 'SACK' : 'TACKLED');
            return;
          }
        }
      }
    }

    /* =========================================================
       10. RETRO 16-BIT CANVAS RENDERER (STADIUM, ROUTES, PLAYERS)
       ========================================================= */
    function renderMatch() {
      const W = canvas.width;
      const H = canvas.height;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

      const topHud = document.getElementById('top-scoreboard-hud');
      if (topHud) topHud.classList.remove('hidden');

      const padX = Math.max(12, Math.round(W * 0.015));
      const padY = Math.max(14, Math.round(H * 0.025));
      const fieldL = padX;
      const fieldR = W - padX;
      const fieldT = padY;
      const fieldB = H - padY;
      const fieldPxW = fieldR - fieldL;
      const fieldPxH = fieldB - fieldT;

      const toPxX = (fx) => fieldL + (fx / FIELD_W) * fieldPxW;
      const toPxY = (fy) => fieldT + (fy / FIELD_H) * fieldPxH;

      // Dynamic Team-Themed Stadium Surround Apron
      const pal = (match && match.stadiumPalette) || { primary: '#1e293b', secondary: '#facc15', homeId: 'NFL', homeCity: 'GRIDIRON', homeName: 'STADIUM' };
      ctx.fillStyle = '#090d16';
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

      ctx.fillStyle = pal.primary;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.28;
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1.0;

      // Sideline benches & stadium border accents
      ctx.fillStyle = pal.secondary;
      ctx.fillRect(fieldL - 6, fieldT - 6, fieldPxW + 12, 4);
      ctx.fillRect(fieldL - 6, fieldB + 2, fieldPxW + 12, 4);

      // Alternating mowed turf stripes (10-yard bands)
      for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
        const x1 = toPxX(i * 10);
        const x2 = toPxX((i + 1) * 10);
        ctx.fillStyle = i % 2 === 0 ? '#146c2e' : '#187d36';
        ctx.fillRect(x1, fieldT, x2 - x1 + 1, fieldPxH);
      }

      // End zones (Left = Home Team End Zone, Right = Away / Target End Zone)
      const leftEzTeam = match ? (match.userIsHome ? match.userTeam : match.cpuTeam) : { primary: '#1d4ed8', name: 'HOME' };
      const rightEzTeam = match ? (match.userIsHome ? match.cpuTeam : match.userTeam) : { primary: '#dc2626', name: 'AWAY' };
      const ezW = toPxX(10) - toPxX(0);

      ctx.fillStyle = leftEzTeam.primary;
      ctx.fillRect(fieldL, fieldT, ezW, fieldPxH);
      ctx.fillStyle = rightEzTeam.primary;
      ctx.fillRect(toPxX(110), fieldT, ezW, fieldPxH);

      // End zone team text
      ctx.save();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.88)';
      ctx.font = `bold ${Math.max(12, Math.floor(ezW * 0.34))}px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif`;
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.translate(fieldL + ezW / 2, fieldT + fieldPxH / 2);
      ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);
      ctx.fillText(leftEzTeam.name, 0, 0);
      ctx.restore();

      ctx.save();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.88)';
      ctx.font = `bold ${Math.max(12, Math.floor(ezW * 0.34))}px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif`;
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
      ctx.translate(toPxX(110) + ezW / 2, fieldT + fieldPxH / 2);
      ctx.rotate(Math.PI / 2);
      ctx.fillText(rightEzTeam.name, 0, 0);
      ctx.restore();

      // Yard lines (every 5 yards) & Hash Marks
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
      for (let yd = 10; yd <= 110; yd += 5) {
        const px = toPxX(yd);
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(px, fieldT);
        ctx.lineTo(px, fieldB);
        ctx.stroke();

        // Yard numbers every 10 yards
        if (yd > 10 && yd < 110 && yd % 10 === 0) {
          const disp = yd <= 60 ? yd - 10 : 110 - yd;
          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.78)';
          ctx.font = `bold ${Math.max(10, Math.floor(fieldPxH * 0.032))}px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif`;
          ctx.textAlign = 'center';
          ctx.fillText(String(disp), px, fieldT + fieldPxH * 0.12);
          ctx.fillText(String(disp), px, fieldB - fieldPxH * 0.09);
        }
      }

      // Single-yard boundary markers (ticks) between every 5-yard line along top & bottom sidelines
      const tickLen = Math.max(5, Math.round(fieldPxH * 0.022));
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.82)';
      ctx.lineWidth = 1.25;
      for (let yd = 11; yd < 110; yd += 1) {
        if (yd % 5 === 0) continue;
        const px = toPxX(yd);
        ctx.beginPath();
        // Top sideline 1-yard tick
        ctx.moveTo(px, fieldT);
        ctx.lineTo(px, fieldT + tickLen);
        // Bottom sideline 1-yard tick
        ctx.moveTo(px, fieldB - tickLen);
        ctx.lineTo(px, fieldB);
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      // Line of Scrimmage (Blue) & First Down Marker (Gold)
      if (match) {
        const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
        const losPx = toPxX(match.los);
        const fdPx = toPxX(Math.max(10, Math.min(110, match.firstDownLine)));

        // Blue Line of Scrimmage
        ctx.save();
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8';
        ctx.lineWidth = 3.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(losPx, fieldT);
        ctx.lineTo(losPx, fieldB);
        ctx.stroke();

        // Sideline LOS Pylons
        ctx.fillStyle = '#0284c7';
        ctx.fillRect(losPx - 7, fieldT - 4, 14, 8);
        ctx.fillRect(losPx - 7, fieldB - 4, 14, 8);
        ctx.restore();

        // Yellow/Gold First Down Line (drawn when not goal-to-go)
        const isGoalToGo = dir === 1 ? match.firstDownLine >= 110 : match.firstDownLine <= 10;
        if (!isGoalToGo) {
          ctx.save();
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#facc15';
          ctx.lineWidth = 3.5;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(fdPx, fieldT);
          ctx.lineTo(fdPx, fieldB);
          ctx.stroke();

          // Sideline First Down Chain Markers
          ctx.fillStyle = '#eab308';
          ctx.fillRect(fdPx - 7, fieldT - 4, 14, 8);
          ctx.fillRect(fdPx - 7, fieldB - 4, 14, 8);
          ctx.restore();
        }
      }

      // Draw Route Arrows & Interactive Lead-Pass Target Reticle
      if (match.possession === 'USER' && (match.phase === 'PRESNAP' || match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK') && match.currentPlay && match.currentPlay.type === 'PASS') {
        match.players.forEach(p => {
          if (p.side === 'OFF' && p.isEligible && p.waypoints) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.strokeStyle = p.id === 'WR1' ? '#facc15' : '#38bdf8';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
            ctx.setLineDash([6, 4]);
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(toPxX(p.x), toPxY(p.y));
            p.waypoints.forEach(wp => ctx.lineTo(toPxX(wp.x), toPxY(wp.y)));
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();
          }
        });

        // Show live lead-pass target reticle & nearest receiver link when aiming on field
        if (match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK' && hoverFieldPos) {
          const hx = toPxX(hoverFieldPos.x);
          const hy = toPxY(hoverFieldPos.y);
          const eligibles = match.players.filter(p => p.side === 'OFF' && p.isEligible);
          let nearestRec = null;
          let bestScore = Infinity;
          eligibles.forEach(r => {
            const d = Math.hypot(r.x - hoverFieldPos.x, r.y - hoverFieldPos.y);
            if (d < bestScore) { bestScore = d; nearestRec = r; }
          });

          ctx.save();
          if (nearestRec) {
            ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.55)';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;
            ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(toPxX(nearestRec.x), toPxY(nearestRec.y));
            ctx.lineTo(hx, hy);
            ctx.stroke();
          }

          // Crisp target bullseye where the ball will land
          ctx.setLineDash([]);
          ctx.strokeStyle = '#facc15';
          ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.arc(hx, hy, 12, 0, Math.PI * 2);
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.beginPath();
          ctx.moveTo(hx - 17, hy);
          ctx.lineTo(hx + 17, hy);
          ctx.moveTo(hx, hy - 17);
          ctx.lineTo(hx, hy + 17);
          ctx.stroke();
          ctx.restore();
        }
      }

      // Draw Players (16-Bit Retro Sprites with Shoulder Pads, Helmets & Number Badges)
      const animClock = performance.now() / 1000;
      const spriteR = Math.max(8, Math.min(13, fieldPxH * 0.024));
      const sortedPlayers = [...match.players].sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y);
      sortedPlayers.forEach(p => {
        const px = toPxX(p.x);
        const py = toPxY(p.y);
        drawDetailedPlayerSprite(ctx, p, px, py, spriteR, animClock);
      });

      // Draw Airborne Football with Arc Shadow & Landing Target Marker
      if (match.phase === 'PASS_FLIGHT' && match.ball) {
        const b = match.ball;
        const t = Math.min(1, b.elapsed / b.duration);
        const arc = Math.sin(t * Math.PI) * b.arcHeight;
        const bx = toPxX(b.x);
        const by = toPxY(b.y - arc);
        const shadowY = toPxY(b.y);
        const tx = toPxX(b.targetX);
        const ty = toPxY(b.targetY);

        // Landing spot ring on turf so user & receiver see where the ball is led
        ctx.save();
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(250, 204, 21, 0.85)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 2.2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(tx, ty, 11 * (1 - t * 0.35), 6 * (1 - t * 0.35), 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.stroke();
        ctx.restore();

        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.45)';
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(bx, shadowY, 6, 3, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();

        ctx.fillStyle = '#92400e';
        ctx.strokeStyle = '#ffffff';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(bx, by, 7.5, 4.5, 0.25, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        ctx.stroke();
      }

      // Render Sideline Scoreboard & Down/Distance Panels on Wide Screens
      if (typeof showSidelineHud !== 'undefined' && showSidelineHud) {
        renderSidelineScoreboards(W, H, sidePanelW, padY);
      }
    }

    function renderSidelineScoreboards(W, H, panelW, padY) {
      if (!match) return;
      const homeT = match.userIsHome ? match.userTeam : match.cpuTeam;
      const awayT = match.userIsHome ? match.cpuTeam : match.userTeam;
      const homeU = match.userIsHome ? match.userUni : match.cpuUni;
      const awayU = match.userIsHome ? match.cpuUni : match.userUni;
      const homeScore = match.userIsHome ? match.userScore : match.cpuScore;
      const awayScore = match.userIsHome ? match.cpuScore : match.userScore;
      const homeHasBall = (match.userIsHome && match.possession === 'USER') || (!match.userIsHome && match.possession === 'CPU');

      const boxMargin = 10;
      const boxW = panelW - boxMargin * 2;
      const boxH = Math.min(H - padY * 2, 340);
      const boxY = Math.max(padY + 4, Math.round((H - boxH) / 2));

      // --- LEFT SIDELINE PANEL: HOME VS AWAY SCOREBOARD ---
      const lx = boxMargin;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f172a';
      ctx.fillRect(lx, boxY, boxW, boxH);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#facc15';
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.strokeRect(lx, boxY, boxW, boxH);

      ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
      ctx.font = 'bold 12px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.textAlign = 'center';
      ctx.fillText('SCOREBOARD', lx + boxW / 2, boxY + 22);

      // Away Team Block
      const awayY = boxY + 38;
      ctx.fillStyle = awayU.jersey;
      ctx.fillRect(lx + 10, awayY, boxW - 20, 74);
      ctx.strokeStyle = awayU.trim;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.strokeRect(lx + 10, awayY, boxW - 20, 74);

      const awayTextColor = awayU.jersey === '#f8fafc' ? '#0f172a' : '#ffffff';
      ctx.fillStyle = awayTextColor;
      ctx.font = 'bold 14px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(`${awayT.id} ${!homeHasBall ? '🏈' : ''}`, lx + boxW / 2, awayY + 22);
      ctx.font = 'bold 28px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(String(awayScore), lx + boxW / 2, awayY + 56);

      // Home Team Block
      const homeY = awayY + 86;
      ctx.fillStyle = homeU.jersey;
      ctx.fillRect(lx + 10, homeY, boxW - 20, 74);
      ctx.strokeStyle = homeU.trim;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.strokeRect(lx + 10, homeY, boxW - 20, 74);

      const homeTextColor = homeU.jersey === '#f8fafc' ? '#0f172a' : '#ffffff';
      ctx.fillStyle = homeTextColor;
      ctx.font = 'bold 14px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(`${homeT.id} ${homeHasBall ? '🏈' : ''}`, lx + boxW / 2, homeY + 22);
      ctx.font = 'bold 28px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(String(homeScore), lx + boxW / 2, homeY + 56);

      // Quarter & Clock Box
      const clkY = homeY + 88;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#020617';
      ctx.fillRect(lx + 10, clkY, boxW - 20, 62);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#334155';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.strokeRect(lx + 10, clkY, boxW - 20, 62);

      const mins = Math.floor(match.clock / 60);
      const secs = Math.floor(match.clock % 60);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#34d399';
      ctx.font = 'bold 13px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(`QUARTER ${match.quarter}`, lx + boxW / 2, clkY + 22);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.font = 'bold 19px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(`${String(mins).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(secs).padStart(2, '0')}`, lx + boxW / 2, clkY + 48);

      // --- RIGHT SIDELINE PANEL: DOWN, DISTANCE & BALL SPOT ---
      const rx = W - panelW + boxMargin;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#0f172a';
      ctx.fillRect(rx, boxY, boxW, boxH);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#38bdf8';
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.strokeRect(rx, boxY, boxW, boxH);

      ctx.fillStyle = '#38bdf8';
      ctx.font = 'bold 12px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText('SITUATION', rx + boxW / 2, boxY + 22);

      const downStr = ['1ST', '2ND', '3RD', '4TH'][Math.min(3, match.down - 1)];
      const dir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const targetEz = dir === 1 ? 110 : 10;
      const ownEz = dir === 1 ? 10 : 110;
      const toGo = Math.max(1, Math.round((match.firstDownLine - match.los) * dir));
      const goalDist = Math.max(1, Math.round((targetEz - match.los) * dir));
      const isGoalToGo = dir === 1 ? match.firstDownLine >= 110 : match.firstDownLine <= 10;
      const distText = isGoalToGo ? `GOAL (${goalDist})` : `${toGo} YDS`;
      const distFromOwn = Math.round((match.los - ownEz) * dir);
      const spotText = distFromOwn <= 50
        ? `OWN ${Math.max(1, distFromOwn)}`
        : `OPP ${Math.max(1, 100 - distFromOwn)}`;

      // Down & To Go Card
      const ddY = boxY + 38;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#020617';
      ctx.fillRect(rx + 10, ddY, boxW - 20, 86);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#facc15';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.strokeRect(rx + 10, ddY, boxW - 20, 86);

      ctx.fillStyle = '#facc15';
      ctx.font = 'bold 22px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(`${downStr} DOWN`, rx + boxW / 2, ddY + 32);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
      ctx.font = 'bold 16px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(`& ${distText}`, rx + boxW / 2, ddY + 64);

      // Field Spot Card
      const spY = ddY + 98;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#020617';
      ctx.fillRect(rx + 10, spY, boxW - 20, 66);
      ctx.strokeStyle = '#334155';
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.strokeRect(rx + 10, spY, boxW - 20, 66);

      ctx.fillStyle = '#94a3b8';
      ctx.font = 'bold 12px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText('BALL ON', rx + boxW / 2, spY + 22);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#38bdf8';
      ctx.font = 'bold 18px "Comic Sans MS", "Comic Neue", cursive, sans-serif';
      ctx.fillText(spotText, rx + boxW / 2, spY + 48);
    }

    function gameLoop(ts) {
      if (!lastFrameTime) lastFrameTime = ts;
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (ts - lastFrameTime) / 1000);
      lastFrameTime = ts;

      if (match && !document.getElementById('screen-game').classList.contains('hidden')) {
        updateMatch(dt);
        renderMatch();
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
    }

    /* =========================================================
       11. INPUT HANDLERS & INITIAL BOOTSTRAP
       ========================================================= */
    window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      keys[e.code] = true;

      if (!match || document.getElementById('screen-game').classList.contains('hidden')) return;

      if (e.code === 'Space') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (match.phase === 'PRESNAP') {
          hikeBall();
        } else if (match.phase === 'DEF_PRESNAP') {
          snapCpuOffenseBall();
        } else if (match.phase === 'KICKING') {
          lockKickAttempt();
        } else if (match.possession === 'USER' && match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK') {
          activateQbScramble();
        } else if (match.possession === 'USER' && match.phase === 'RUN_PLAY' && match.ballCarrier) {
          match.ballCarrier.jukeTimer = 0.45;
          sfx.playClick();
        } else if (match.possession === 'CPU' && match.phase === 'DEF_LIVE') {
          const def = match.players.find(p => p.id === match.selectedDefenderId);
          if (def) {
            def.diveTimer = 0.38;
            sfx.playClick();
          }
        }
      }

      if (e.code === 'KeyQ' && match.possession === 'CPU' && (match.phase === 'DEF_LIVE' || match.phase === 'DEF_PRESNAP')) {
        cycleSelectedDefender();
      }

      // Offensive Playbook A / D Carousel Scroll
      if (match.phase === 'PLAYCALL' && offPlayStep === 'PLAY') {
        const formObj = OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedOffFormationId) || OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[0];
        const allFormPlays = PLAYBOOK.filter(formObj.match);
        const maxStart = Math.max(0, allFormPlays.length - 5);
        if (e.code === 'KeyA' && offCarouselIdx > 0) {
          sfx.playClick();
          offCarouselIdx -= 1;
          renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
        } else if (e.code === 'KeyD' && offCarouselIdx < maxStart) {
          sfx.playClick();
          offCarouselIdx += 1;
          renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
        }
      }

      // Defensive Playbook A / D Carousel Scroll
      if (match.phase === 'DEF_PLAYCALL' && defPlayStep === 'PLAY') {
        const formObj = DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedDefFormationId) || DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[1];
        const maxStart = Math.max(0, formObj.plays.length - 5);
        if (e.code === 'KeyA' && defCarouselIdx > 0) {
          sfx.playClick();
          defCarouselIdx -= 1;
          renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
        } else if (e.code === 'KeyD' && defCarouselIdx < maxStart) {
          sfx.playClick();
          defCarouselIdx += 1;
          renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
        }
      }

      // Number Keys 1-5: Select Formation/Play in Playcall OR Throw to Receiver [1-5]
      if (['Digit1', 'Digit2', 'Digit3', 'Digit4', 'Digit5'].includes(e.code)) {
        const idx = parseInt(e.code.replace('Digit', ''), 10) - 1;
        if (match.phase === 'PLAYCALL') {
          if (offPlayStep === 'FORMATION') {
            const formObj = OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[idx];
            if (formObj) {
              sfx.playClick();
              selectedOffFormationId = formObj.id;
              offPlayStep = 'PLAY';
              offCarouselIdx = 0;
              renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
            }
          } else {
            const formObj = OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedOffFormationId) || OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[0];
            const formPlays = PLAYBOOK.filter(formObj.match);
            const chosen = formPlays[offCarouselIdx + idx];
            if (chosen) {
              sfx.playClick();
              selectOffensivePlay(chosen);
            }
          }
        } else if (match.phase === 'DEF_PLAYCALL') {
          if (defPlayStep === 'FORMATION') {
            const formObj = DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[idx];
            if (formObj) {
              sfx.playClick();
              selectedDefFormationId = formObj.id;
              defPlayStep = 'PLAY';
              defCarouselIdx = 0;
              renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
            }
          } else {
            const formObj = DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedDefFormationId) || DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[1];
            const chosenDef = formObj.plays[defCarouselIdx + idx];
            if (chosenDef) {
              sfx.playClick();
              startUserDefensivePlay(chosenDef.key, formObj.id, chosenDef.title);
            }
          }
        } else if (match.possession === 'USER' && match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK') {
          const rec = match.players.find(p => p.side === 'OFF' && p.isEligible && p.num === idx + 2);
          if (rec) passToReceiver(rec);
        }
      }
    });

    window.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => {
      keys[e.code] = false;
    });

    function cycleSelectedDefender() {
      if (!match) return;
      const defs = match.players.filter(p => p.side === 'DEF');
      if (!defs.length) return;
      const curIdx = defs.findIndex(d => d.id === match.selectedDefenderId);
      const next = defs[(curIdx + 1) % defs.length];
      match.selectedDefenderId = next.id;
      sfx.playClick();
      triggerBanner(`CONTROL: ${next.name}`, 850);
    }

    canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
      if (!match) { hoverFieldPos = null; return; }
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const moveX = e.clientX - rect.left;
      const moveY = e.clientY - rect.top;
      const W = canvas.width;
      const H = canvas.height;
      const padX = Math.max(12, Math.round(W * 0.015));
      const padY = Math.max(14, Math.round(H * 0.025));
      const fieldPxW = W - padX * 2;
      const fieldPxH = H - padY * 2;
      const fx = ((moveX - padX) / fieldPxW) * FIELD_W;
      const fy = ((moveY - padY) / fieldPxH) * FIELD_H;
      if (fx >= 0 && fx <= FIELD_W && fy >= 0 && fy <= FIELD_H) {
        hoverFieldPos = { x: fx, y: fy };
      } else {
        hoverFieldPos = null;
      }
    });

    canvas.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => {
      hoverFieldPos = null;
    });

    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
      if (!match) return;
      const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const clickX = e.clientX - rect.left;
      const clickY = e.clientY - rect.top;

      const W = canvas.width;
      const H = canvas.height;
      const padX = Math.max(12, Math.round(W * 0.015));
      const padY = Math.max(14, Math.round(H * 0.025));
      const fieldL = padX;
      const fieldR = W - padX;
      const fieldT = padY;
      const fieldB = H - padY;
      const fieldPxW = fieldR - fieldL;
      const fieldPxH = fieldB - fieldT;

      if (match.possession === 'USER' && match.phase === 'PASS_DROPBACK') {
        const fx = ((clickX - fieldL) / fieldPxW) * FIELD_W;
        const fy = ((clickY - fieldT) / fieldPxH) * FIELD_H;
        // Always throw directly to the clicked spot on the field so the player leads their receiver
        throwToFieldPoint(fx, fy);
      } else if (match.possession === 'CPU' && (match.phase === 'DEF_LIVE' || match.phase === 'DEF_PRESNAP')) {
        const fx = ((clickX - fieldL) / fieldPxW) * FIELD_W;
        const fy = ((clickY - fieldT) / fieldPxH) * FIELD_H;
        let closest = null;
        let minD = 7;
        match.players.forEach(d => {
          if (d.side === 'DEF') {
            const dist = Math.hypot(d.x - fx, d.y - fy);
            if (dist < minD) { minD = dist; closest = d; }
          }
        });
        if (closest) {
          match.selectedDefenderId = closest.id;
          sfx.playClick();
          triggerBanner(`CONTROL: ${closest.name}`, 850);
        }
      }
    });

    window.addEventListener('resize', resizeCanvas);

    // Wire UI Buttons
    document.getElementById('btn-sound-toggle').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.enabled = !sfx.enabled;
      document.getElementById('btn-sound-toggle').textContent = `SFX: ${sfx.enabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF'}`;
      sfx.playClick();
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.diff-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        sfx.playClick();
        selectedDifficulty = btn.getAttribute('data-diff');
        document.querySelectorAll('.diff-btn').forEach(b => {
          const active = b.getAttribute('data-diff') === selectedDifficulty;
          b.classList.toggle('btn-gold', active);
          b.classList.toggle('btn-slate', !active);
        });
      });
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-new-career').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      openTeamSelector('CAREER');
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-continue-career').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      const contBtn = document.getElementById('btn-continue-career');
      if (contBtn) {
        contBtn.disabled = true;
        contBtn.textContent = 'LOADING...';
      }
      const loaded = await loadCareerAsync();
      if (contBtn) {
        contBtn.disabled = false;
        contBtn.textContent = 'CONTINUE';
      }
      if (loaded && loaded.teamId) {
        career = loaded;
        const t = getUserTeam(career.teamId);
        showToast(`LOADED ${t.city} ${t.name} (SEASON ${career.season} • WEEK ${Math.min(18, career.week)})`);
        openHub('office');
      } else {
        showToast('NO DYNASTY SAVE FOUND FOR YOUR ACCOUNT! START A NEW DYNASTY FIRST.');
      }
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-quick-play').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      openTeamSelector('QUICKPLAY');
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.conf-filter').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        sfx.playClick();
        selectedConfFilter = btn.getAttribute('data-conf');
        document.querySelectorAll('.conf-filter').forEach(b => {
          const active = b.getAttribute('data-conf') === selectedConfFilter;
          b.classList.toggle('btn-gold', active);
          b.classList.toggle('btn-slate', !active);
        });
        renderTeamGrid();
      });
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.uni-choice-btn').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        sfx.playClick();
        setUserUniformChoice(btn.getAttribute('data-uni-choice'));
      });
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-back-title').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      switchScreen('screen-title');
    });

    document.querySelectorAll('.hub-tab').forEach(btn => {
      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        sfx.playClick();
        openHub(btn.getAttribute('data-hubtab'));
      });
    });

    const hubSaveBtn = document.getElementById('btn-hub-save');
    if (hubSaveBtn) {
      hubSaveBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        sfx.playClick();
        hubSaveBtn.disabled = true;
        hubSaveBtn.textContent = 'SAVING...';
        await saveCareer(true);
        hubSaveBtn.disabled = false;
        hubSaveBtn.textContent = 'SAVE';
      });
    }

    document.getElementById('btn-hub-menu').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      await saveCareer(false);
      switchScreen('screen-title');
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-hike').addEventListener('click', hikeBall);
    document.getElementById('btn-audible').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      openPlaycallOverlay();
    });
    document.getElementById('btn-off-back-formations').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      offPlayStep = 'FORMATION';
      offCarouselIdx = 0;
      renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
    });
    document.getElementById('btn-off-scroll-left').addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (offCarouselIdx > 0) {
        sfx.playClick();
        offCarouselIdx -= 1;
        renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('btn-off-scroll-right').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const formObj = OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedOffFormationId) || OFFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[0];
      const allFormPlays = PLAYBOOK.filter(formObj.match);
      const maxStart = Math.max(0, allFormPlays.length - 5);
      if (offCarouselIdx < maxStart) {
        sfx.playClick();
        offCarouselIdx += 1;
        renderOffensivePlaybookUI();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('btn-def-back-formations').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      defPlayStep = 'FORMATION';
      defCarouselIdx = 0;
      renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
    });
    document.getElementById('btn-def-scroll-left').addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (defCarouselIdx > 0) {
        sfx.playClick();
        defCarouselIdx -= 1;
        renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('btn-def-scroll-right').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const formObj = DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS.find(f => f.id === selectedDefFormationId) || DEFENSIVE_FORMATIONS[1];
      const maxStart = Math.max(0, formObj.plays.length - 5);
      if (defCarouselIdx < maxStart) {
        sfx.playClick();
        defCarouselIdx += 1;
        renderDefensivePlaybookUI();
      }
    });
    document.getElementById('btn-def-hike').addEventListener('click', snapCpuOffenseBall);
    document.getElementById('btn-def-presnap-switch').addEventListener('click', cycleSelectedDefender);
    document.getElementById('btn-def-audible').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      openDefensivePlaycallOverlay();
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-call-punt').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      document.getElementById('overlay-playcall').classList.add('hidden');
      triggerBanner('PUNTED DOWNFIELD!');
      const prevDir = match.driveDir || 1;
      const puntLandX = Math.max(15, Math.min(105, match.los + prevDir * 40));
      match.driveDir = -prevDir;
      match.los = puntLandX;
      match.firstDownLine = Math.max(10, Math.min(110, match.los + match.driveDir * 10));
      match.down = 1;
      setTimeout(() => beginDefensiveSeries(true), 1200);
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-call-fg').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      openKickOverlay(false);
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-kick-lock').addEventListener('click', lockKickAttempt);
    document.getElementById('btn-sim-def-drive').addEventListener('click', simulateCpuDrive);

    document.getElementById('btn-sim-continue').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      document.getElementById('overlay-sim').classList.add('hidden');
      if (!match) return;
      if (match.phase === 'HALFTIME') {
        match.driveDir = 1;
        match.los = 35;
        match.firstDownLine = 45;
        match.down = 1;
        openPlaycallOverlay();
        return;
      }
      if (checkQuarterEnd()) return;
      match.driveDir = 1;
      match.los = 35;
      match.firstDownLine = 45;
      match.down = 1;
      openPlaycallOverlay();
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-boxscore').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      openBoxScoreModal();
    });
    const floatBoxscoreBtn = document.getElementById('btn-boxscore-float');
    if (floatBoxscoreBtn) {
      floatBoxscoreBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        sfx.playClick();
        openBoxScoreModal();
      });
    }
    document.getElementById('btn-close-boxscore').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      document.getElementById('overlay-boxscore').classList.add('hidden');
    });

    const handleQuitGame = () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      const wasQuick = match ? match.isQuickPlay : true;
      match = null;
      if (!wasQuick && career) openHub('office');
      else switchScreen('screen-title');
    };
    document.getElementById('btn-pause-game').addEventListener('click', handleQuitGame);

    document.getElementById('btn-postgame-done').addEventListener('click', () => {
      sfx.playClick();
      const wasQuick = match ? match.isQuickPlay : true;
      match = null;
      if (!wasQuick && career) openHub('office');
      else switchScreen('screen-title');
    });

    // Touch controls
    const turboBtn = document.getElementById('btn-turbo');
    turboBtn.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => { keys['TURBO'] = true; });
    turboBtn.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { keys['TURBO'] = false; });
    turboBtn.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { keys['TURBO'] = false; });

    document.getElementById('btn-qb-scramble').addEventListener('click', () => {
      activateQbScramble();
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-juke').addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (match && match.ballCarrier) {
        match.ballCarrier.jukeTimer = 0.45;
        sfx.playClick();
      }
    });

    document.getElementById('btn-def-switch').addEventListener('click', cycleSelectedDefender);
    document.getElementById('btn-def-tackle').addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (!match) return;
      const def = match.players.find(p => p.id === match.selectedDefenderId);
      if (def) {
        def.diveTimer = 0.38;
        sfx.playClick();
      }
    });

    // Initial Title Screen Setup
    window.addEventListener('load', () => {
      resizeCanvas();
      requestAnimationFrame(gameLoop);
    });
  </script>
</body>
</html>
